1930-l naṭanta tērtalil nayaṉmik kaṭci veṟṟi peṟṟatu. 'cuyarācciyak kaṭciyiṉ cuvaṭu maṟintatu. itaṟkup periyāriṉ taṉmāṉa iyakkattiṉ vaḷarcciyē karaṇiyamākum. āyiṉum nayaṉmik kaṭci periyāriṉ koḷkikaḷi ēṟka añciyatu. 
1931 ākasṭil virutunakaril taṉmāṉa iyakkattiṉ mūṉṟāvatu mānila mānāṭu naṭantatu. itaṟkut talimiyēṟṟavar vayavar. ārkē. caṇmukam āvār. mūṭanampikkikaḷi oḻikka vēṇṭumeṉṉum karuttum, katarttoḻil intiyap poruḷiyali vaḷarkkāteṉṉum karuttum im mānāṭṭil valiyuṟuttappaṭṭaṉa. camayam, mūṭa nampikki cuḷukku iruppiṭamāṉateṉṉum karutti intukkaḷēyaṉṟiiculāmiya rum kiṟittavarumkūṭa etirttaṉar. iyakkattiṟku etirikaḷ ērāḷamāyiṉar, āyiṉum, pakuttaṟivik kūṟum pala ayalnāṭṭu nūlkaḷum, intu camaya itikāca, tolkati, cāttiraṅkaḷiluḷḷa pakuttaṟivukku ovvātaviyum, coṟpoḻivukaḷālum veḷiyīṭukaḷālum makkaḷukku viḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. etirppukkiṭiyē iyakkam vēkamāka vaḷarntatu. * veḷināṭṭup payaṇam

tirāviṭar kaḻakattiṉ kuralāka 'viṭutali' yum, kuṭiyaracu'm, aṇṇā avarkaḷi āciriyarākak koṇṭa tirāviṭanāṭu 'm, eṉ.vi. naṭarācaṉi āciriyarākak koṇṭa tirāviṭaṉum', ṭi. em. pārtta cāratiyi āciriyarākak koṇṭa tamiḻ ulakamum' veḷivantaṉa. aṇṇāviṉ pēccukaḷ, tirāviṭar nili, nalla tīrppu, nāṭum ēṭum mutaliya nūlkaḷāka veḷivantaṉa. 'tirāviṭa eḻuttāḷar kaḻakam' eṉṟa amippum ēṟpaṭṭatu. itil ecu. kurucāmi talivarākavum, pulavar pu. celvarācu ceyalāḷarākavum paṇiyāṟṟiṉar. ittakiya ēṭukaḷum, aṇṇāviṉ pēccukaḷum māṇavarkaḷip peritum kavarntaṉa. itaṉāl 'tirāviṭar māṇavar kaḻakam' eṉṟa amippum ēṟpaṭṭatu. ka. aṉ paḻakaṉ, irā. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, tavamaṇi irācaṉ, iḷamvaḻuti, cu a.mati yaḻakaṉ, ēpi.caṉārttaṉam mutaliyōr immāṇavar kaḻakattil paṅkēṟṟa ṉar. piṉṉar mu. karuṇāniti, tilli villāḷaṉ, araṅkaṇṇal, appāvu, kōkulakiruṭṭiṇaṉ, poṉṉuvēlu mutaliyōrum itil īṭupaṭṭa ṉar. 
tirāviṭar kaḻakatti vaḷarppataṟkeṉṟē karuñcaṭṭit toṇṭar paṭi eṉṉum amipput tōṉṟiyatu, ittoṇṭarkaḷ muḻunērap paṇiyāḷarkaḷākavum, muṭukkamāy uḻippavarkaḷākavum iruntatāl kaḻakam kaṭitu vaḷarntatu. 146-l maturiyil 'curuñcaṭṭit toṇṭar mānāṭu naṭantatu. itaṟkuppiṉ ippaṭikku aracu taṭi vitittatu. āyiṉum, taṭiyi mīṟit taṭiyaṭipaṭṭut toṇṭarkaḷ kaḻakatti vaḷarttaṉar. mīṇṭum inti etirppu

1949 mē 14-l intiyāviṉ talimi āḷunarāyirunta ci. irāci kōpālāccāri tiruvaṇṇāmalik kōyilil pātāḷaviṅkat tiruvaṟiyit tiṟantuvikka vantapōtu periyārum, avaruṭiya toṇṭarkaḷuḷ oruvarāṉa maṇiyammiyum avaric cumucukamāyc cantittaṉar. piṉṉar, eḻupattōr akavi nirampiya periyārukkum, irupattāṟu akavi nirampiya mariyammiyārukkum pativut tirumaṇam naṭantatu. itaṉi etirttu aṇṇā uṭpaṭap palarum kaṇṇīr cinti veḷiyēṟi 1791949-l "tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam' eṉṉum putiya amippit toṭaṅkiṉar. iraṇṭaṭi akalamum mūṉṟaṭi nīḷamum koṇṭa koṭiyiṉ mēṟpakuti kaṟuppākavum, kīḻppakuti civappākavum naṭuvil utayacūriyaṉ ciṉṉattuṭaṉum irukkum koṭiyic ciṉṉamākak koṇṭaṉar. kumukāyac cīrtiruttam, pakuttaṟivup paṇpāṭukaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟiyē ik kaṭci aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatu. potuk kuḻu, koḷki viḷakkak kuḻu amippukkuḻu, nitikkuḻu eṉpaṉavākappirittu aṇṇāvipappotucceyalāḷarākak koṇṭu tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu. ik kaḻakam naṭattiya pōrāṭṭattiṉāl aracu 187 .1950-l inti cuṭṭāyap pāṭam aṉṟu eṉṟu kūṟiyatu. itaṉālum makkaḷiṉ celvākkik kaḻakam peṟṟatu, nerukkaṭi 
1950-l ceṉṉi uyar nayaṉmi maṉṟattil vakuppuvārip paṭinikarāṉiyam cellāteṉṟu tīrppu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatik kaṇṭittuk kaḻaka ēṭukaḷil eḻutiyum, nāṭeṅkilum kaḻakattār kaṇṭaṉak kūṭṭaṅkaḷi naṭattiyum vantaṉar. itaṉāl kaḻaka ēṭāṉa tirāviṭanāṭu' mūvāyiramurupāīṭṭuttoki cuṭṭavēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. it toki yi makkaḷē kaṭṭiṉar. itaṟkuppiṉ kaḻakatti aṭiyōṭu oṭukka aracu muṉintatu. aṇṇāviṉ 'āriyamāyi' eṉṟa nūlukkut taṭi vitittatu. aṇṇāvukkum aracu āṟu tiṅkaḷ kaṭuṅkāval taṇṭaṉi - vaḻaṅkiyatu. aṇṇāvi viṭutali ceyyakkōri nāṭeṅkum kiḷarccika ḷēṟpaṭṭaṉa. aṇṇā viṭutali āṉār. itaṉiyaṭuttu aracu, kaḻakattavar palar mītu vaḻakkukaḷ toṭuttut taṇṭittatu. kaḻakattavarum toṭarntu pala pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷil īṭupaṭṭaṉar. 195ṭicampar 13, 14, 15, 16 ākiya nāḷkaḷil ceṉṉiyil tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam mutaṉ mānila mānāṭṭiṉi naṭattiyatu. tamiḻaracuk kaḻakat talivar mā.po.civañāṉamum, taḷapati viṉāyakamum tiruttaṇi tamiḻakattukkē cēravēṇṭumeṉappōrāṭiṉar. ippōrāṭṭattil tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakamum paṅkukoṇṭatu. itaṉāl pala iṉṉalkaḷiyum aṭintatu. kallakkuṭip pōrāṭṭam 
tiruccikku arukiluḷḷa "ṭālmiyāpuram' eṉṉum ūriṉ peyarik “kallakkuṭi' eṉa māṟṟumpaṭi tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam pōrāṭṭam naṭattiyatu. itu toṭarntu pala nāḷkaḷ naṭantatu. mu. karuṇāniti, pāvalar kaṇṇatācaṉ mutaliyōr talimi tāṅkiṉar. toṭarvaṇṭippātiyil paṭuttu naṭattiya ippōrāṭṭattil palarum ciṟippaṭṭaṉar. kāvalar cuṭṭatil iruvar māṇṭaṉar. nāṭeṅkilum kaṇṭaṉaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. pōrāṭṭam veṟṟipeṟṟatu, 
āṭcipīṭatti nōkki 
1952 ām āṇṭu potuttērtalil taṅkaḷ karutti ēṟpōri ātaritta kaḻakam 1957-l tāṉē nēraṭiyākat tērtalil niṟka muṭivu ceytatu. itaṟkiṭiyil naṭanta kēvikēcāmiyiṉ paṭukoliyum, caṅkara liṅkaṉār "tamiḻnāṭu' eṉap peyariṭumpaṭi 78 nāḷ uṇṇā nōṉ piruntu uyirviṭṭa nikaḻcciyum kaḻakattiṟku ākkamāyiṉa. 197 tērtalil timuka 15 iṭaṅkaḷip peṟṟu aṇṇā talimiyil etirkkaṭci variciyil amarntatu. ī.vē.ki.campattum, irātarumaliṅkamum nāṭāḷu maṉṟam ceṉṟaṉar. piriviṉik koḷkiyik kiviṭal 
1962-l ti.mu.kaḻakattiṉar nāṭu muḻuvatilum vilivāciyi etirttup pōrāṭicciṟi ceṉṟaṉar. pōrāṭṭam veṟṟipeṟṟatōṭu makkaḷiṉ pērātaraviyum peṟṟatu. ciṟimīṇṭa aṇṇā tirāviṭa nāṭṭup piriviṉi'yik kiviṭṭār, cīṉappōri etirttu oṉṟu paṭṭa nāṭṭuppaṟṟuṭaṉ pōriṭa makkaḷukku aḻippu viṭuttār, 
1962-l naṭanta tērtalil kaḻakattiṉar 52 iṭaṅkaḷip peṟṟaṉar. aṇṇā tōṟṟu nāṭāḷumaṉṟa mēlavi uṟuppiṉarāka amarttap peṟṟār. mīṇṭum intit tiṇippi etirttuk kaḻakam pōrāṭṭam toṭaṅkiyatu. caṭṭa erippup pōrāṭṭam itil ciṟappāṉatu. palarum ciṟittaṇṭaṉi peṟṟaṉar, tīkkuḷippu 
intiyi etirttu kīḻppaḻuvūr ciṉṉacāmi, kōṭampākkam civaliṅkam, araṅkanātaṉ, aiyampāḷiyam vīrappaṉ, cattiyamaṅkalam

periyār pāti 
1949l tammiṭamiruntu pirintu timuka ēṟpaṭṭa piṉṉum periyār taṉittu niṉṟu pala pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷi naṭattiṉār. 1950]-l vaṭanāṭṭār kaṭikaḷiṉ muṉpu maṟiyal ceytār. 1952-l toṭarvaṇṭi niliyaṅkaḷi luḷḷa peyarp palakikaḷil eḻutappaṭṭa inti eḻuttukaḷi aḻittut tamatu inti etirppit terivittār. 1954-l kulakkalvit tiṭṭatti etirttup pōriṭṭār. avvāṇṭil parmāvil naṭanta ulakap putta mānāṭṭil kalantu koṇṭār. am mānāṭṭil kalantukoṇṭa tāḻttappaṭṭōriṉ taṉipperum talivarum ulaka māmētiyumāṉa par. pi. ār. ampētkār avarkaḷik kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāviṉār. 1955-l intiyi etirttut tēciyak koṭiyiṉik koḷuttum pōrāṭṭat til īṭupaṭṭār. 1956-l irāmāyaṇak katitāyakaṉ irāmaṉuṭiya paṭatti erittut tiraviṭap paṇpiṉip pōṟṟiṉār.1957-l pārppaṉ uṇṭic cālikaḷiṉ muṉ maṟiyal naṭattic cātic iḻiviṉip pōkki ṉār. intiya araciyal caṭṭattiṉi erittut tamatu inti etirppik kāṭṭiṉār. 1959-l vaṭanāṭu muḻuvatum cuṟṟuppayaṇam mēṟkoṇṭu intu camayattiluḷḷa pakuttaṟivukku ovvāta karuttukaḷi makkaḷukku viḷakkiṉār. pala moḻikaḷilum uḷḷa irāmāyaṇatti makkaḷukku viḷakkik kūṟiṉār. tāḻttappaṭṭōr, piṟpaṭṭōr urimikaḷip paṟṟi nāṭeṅkilum paṟi cāṟṟiṉār. 1960-l tamiḻnāṭu tavirtta intiyap paṭattiṉierippatāka aṟikkiviṭṭu araciṉ kavaṉatti īrttār.1962l āṇṭu paccit tamiḻar' kāmarācar avarkaḷit tērtalil ātarittu veṟṟipeṟacceytār. 1967-l timuka veṟṟipeṟṟatum aṇṇāvum periyārum oṉṟiṇintaṉar.1970l periyār 'pakuttaṟivāḷar kaḻakam", 'cintaṉiyāḷar kaḻakam' ākiyavaṟṟit toṭaṅki vittār. 1971l cēlam mānāṭṭil intuc camayak kaṭavuḷarkaḷiṉ iḻitakimikaḷi makkaḷukku viḷakkikkūṟi, ik kaṭavuḷarkaḷiṉ tōṟṟaravukaḷum, tol katikaḷum, maṟikaḷumē cāti vēṟupāṭukaḷi vaḷarttaṉa veṉpati nāṭaṟiyac ceytār. 1971-l naṭanta iṭit tērtalil timuka. 185 iṭaṅ kaḷip peṟṟu mīṇṭum veṟṟipeṟṟatu. periyār perumakiḻvaṭintu, tūyatiraviṭariṉ amiccaraviyi vāḻttiṉār.

tāynila varalāṟu 
maṟivu 
ivvāṟu oru nūṟṟāṇṭuk kālam vari kumukāya nalam nāṭi, pakuttaṟivup pātiyil naṭipōṭṭa tanti periyār 1973 ṭicampar tiṅkaḷ 24 ām nāḷ vēlūr maruttuvamaṉiyil uyir nīttār. ivar pārppaṉiyattiṟkup pakivar; āṉāl, pārppaṉarukku neruṅkiya naṇpar. intu matattiṟkōr etiri; āṉāl, cātiyaṟṟa camayattiṉi vaḷartta talivar. viṭāppiṭiyāṉavar; āṉāl, evariyum kavarum iṉiya naṇpar, tanti periyār tamiḻiṉattiṉ ciṟpi: intiya varalāṟṟil oru nāyakar.ulaka ōrmi cintaṉiyāḷarkaḷil taṉiyiṭam peṟṟavar. 
mēṟkūṟiya mukāmic ceytikaḷiyum, avaṟṟāl cipi. irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻakam aṭinta māṟṟaṅkaḷiyum maṉattiṟkoṇṭu avaṟṟiṟku muṉṉum, piṉṉum tamiḻakattil naṭanta ceyalkaḷip paṟṟi iṉic curuṅkak kāṇpōm. aḵtāvatu kipi. pattoṉpatu, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ tamiḻaka varalāṟṟi mēlēḻuntavāriyākap pārppōm. 
(ā) poruḷiyal kācukaḷ 
ki.pi. 19, 20 ām nāṟṟāṇṭukaḷil ceṉṉi mānilattiṉ poru ḷiyal vaḷarccip pōkkiṉi āyumpōtu vāṇikam, vēḷāṇmi, pōkkuvarattu mutaliyavaṟṟip paṟṟit taṉittaṉiyē kūṟutal cālavum poruntum. mutalil poruḷiyal tuṟiyiṉ iḻunāṇāka irukkum kācukaḷip (nāṇayaṅkaḷi paṟṟic ciṟitu aṟital vēṇṭum. ik kālattil palatarappaṭṭa kācukaḷ (nāṇayaṅkaḷ tamiḻakattil puḻakkattil iruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ ārkkāṭṭu navāpu veḷiyiṭṭa urupākkaḷum, poṉ mokarākkaḷ' eṉappaṭum kācukaḷum veṉīciyak kācukaḷum, paṟaṅkippēṭṭi mokarākkaḷum, aitarāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa aitāri, poṉ mokarākkaḷ, cutāriyavarākaṉkaḷ ākiyaviyum, marāttiyariṉ urupākkācukaḷum, ceṉṉiyil acciṭappeṟṟa naṭcattira varākaṉkaḷ, ceṉṉi varākaṉkaḷ, paṇaṅkaḷ, tuṭṭukaḷ mutaliyaṉavum kuṟippiṭat takkaṉavākum. mējar jeṉaral) vayavar tāmacu maṉṟō (1820 - 187) ceṉṉi āḷunarāyiruntapōtu ik kācukaḷ yāvum oḻikkappaṭṭup putiya urupāccācukaḷ acciṭappaṭṭaṉa. cātāraṇa varākaṉ mūṉṟu urupāyākavum, naṭcattira varākaṉ mūṉṟari urupāyākavum matippiṭap peṟṟaṉa. ceṉṉiyilirunta kācu acciṭumcāli putiya neṟimuṟikaḷiṉ paṭi kācukaḷi veḷiyiṭṭatu. necavu 
ik kālattil ceṉṉi mānilattuṭaṉ naṭanta vāṇikattāl veḷināṭṭu vaṇikarē perumporuḷ īṭṭiṉar. kuṟippāka, āṅkila vaṇikakkuḻu koḻutta ūtiyatti ilāpatti aṭintu vantatu.

āṅkilēyarkaḷit tavira, piṟa nāṭṭār intiyāvuṭaṉ vāṇikam ceyyat taṭi vitikkappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl intiyap poruḷiyal muṭamākkappaṭṭatu. tamiḻaka necavāḷarkaḷuṭaṉ āṅkilakkuḻuvār vaṇika oppantam ceytukoṇṭu necavut toḻilit taṅkaḷiṉ taṉiyurimiyākkip pallāyiram kuṭumpaṅkaḷi aḻittaṉar. āṅkilak kuḻuvār vēṇṭāta tuṇikaḷi neytu vēṟiṭattil viṟṟup poru vīṭṭiya necavāḷariṉ viralkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. uyartarap paruttikaḷikkupavār ilaṅkācayarukku ēṟṟumati ceytu, nālāka nūṟṟu. āṭiyāka neytu intiyā pōṉṟa kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaḷit taṅkaḷiṉ cantikaḷākikkoṇṭu av vāṭikaḷip paṉmaṭaṅku ūtiyattiṟku ilāpattiṟku viṟṟaṉar. itip pōlavē paki mūlapporuḷkaḷiyum iṅkilāntukku ēṟṟicceṉṟu ceyporuḷkaḷāka māṟṟit taṅkaḷiṉ paranta kuṭiyēṟṟac cantikaḷil viṟṟup paṇam kuvittaṉar. - piṟa poruḷkaḷ 
tamiḻakattil kiṭitta kaṭal muttukaḷ, naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷ, cāyavakikaḷ, carkkarip poruḷkaḷ, apiṉi, kañcā, tēkku, karuṅkāli, cemmaram mutaliya kāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ iṅkilāntukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. poṉ, cempu, tuttanākam, veḷḷīyam, kārīyam ākiya māḻi (ulōkap poruḷkaḷum, matuvakikaḷum, kutirikaḷum, kaṇṇāṭipporuḷkaḷum, mēlināṭukaḷiliruntu iṟakkumati ceyyap 
tuṟimukaṅkaḷ 
ceṉṉi maṇṭila vaṇika vaḷarccikkut tuṟimukaṅkaḷ peritum utaviṉa. ceṉṉit tuṟimukam1921ām āṇṭilum, vicākappaṭṭiṉam tuṟimukam 1925 ām āṇṭilum periya tuṟimukaṅkaḷukkāṉa ēntuka ḷuṭaṉ amikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivi iraṇṭittavira 104 ciṟiya tuṟi mukaṅkaḷ ceṉṉi maṇṭilattiṉ iru kaṇkaḷilum iruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ mikavum periyavi tārattukkuṭi, koccit tuṟimukaṅkaḷākum, ceṉṉit tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉam ceyaṟkiyāṉatu. iṉṟuḷḷa itaṉ kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭa 1875-l aṭikkal nāṭṭappaṭṭu 1881-l kaṭṭi muṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarcciyaṭinta it tuṟimukam iṉṟu tamiḻakattiṉ taliciṟanta tuṟimukamākak kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu. koccit tuṟimukam1920, ām āṇṭu toṭaṅki 1928 ām āṇṭu kaṭṭi muṭikkap paṭṭatu. 227 ēkkar parappil toṭaṅkappeṟṟa it tuṟimukattil 20 liruntu 30 vari ciṟu kappalkaḷ niṉṟaṉa. ceṉṉi, koccit tuṟi mukaṅkaḷ orē kaḻakattāl āḷappaṭṭaṉa. it tuṟimukaṅkaḷuṭaṉ toṭarvaṇṭip pātikaḷum iṇikkappaṭṭaṉa. vicākappaṭṭiṉam tuṟimukattuṭaṉ vaṅkāḷa - nākapurit toṭarvaṇṭip pātikaḷ iṇintaṉa. maṉṉār vaḷikuṭāvil aminta tūttukkuṭiṭat

tāynila varalāṟu 
tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉam paṇṭikkālam mutalē ciṟappuṟṟa tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉamāka viḷaṅki vantatu. 1911-2017 ilakkam urupā celaviṭappaṭṭu it tuṟimukam mēlum virivākkappaṭṭatu. 1926-l tuṟimukak kāppukkaḻakam amikkappeṟṟatu. iṉṟuḷḷa tuṟimukaṅkaḷ ik kaḻakap poṟuppāṭciyiṉ kīḻuḷḷaṉa. toḻiṟcālikaḷ 
tamiḻakattil palatiṟappaṭṭa kittoḻilkaḷum, kinuṭpat toḻilkaḷum toṉṟutoṭṭu naṭantuvantaṉa. 1905-l ivaṟṟip parappuvataṟkut toḻil nuṭpam toḻiṟkūṭaṅkaḷiṉ iyakkunar oruvar amarttappaṭṭār.1908-l utakiyil kūṭiya mānāṭṭil mutalāḷiyarum, toḻilnuṭpak kaliñarkaḷum kalantukoṇṭu toḻil vaḷarcciyip paṟṟi āyntaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ muṭivuppaṭi toḻiṟkūṭaṅkaḷip purakkum iyakkunarakam 1914-l nirantaramāka amikkappeṟṟatu. itaṉi aṭuttut toḻilkaḷ toṭarpāṉa aṟivurikaḷiyum, ākkap paṇikaḷiyum payiṟcikaḷiyum virivupaṭuttum palvēṟu atikārikaḷ amarttappaṭṭa ṉar. 1923-l toḻiṟcālikaḷukku aracu utavum caṭṭamum naṭimuṟi yākkappaṭṭatu. toḻiṟcālikaḷiṉ kaḻakamum amikkap peṟṟatu. itaṉāl, toḻiṟcālikaḷ putiyamuṟiyil virivaṭintaṉa. putiya toḻiṟcālikaḷ toṭaṅkappeṟṟaṉa. kittoḻilkaḷ, kuṭicit toḻilkaḷ, vēḷāṇmittuṇit toḻilkaḷ ākiyavi ēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. nīrūṟṟuk kuḻāyk kiṇaṟukaḷit tōṇṭutal, kuḻāykaḷ mūlam nīriṟittal ākiya toḻil nuṭpaṅkaḷ virivaṭintatāl vēḷāṇmi vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. toḻiṟ kalvikkūṭaṅkaḷ niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. toḻil vāṇikam toṭarpāṉa ceytikaḷic cērttaḷikkum niliyamum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. putiya kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷiyum, aṟiviyaṟpaṭi toḻilkaḷi virivākkum muṟikaḷiyum inta niliyam makkaḷukku aṟivikkattoṭaṅkiyatu. ceṉṉiyil niṟuvappaṭ kalippaḷḷiyilum pala āyvukaḷ mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. iṅku 1898-l 'alumiṉiya ' ēṉaṅkaḷ ceyyavum muyaṟci mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. ēṉaṅkaḷum, palvēṟu taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum alumiṉiyattāl ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. potumakkaḷum, paṭiyiṉarum alumiṉiyattālāṉa poruḷ kaḷip peritum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. itaṉāl, ittoḻil mikak kuṟukiya kālattiṟkuḷ māperum vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. it toḻiṟkūṭatti 1903 viruntu aracē mēṟkoṇṭu naṭattiyatu. 
aṭuttu, mikapperiya toḻilākat tamiḻakattil karutappaṭṭatu tōl pataṉiṭum toḻilākum, uḻavarkaḷiṉ kapiliyēṟṟattiṟku vēṇṭiya toṇṭam, cāl tikkat tōl atikam payaṉpaṭṭatu. āṉāl, itaṉic cariyākap pataṉiṭum muṟi akkālattil aṟiyappaṭāta tāl ivi orucila tiṅkaḷē uḻittaṉa. 1903 ām āṇṭu aracu īrāyiram 
kipi. 19, 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tamiḻakam 
131 
urupā otukkik kalip paḷḷiyil tōl pataṉiṭum muṟiyi āyvu ceytu veṟṟikaṇṭatu. intap putiya pataṉiṭum muṟikku rōm" pataṉiṭutal eṉṟu peyar. itaṉaṭippaṭiyiltāṉ ceṉṉiyi aṭutta kurōmpōṭṭi, pataṉiṭum toḻiṟpēṭṭiyāka māṟiyatu. ip putiya muṟi virivaṭintatum tōṟporuḷkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyum uyarntatu. ittoḻilāl tamiḻakam mikapperiya veḷināṭṭuc celāvaṇipip peṟṟatu. kuṟippākac ceruppukaḷ, pātakkūṭukaḷ (pūṭcukaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟāl perum varuvāy kiṭittatu. 1971-l aracē mikapperiya tōl pataṉiṭum toḻiṟcāliyi amittatu. 
toḻiṟkūṭaṅkaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyāl ēṟpaṭṭa kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷum vaḷarccikaḷum ellāt tuṟikaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. mēlē kūṟiya necavut toḻilil vicittaṟikaḷāl neyyum muṟi pukuttappaṭṭatu, itil mutalil kittuṇṭukaḷ neytu veṟṟi kaṇṭapiṉ cēlikaḷ mutaliyaṉa neyyappaṭṭaṉa. 
1906-l cēlattil necavāli oṉṟu toṭaṅkappeṟṟatu. itilum piṉṉart tōṟṟuvikkappeṟṟa necavālikaḷilum vicittaṟikaḷ pukuttap paṭṭu it toḻil putiya muṟiyil virivākkappaṭṭatu. ivaṟṟil parutti, paṭṭu, tāvari (kampaḷa! āṇkaḷum neyyappaṭṭaṉa. taṉiyār palarālum āṅkilak kuḻuvār kūṭṭuṟavālum necavālikaḷ pala toṭaṅkap peṟṟaṉa. kōviyilum ataṉ cuṟṟuppuṟaṅkaḷilum necavālikaḷum pañcālikaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. 
1914 ām āṇṭu ēṟpaṭṭa mutal ulakappōrukkuk karikkōl peṉcil atikam tēvippaṭṭatu. 1915-l karikkōl uruvākkattiṟ keṉṟē periya peṉcil toḻiṟcāli toṭaṅkappeṟṟatu. piṉṉar it toḻiṟcāli mikapperiya aḷavil vaḷarntatu. 1979-l utakiyil paḻaccāṟu piḻintu puṭṭikaḷil aṭikkum toḻiṟcāli toṭaṅkap peṟṟatu. itip pōlavē curumpāṉalakaḷum, carkkariyālikaḷum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. 1920-l karumpu payiriṭum toḻilik kaṇkāṇikkum poṟuppu, vēḷāṇmi iyakkunariṭam oppaṭikkappaṭṭatu. 1926-l kūṭṭuṟavuc carkkariyāli oṉṟum toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. iṉṟu tamiḻakattil vēḷāṇmiyiṉ tuṇit toḻilākavum taṉic ciṟappuṭiya toḻilākavumuḷḷatu karumpālit toḻilē. 
ivvāṟu vaḷarcciyaṭinta toḻilkaḷāl tamiḻakattil toḻiṟ kaḻakaṅkaḷum, toḻilāḷar caṭṭaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. toḻiṟ kalviyum, kalvik kūṭaṅkaḷum taṉittuṟiyiṉ kīḻ virivaṭintaṉa. vēḷāṇmi 
namnāṭṭiṉ uyirnāṭiyākat tikaḻvatu vēḷāṇmi , ki.pi.19, 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil pala māṟṟaṅkaḷum vaḷarcciyum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. 
132 
tāynila varalāṟu 
āyiṉum, putiya muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟip payiriṭātatālum paruvamaḻi poyttatālum pala pañcaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. āṅkilariṉ ēṟṟumati kaḷil mukāmi peṟṟa parutti, carkkari, pukiyili pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷiyē makkaḷ peritum payiriṭṭaṉar. iviyum ik kālap paṟṟākkuṟikkum, pañcattiṟkum karaṇiyamāyiṉa, 
vēḷāṇmik kalviyi vaḷarppataṟkākac ceṉṉi ciyatu citāppēṭṭiyil 1854-l 350 kuṟukkam (ēkkar nilattil oru paṇṇiyamikkappaṭṭatu. itil iṅkilāntiliruntu iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭa uḻavuk karuvikaḷik koṇṭu payiriṭum muṟi kaṟpikkap paṭṭatu. ceyaṟki muṟiyil uram uruvākkum muṟiyum kaṟpikkap paṭṭatu. 1875-l iṅkē oru vēḷāṇmik kallūri ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ik kallūriyi nilavarittuṟiyē kaṇkāṇittatu. 1884-l kalvittuṟi - iyakkunariṉ kaṇkāṇippukku itu māṟṟappaṭṭatu. 1905-l ēṟpaṭṭa vēḷāṇmit tuṟiyiṉ taṉiyāṭciyiṉkīḻ ik kallūri koṇṭuvarap paṭṭapiṉ uyarmaṭṭa vēḷāṇmik kalviyik kaṟpikkum kallūriyāka vum, tamiḻnāṭu muḻumikkum talimi vēḷāṇmik kalvikkūṭamāka vum māṟiyatu. vēḷāṇmit tuṟiyiṉ kiḷikaḷ māvaṭṭantōṟum toṭaṅkappeṟṟaṉa. ik kallūriyil payiṟci peṟṟōr māvaṭṭaṅ kaḷil putiya muṟikaḷil payiriṭum muṟiyik kaṟpittum, ceyalmuṟi viḷakkam aḷittu vantaṉar. 1906-l vēḷāṇmit tuṟikkeṉat taṉi iyakkunar amarttappaṭṭār. avarukkut tuṇiyākat tuṇi iyakkunar iru varum, poṟiyāḷarum amarttappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl ciyatu (citāppēṭṭiyiliruntu vēḷāṇmikkalluāri, kalvi iyakkunariṭa miruntu vēḷāṇmi iyakkunariṉ āṭcikku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. 1908-l ciyatuppēṭṭiyilirunta vēḷāṇmik kallūri kōyampattūrukku māṟṟap paṭṭatu. kōyampattūrukku māṟṟam peṟṟapiṉ parutti, karumpu, mutaliyaṉa payiriṭum putiya muṟikaḷik kaṇṭupiṭippatilum, vētiyal uram, oṭṭu vitikaḷ mutaliyaṉa kaṇṭupiṭippatilum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyatu. itaṟkāṉa palvēṟu atikārikaḷum aṟiviyalāḷarum viññāṉikaḷum amarttappaṭṭaṉar. iṉṟu kōvik kallūri, vēḷāṇmikkalviyil pala tiṟappaṭṭa uyar paṭṭap paṭippukaḷiyum, āyvukkūṭaṅkaḷiyum koṇṭu taṉip palkalikkaḻakamākat tikaḻkiṟatu. iṉṟuvari iḵtu āṟṟiyuḷḷa paṇikaḷ eṇṇilaṭaṅkā. iṉṟu ūrāṭci oṉṟiyaṅkaḷ tōṟum vēḷāṇmit tuṟi atikārikaḷ putiya muṟiyil payiriṭum muṟikaḷi viḷakki araciṉ utaviyāl vēḷāḷarukkup pala caluki kaḷi nalkip payaṉaṭiyac ceykiṉṟaṉar. payirkaḷukku varum nōykaḷ taṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kuṟukiya kālattil mikutiyāka viḷiyum payirkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa, parutti payiriṭum toḻilum, karumpu payiriṭum toḻilum mēli nāṭukaḷippōl muṉṉēṟiyuḷḷaṉa. 
11) 
kipi 18, 2017 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tamiḻakam 
kālnaṭi vaḷarcci | 
kālnaṭi vaḷarcciyum vēḷāṇmittuṟiyum, iru kaṇkaḷ pōṉṟavi. 1897 vari kālnaṭi vaḷarcciyik kaṇkāṇikkum tuṟi, vēḷāṇmittuṟiyiṉ oru pakutiyākavē iruntatu. piṉṉar taṉit tuṟiyākik kālnaṭikaḷukku varum nōykaḷ, tīṉi, vaḷarppu muṟi mutaliyaṉa āyappaṭṭaṉa. taṉiyatikārikaḷ ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉar. 1903-l taṉiyē kālnaṭi maruttuvakkallūri oṉṟu ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. nāṭu muḻuvatum kālnaṭi maruttuvamaṉikaḷ amikkappeṟṟaṉa. 1930-31-l 18 maruttuvamaṉikaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟu uyarpaṭṭappaṭippukaḷ. kūṭiya kālnaṭi maruttuvak kallūri ceṉṉiyil amikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. itil payiṉṟa maruttuvarkaḷ māvaṭṭak kālnaṭi maruttuvarkaḷ ḷākavum, kālnaṭi virivākka ārāycciyāḷarkaḷākavum uḷḷaṉar. kālnaṭip parappu, virivākkam mutaliyavaṟṟil tēṟiyavarkaḷum paṇi yāṟṟukiṉṟaṉar. ceyaṟki muṟiyil karuttarikkacceytu ciṟanta kālnaṭikaḷi uruvākkam ceyyum muṟikaḷum, nōyttaṭuppu muṟikaḷum iṉṟu pukaḻ peṟṟu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ciṟappākak kōḻi vaḷarppu, mīṉ vaḷarppu, paṉṟi vaḷarppu mutaliyaṉa pala ariya tiṭṭaṅkaḷiṉkīḻc ceyalpaṭṭuvarukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu itu palkalikkaḻakamāka uyarntuḷḷatu. nīrppācaṉattuṟi 
vēḷāṇmikku uṟutuṇiyāṉatu nīrppācaṉam. 1930-31 ām āṇṭil 45 kal nīḷamuḷḷa kālvāykaḷ iruntaṉa. itil pāp kal nīḷam vēḷāṇmi nīrppācaṉattiṟkum, maṟṟatu pōkkuvarattiṟkum payaṉpaṭṭatu. kālappōkkil nīrppācaṉa ēntukaḷ atikarikkappaṭṭu vēḷāṇmi vaḷarcci aṭintatu. tamiḻakattil ōṭum kāvēriyāṟṟi viruntu kālvāykaḷiṉ mūlamum, aṇikkaṭṭukaḷiṉ mūlamum nīrp pācaṉa ēntukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl, palavāyiram kuṟukkam (ēkkarkaḷ) mūṉṟu pōkam viḷiccal kāṇum nilaṅkaḷāyiṉa. kāvēri yilum, koḷḷiṭattilum ōṭivarum nīri vīṇākkāmal pakirntaḷikka mēlaṇi kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. pēraṇiyum cīrtirutti amikkappaṭṭa tōṭu kālvāykaḷum cīrāka amikkappaṭṭaṉa. kāvēriyip pōlavē kōtāvari, kiruṭṭiṇi yāṟukaḷum vēḷāṇmikkup payaṉpaṭum vakiyil avaṟṟiṉ kuṟukkē aṇikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiliruntu pala kālvāykaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. kañcam māvaṭṭattil amikkap. paṭṭa 'rucikulyāt tiṭṭam' nīrit tēkkivikkavum, nēraṭiyāka nīrpāyccavum payaṉpaṭṭatu. tamiḻakattil maturi, tirunelvēli māvaṭṭaṅkaḷukkup periyāṟṟut tiṭṭam vēḷāṇmikkup payaṉpaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. piṉṉar ēṟpaṭṭa mēṭṭūr aṇit tiṭṭam, tuṅka" pattiri nīrttēkkam, kiruṭṭiṇi nīrttēkkam mutaliyaṉavum, pala ciṟiya pācaṉattiṭṭaṅkaḷum mānilattil vēḷāṇmiyi vaḷamuṟac ceytaṉa. 
134 
tāynila varalāṟu 
iṉṟu ittuṟi mikacciṟappuṭaṉ ceyalpaṭṭu varukiṟatu. vēḷāṇmi vaḷarccik kēntācuk kūṭṭuṟavuk kaḻakaṅkaḷum, vippakaṅcuḷum vaḷarcci yaṭintaṉa. (i) pañcam 
nāṭṭilēṟpaṭum pañcattip paṟṟariya vivaraṅkaḷic cērkkavum, pañcamēṟpaṭṭa pakutikaḷil pañca oḻippu vēlikaḷ mēṟkoḷḷavum, iṉip pañcam varāmal taṭukkum vaḻivakikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷavum nila aḷaviyāḷarum, puḷḷi vivaram cēkarippōrum, nilavarit tuṟiyiṉ rumē ciṟanta paṇiyāḷarāvar. ivarkaḷ tarum vivaraṅkaḷik koṇṭu tāṉ pañcanīkka vēlikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷamuṭiyum. kiḻāṉ telkavuci ṭelheḷaci 1848-1856) kālattil tiruttiyamikkappaṭṭa potup paṇittuṟic cīrtiruttaṅkaḷiṉpaṭi ovvoru mānilattilum potup paṇikaḷi āyum āṇikkuḻu amikkappaṭṭatu. ak kuḻuviṉ parinturikaḷiṉpaṭi ovvoru mānilat talinakarilum oru potup paṇittuṟi aluvalakam ēṟpaṭṭatu. atil pala poṟiyāḷarum, palvēṟu aluvalarum paṇiyāṟṟiṉar. ivarkaḷ yāvarum iṅkilānti viruntu vantavarkaḷ. kālvāykaḷ veṭṭutal, pālaṅkaḷ kaṭṭutal, neṭumpātikaḷ amittal mutaliya paṇikaḷi ittuṟi mēṟkoṇ ṭatu. 1854 ām āṇṭiṟkuḷ pōtumāṉa potuppaṇittuṟi aluval kaṅkaḷ niṟuvappaṭṭu it tuṟi virivākkappaṭṭatu. intiyāviluḷḷa pala āṟukaḷiliruntu kālvāykaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa; akaṇakaḷ kaṭṭap paṭṭaṉa. ataṉpaṭi 1850]-l kiruṭṭiṇa yāṟṟukkuk kuṟukkē aṇi kaṭṭappeṟṟatu. kāviriyiṉ kuṟukkēyum, akaṇa kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. 18:5f-l ceṉṉaṉayiliruntu arakkōṇam vari toṭarvaṇṭippāti pōṭappaṭṭatu. 1854l ceṉṉiyiliruntu peṅkaḷūr variyilum ip pāti nīṇṭatu. 1873-l arakkōṇattiliruntu ciriccūr vari toṭar vaṇṭippāti pōṭappaṭṭatu. 1877 ām āṇṭukkuḷ ceṉṉi - arakkōṇam pāti iraṭṭippāti yāyiṟṟu. itip pōlavē 1853-liruntu 1856kkuḷ ceṉṉi nakaram kalkattā, pecāvar, pampāy ākiya nakaraṅkaḷuṭaṉ tantik kampiyāl iṇikkappaṭṭatu. neṭum pātikaḷ pōṭappaṭṭaṉa. vaṇikac cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ mēṟ koḷḷap paṭṭaṉa. āyiṉum nāṭṭil aṭikkaṭi pañcamēṟpaṭṭatu. 'tātu - īcuvara" pañcam (1876-78) 
kipi1870iliruntu 1878 vari ceṉṉi mānilattil paṉṉiraṇṭu māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil paruvamaḻi poyttatāl pañcamēṟpaṭṭatu. itaṉit tātu īcuvarapañcam eṉpar. ip pañcattāl pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ māṇṭaṉar. kuṟippāka, vēluār māvaṭṭattiltāṉ ip pāñcattāl makkaḷiḻappu atikamēṟpaṭṭatu. uṇaviṉ makkaḷ kaṟṟāḻi, mūṅkilarici, mūṅkil kuṟuttu, koṭṭikkiḻaṅku, pullarici, taḻikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟit tiṉṟaṉar. caḷimaṇ tiṉṟu palar māṇṭaṉar. 
kipi. 19, 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tamiḻakam 
135 
pañca oḻippup paṇikaḷ kareṅkilum mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. pellārikkum ūpḷikkumiṭiyēyuḷḷa toṭarvaṇṭip pāti ip pañca nīkkap paṇiyiṉ oru pakutiyākat toṭaṅki muṭikkappeṟṟatu. itaṟku 6,55,710 urupā celaviṭappaṭṭatu. itippōlavē pala toṭarvaṇṭippātikaḷ putitāka amikkum paṇiyum, cīrtiruttum paṇiyum mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. neṭumpātikaḷ amittal allatu cīrpaṭuttutal, kālvāy, kuḷaṅkaḷ tōṇṭutal, tūreṭuttal mutaliya paṇikaḷum mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa, nāṭeṅkilum kañcittoṭṭikaḷ vikkappaṭṭaṉa. ceṉṉi mānilattil maṭṭum ip pañca oḻippukku 6,30,9244 urupāy celaviṭappaṭṭatu. varikaḷ taḷḷupaṭi ceyyappaṭ ṭaṉa, 1,21,26,000 urupā. iv varittaḷḷupaṭiyāl aracukku varuvāy kuṟintatu. toṟṟunōy paravāmal taṭuppu naṭavaṭikkikaḷ mēṟkoḷ ṉappaṭṭaṉa. ip pañca oḻippup paṇiyiṉ oru kūṟākap pakkiṅkām kālvāyiṉ orupakuti veṭṭappaṭṭatu. ceṉṉit tuṟimukam 200 kuṟukkam ēkkar parappil 37 aṭi āḻattiṟkut tōṇṭum paṇi 1875-l toṭaṅkap peṟṟu 1887-l muṭikkappeṟṟatu. pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa tamiḻarkaḷ kuṭipeyarntu teṉṉāppirikkā, ilaṅki, picittīvu, parmā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkut tōṭṭakkūlikaḷāyc ceṉṟu viṭṭaṉar, tarakarkaḷ (kaṅkātarikaḷ ivarkaḷi aṭimikaḷ pōl vili pēci innāṭukaḷil viṟṟaṉar. āppirikka nāṭṭu āvaṇaṅkaḷil "vilaṅkāṇṭikaḷ', 'anāka ricuk kuṭikaḷ' eṉṟellām it tamiḻarkaḷ pativu ceyyappaṭṭaṉar. kāntiyaṭikaḷ teṉṉāppirikkāvil pāṭuṭippu (cattiyākkirakam) toṭaṅkiyatē inta āvaṇak kuṟippukaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu tāṉ eṉpatu īṇṭu niṉivukūrattakkatu. 
ik kālattil aracappaṭinikarāḷiyākavirunta iliṭṭaṉ kiḻāṉ (ki.pi.1876-1880) 1878-l pañcanīkka āyvukkuḻu oṉṟi amittāṉ. ak kuḻuviṉ talivaṉāṉa vayavar iriccarṭu tempiḷ nāṭeṅkilum kaṟṟi acariya parinturikaḷi aracukku aḷittāṉ. intiyā muḻuvatum paraviya ip pañcattāl.īrilakkam makkaḷ māṇṭaṉar. patiṉōru kōṭi urupā celaviṭappaṭṭatu. atil pātikkumēl (630,244 urupā) ceṉṉi mānilattiṟkuc celaviṭappaṭṭatu. iraṇṭu kōṭi varittaḷḷu paṭi ceyyappaṭṭatu, tempiṉ kuḻuviṉ parinturippaṭi pala pañca nīkka vēlikaḷ mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. āṇṭutōṟum varavu celavut tiṭṭattil oru kuṟippiṭṭa toki pañca nīkkattiṟkeṉa otukkappaṭ ṭatu. pañcam varāmal taṭukkum muṉṉēṟpāṭukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. vēḷāṇmi, toḻil, kittoḻil, vaṇika muṉṉēṟṟap paṇikaḷ mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkut toṭarvaṇṭip pātikaḷum, pōkkuvarattuēntukaḷum utaviṉa. 1857-l pañcanīkkac caṭṭam koṇṭu ivarappaṭṭatu.

tāynila varalāṟu 
kkāṟṟu nāṭukaḷilum tam tiruvaḷma puttūr, 
1891-82 ām āṇṭukaḷil mīṇṭum ceṉṉi mānilattil oru koṭiya pañcamēṟpaṭṭatu. 1890-91 ām āṇṭukaḷil vaṭakiḻakkup paruvakkāṟṟu poyttatu. paruvamaḻi peyyavilli. itaṉāl aṭutta 1891-92 ām āṇṭukaḷilum pañcam ēṟpaṭṭatu. ceṉṉi mānilattil kaṭappi, nellūr, kāñcipuram, tiruvaḷḷūr, vēlūr, tiruvaṇṇā mali, viḻuppuram, kaṭalūr, īrōṭu, kōyamputtūr, maturi, tēṉi, tiṇṭukkal, tūttukkuṭi, tirunelvēli, malapār mutaliya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ peritum pātikkappaṭṭaṉa. aracu pañca nīkkap paṇikaḷukkāka 18,44139 urupā celaviṭṭatu. 1990 ām āṇṭu mutal 1897 ām āṇṭu variyil varikaḷ taḷḷupaṭi ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.vilakkuc ceyyappaṭṭavarittoki 40b8,137urupā ākum. kiṇaṟu veṭṭa māṭukaḷ vāṅka, nilaccīrtiruttam ceyyak kaṭaṉkaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. 
1896-97 ām āṇṭukaḷil mīṇṭum ceṉṉi mānilattil pañcam ēṟpaṭṭatu. ip pañcam intiyā muḻuvatum kāṇappaṭṭatu. vaṭaparmā vilum ēṟpaṭṭatu. ceṉṉi maṇṭilattil kañcam, vicākappaṭṭiṉam, karu nūl, pellāri, aṉantappūr, kaṭappi mutaliya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ peritum pātikkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉippōkkap payiriṭuvōrukku nilaccīrtiruttam ceyyavum, māṭukaḷ, māṭṭut tīvaṉaṅkaḷ vāṅkavum, vitikaḷ, uṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa vāṅkavum cumār 24 kōṭi urupā cuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. itaṟkāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷum koṇṭuvarappaṭṭaṉa. aracukkuc contamāṉa kāṭukaḷ mēyccalukku viṭappaṭṭaṉa. 1897-l ceṉṉiyāḷunar pañca nīkkappaṇikaḷi āynturikka ōr āṇik kuḻuvi aḻittāṉ. intiyāviṉ aracap paṭinikarāḷiyāka virunta iraṇṭām elciṉ (1894-99), vayavar. cēmcul eṉpavaṉiṉ talimi yil oru pañca āyvuk kuḻuvi amittāṉ. 1896-l 'piḷēkku" nōy ēṟpaṭṭatu. cīṉak kappal pampāy vantatum intiyāvil paraviya innōy nāṭu muḻuvatum paraviyatu. itaṉi āḻikka aracu kaṭum naṭavaṭikkikaḷi mēṟkoṇṭatu. itaṉāl āṅkila atikārikaḷukkum potumakkaḷukku miṭiyē mōtalkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. 1897-l pūṉāvil intu oruvariṉ vīṭṭil pukunta piḷēkkut taṭuppu āṅkila atikārikaḷ kollappaṭṭa ṉar. ceṉṉiyil 19pāl mīṇṭumoru pañcanīkka āyvukkuḻu ēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭatu. ikkuḻu toṭarvaḻi, cālip pōkkuvarattukaḷi virivupaṭutta vum, nīrppācaṉa ēntukaḷic ceyyavum, taricu nilaṅkaḷip paṇpaṭutta vum kūṭṭuṟavuk kaḻakaṅkaḷi amikkavum parinturittatu. 
1880 ām āṇṭu pañca āyvukkuḻuviṉ parinturikkēṟpa, kiruṭṭiṇi, kōtāvari āṟṟuppāyccalkaḷ virivupaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. kāviri nīrppacāṉa ēntukaḷ virivāyiṉa. pellāriyil tuṅkapattiriyil liruntu kālvāykaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. kañcattil rucikuliyā tiṭṭam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. cēlattil mēṭṭūr aṇittiṭṭam toṭaṅkap paṭṭatu. pala ērikaḷum nīrttēkkaṅkaḷum kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.

73 intiya nayaṉmiyar kuṟṟavaḻakkukaḷi ucāvavum amarttap paṭṭaṉar. kuṟṟa vāḻakkukaḷi ucāvat taṉi nītimaṉṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. muṟimaṉṟa nayaṉmiyar ceṣaṉs nītipatikaḷ, tuṇimuṟimaṉṟa nayaṉmiyar ceṣaṉs nītipatikaḷ, māvaṭṭa naṭuvar (mājisṭ rēṭkaḷ, tuṇi naṭuvar (mājisṭrēṭkaḷ, mānila naṭuvar (mājisṭi rēṭkaḷ), vaṭṭa naṭuvar (mājisṭrēṭkaḷ), naṭuvarāya nayaṉmiyar peṉc mājisṭrēṭkaḷ), matippaminaṭuvar (kavurava mājisṭ rēṭkaḷ), paṭinakara naṭuvar (mājisṭrēṭkaḷ) mutaliya palatarappaṭṭa kuṟṟa ucāval nayaṉmiyar amarttappaṭṭaṉar. 1890-l ceṉṉi mānilattil 52 naṭuvarāya maṉṟaṅkaḷē peṉc kōrṭṭu iruntaṉa, āṉāl, 1930-l itu 2017 āka uyarntatu. itip pōlavē matippami naṭuvarkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyum uyarntukoṇṭē pōyiṟṟu, peṇ cuḷum matippumi naṭuvarāka amarttappaṭṭaṉar. 1930-l ceṉṉi yil iḷaṅkuṟṟavāḷikaḷukkeṉa oru nayaṉmi maṉṟam (Juveniel Court) ēṟpaṭṭatu. itaṟkuc campaḷam peṟum nayaṉmiyarum, matippami nayaṉmiyarumāka iruvar amarttappaṭṭaṉar. itil matippumi nayaṉmiyarākap peṇkaḷē vaḻakkamāka amarttappaṭṭaṉar. 
ivvāṟu nayaṉmi potumakkaḷukku mika eḷitil caṭṭappaṭi camamākak kiṭittatu. kuṟippāka vaḻakkukaḷi ucāva intiyar mēlum mēlum amarttappaṭṭaṉar. kumukāyattil ciṟanta periya māntar matippami nayaṉmiyarāka amarttappaṭṭaṉar. paṭiyiṉarukkum, ciṟuvarkaḷukkum taṉi nayaṉmi maṉṟaṅkaḷ kaṇṭōṉmeṇṭ, jūveṉaṉal ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. peṇkaḷum naṉmiyarāyiṉar. 1935 ām āṇṭu aracuc caṭṭattiṟkuppiṉ nāṭāḷum muṟi vaḷarcci peṟṟapiṉ nayaṉmi maṉṟaṅkaḷ perukiyatōṭu mēlum cīrpaṭṭaṉa. piṉṉar āṭcit tuṟiyiliruntu pirikkappaṭṭut taṉik kavaṉattuṭaṉ nayaṉmi vaḻaṅkap paṭṭatu. 1935ām āṇṭuc caṭṭappaṭi tilliyil oru vaṭṭamippu: nayaṉmi maṉṟam peṭaral nītimaṉṟam ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. itil

1894 ām āṇṭuc ciṟiccālikaḷ caṭṭattiṟku muṉ ciṟik kōṭṭaṅkaḷ yāvum uḷḷūrc caṭṭaṅkaḷiṉpaṭiyē purakkappaṭṭaṉa. inta āṇṭukkup piṉtāṉ ciṟiccālikaḷ araciṉ caṭṭattuṟiyiṉ kīḻ kavaṉikkappaṭṭatu. 1869-l niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭa ciṟikkāval caṭṭamum, 1887-l koṇṭu varappaṭṭa tiruttac caṭṭamum, kaṭiciyāka 1894-l tiruttappaṭṭatu. ciṟikaḷil orē mātiriyāṉa naṭimuṟi neṟikaḷ kaṭipiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟiccālikaḷ kāṟṟōṭṭa ēntukaḷuṭaṉ amikkappaṭṭu uṇavu, uṭi mutaliya ēntukaḷuṭaṉ --ā kāppaṭṭaṉa. kolikkuṟṟam ceytavarkaḷ atāvatu tākkut taṇṭaṉi peṟum kuṟṟavāḷikaḷukkut taṉi aṟikaḷ amikkap paṭṭaṉa. āṭumāṭukaḷip paṭṭiyil aṭippatu pōl kuṟṟavāḷikaḷi aṭikkāmal taṇṭaṉik kēṟpat takarittaṉiyaṟikaḷil aṭikkap paṭṭaṉar. 1919-l kuṟṟavāḷikaḷi nōyāḷikaḷip pōl naṭatti, avarkaḷum tiruntik kumukāyattil nalla māntarāka vāḻa vaḻi ceyyum caṭṭam koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum kuṟṟaṅkaḷiyē toṭarntu ceytu ciṟittaṇṭaṉip peṟum kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ piṟa kuṟṟavāḷikaḷuṭaṉ cērāmal taṉiyē vittuk kaṇkāṇikkappaṭṭaṉar. tirunti vāḻum vāṇāṭkuṟṟavāḷikaṭkut taṉiyē ciṉṉaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkap paṭṭup piṟakuṟṟavāḷikaḷi mēykkumpaṭi amarttappaṭṭaṉar. ciṟic cālikkuḷḷum "vakuppukaḷ' pirikkappaṭṭuk kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ pala calukikaḷ tuytta ṉar. 1930 ām āṇṭuc caṭṭappaṭi, 'e', 'pi', 'ci' eṉṉum pirivukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. kumukāyattil uyarnta vāḻkki ēntuka ḷuṭaṉ vāḻntu, mutaṉmuṟiyākak kuṟṟam cātāraṇa kuṟṟaṅkaḷ) ceytavarkaḷ 'e' vakuppilum, uyarvu, paṭippu, patavi ākiyavaṟṟil uyarntavarkaḷukku 'pi' vakuppilum iṭam koṭukkappaṭṭatu. nalla naṭattiyuṭiya kuṟṟavāḷikaḷiṉ taṇṭaṉikkālam kuṟikkappaṭṭatu, 
1973-74-l kuṟṟavāḷikaḷiṉ naṉṉaṭattiyik kaṇkāṇikkak kaṇkāṇippukku ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. oru kuṟṟavāḷi nalla naṭatti yuṭaṉ 23 pākat taṇṭaṉiyik kaḻittuviṭṭāl ik kuḻu mītit taṇṭaṉiyik kuṟittu viṭutali ceyyak kirutturi kūṟiyatu. kuṟṟavāḷikaḷukkuk kalviyaṟivum, camayap parappurikaḷum aḷikkap paṭṭaṉa. pala kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ accuk kōppavarkaḷākavum, pātakkūṭu tippavarkaḷākavum ippaṭip pala tuṟikaḷil payiṟci peṟṟu viṭutali yāṉavuṭaṉ piḻippukkum vaḻitēṭum vāyppuk kiṭittatu. ciṟanta nūlakamum ciṟiyil amikkappaṭṭatu. attōṭu kuṟṟavāḷikaḷuk

1857 ām āṇṭuc caṭṭappaṭiyē ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakam niṟuvap peṟṟatu. 1904 ām āṇṭil ēṟpaṭṭa caṭṭam palkalikkaḻakac ceyalkaḷi oppukkoṇṭu kalviyiṉ virivākkattiṟku vaḻikōliyatu. itē āṇṭil intiyak kalvic caṭṭam koṇṭuvarappaṭṭu nāṭu muḻuvatilumuḷḷa palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ ellikku uṭpaṭṭa kallūrikaḷukkāṉa pāṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷiyum, pāṭanūlkaḷiyum vakut taḷittu mēlāṇi celuttik kaṭṭuppaṭutta vaḻi ceytatu. uyar paṭṭap paṭippu vakuppukaḷip palkalikkaḻakap pērāciriyarkaḷē naṭattavum, ovvoru tuṟiyilum ārāyccikkūṭam amikkavum ēṟpāṭāyiṟṟu. āyiṉum, 1913 ām āṇṭu vari palkalikkaḻakam oru tērvuk kaḻaka mākavē tērvukaḷi naṭatti muṭivu kūṟum kaḻakam) ceyalpaṭṭatu. itaṟkuppiṉtāṉ palkalikkaḻakattil perumivāynta tiṇikkaḷa virukkikaḷ (IDepartimernalChirs) ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. mutaṉmuṟiyākac ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakattil intiya varalāṟu, tolliyal ākiya iraṇṭiṟkum oru tiṇikkaḷam amikkappaṭṭut tiṇikkaḷat talivarum amarttappaṭṭār. itippōlavē moḻit tiṇikkaḷa mum, intiyap poruḷiyal tuṇikkaḷamum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. 1923ām āṇṭu ceṉṉi mēlaviyil niṟivēṟṟappeṟṟa palkalikkaḻakac caṭṭappaṭi ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakam pāṭam payiṟṟuvikkum oru niṟuvaṉamākavum, taṉittiyaṅkum āṭci niṟuvaṉamākavum māṟṟappaṭṭatu. kaṇkāṇakar (Chancellor), tuṇik kaṇkāṇakar (Vice-Chanceller), iṇik kaṇkāṇakar (ProChariceller), āṭcikkuḻu pāṭattiṭṭakkuḻu mutaliya patavikaḷ ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ceṉṉi mānila āḷunar kaṇkānakar āṉār. kalviyamiccar, iṇik kaṇkāṇakar āṉār. ivarkaḷ viḻāt talivarkaḷē. muḻunēramum palkalikkaḻakatti naṭattic celvac campaḷam peṟum tuṇik kaṇkāṇakar amarttap paṭṭār. ivar āṭcik kuḻuvilirukkum uṟuppiṉar aivarāl tērn teṭukkappeṟṟu mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ pataviyiliruppār.

kalvi paravalāka vaḷaralāyiṟṟu. itil tēṟiyavarkaḷ aracup paṇikaḷukkum, kallūrip paṭippukkum cellat takutiyuṭiyavarkaḷ āyiṉar. uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷiṉ mēlāṭci, kalvi iyakkunariṉkīḻ vantatu. māvaṭṭak kalviyatikārikaḷum, makaḷir paḷḷi āyvāḷarkaḷum ivaṟṟik kaṇkāṇittaṉar. taṉiyār paḷḷikaḷukku aracu pōkka utaviyaḷittatu. pala paḷḷikaḷ māvaṭṭa, vaṭṭa nakarāṇmik kaḻakaṅkaḷālum naṭattap paṭṭaṉa. 1923-l aracu ovvoru māvaṭṭattilum kaḻaka uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷi ēṟpaṭutta vēṇṭumeṉum caṭṭattik koṇṭu vantatu. itaṉpaṭi, māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷiṉ uṟuppiṉar kuḻukkaḷē ivaṟṟip purakkavum vaki ceyyappaṭṭatu. nīlamali māvaṭṭam, ellippuṟap pakutikaḷil ittakiya paḷḷikaḷit taṉikkuḻukkaḷ naṭattiṉa. ik kaḻakaṅkaḷukkuk karutturiyum, aṟivuriyum kūṟum urimi maṭṭumē acarikkappaṭṭatu. 1928-l kaḻakaṅkaḷ tirutti amikkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉpaṭi kaḻakattalivarkaḷ (Presidents) uṟuppiṉarkaḷālēyē tērnteṭukkap paṭṭup paḷḷikaḷip purakkum pala urimikaḷip peṟṟaṉar. paḷḷi āṭcik kuḻuvil talimiyāciriyarkaḷum, āciriyarkaḷ cilarum iṭam peṟṟaṉar. itaṉāl, paḷḷikaḷiṉ eṇṉarikki perukiyatu. palarum paṭikka vāyppum ēṟpaṭṭatu. muslīm peṇkaḷum, tāḻttappaṭṭō rum kalviyēntupeṟat taṉic calukikaḷ aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟittavak kaḻakaṅkaḷ taṉittup pal paḷḷikaḷi naṭattiṉa. 1975 ām āṇṭu vari paḷḷip pāṭaṅkaḷ yāvum āṅkilattilēyē kaṟpikkappaṭṭaṉa. inta āṇṭiṟkup piṟakutāṉ tamiḻ, teluṅku, maliyāḷam, kaṉṉaṭam, arapi mutaliya moḻikaḷilum kaṟpikkum kaṭṭāyat tiṭṭam koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa ṭatu. itaṟku muṉṉum ittakiya tāymoḻikaḷilum kaṟpikkalā meṉṉum virupput terivu (0ption) iruntatu. āṉāl, kiṟittavak kaḻakap paḷḷikaḷ āṅkilattil maṭṭumē kaṟpittatālum, āṅkila vaḻippayiṉ ṟōrukku aracuppaṇi eḷitil kiṭittatālum, tāymoḻiyil payila māṇavarkaḷē muṉvaravilli. uyarnilip paḷḷik kalvi kāmarācar kālam vari ēḻiyeḷiyōrukkuk komput tēṉākavē iruntatu. ivartāṉ paṭṭitoṭṭikaḷilellām paḷḷikaḷit tiṟantu, naṇpakal uṇavaḷittu, tāy moḻiyilēyē paṭikkum vāyppiyum mutalil nalkiṉār. 
1881-l 'meṭrikulēcaṉ ' tērvukkuc ceṉṟavarkaḷiṉ eṇ ṇikki nam mānilam muḻuvatum 3,51pitāṉ. ari nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṉṉum ōraḷavē vaḷarcciyēṟpaṭṭatu. 1911-l tāṉ mutaṉmutal paḷḷi iṟutippaṭippu (S.S.L.C.) koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu. ataṟkumuṉ mēli nāṭṭu muṟiyil aminta 'meṭrikulēcaṉ' paṭippē iruntatu. 1925-l tāṉ tāymoḻikaḷilum kaṟpikkum pāṭattiṭṭamum neṟimuṟikaḷum kaṭṭāyamākkappaṭṭaṉa,

toṭakkappaḷḷip paṭippu 
1930, ām āṇṭuvari toṭakkappaḷḷip paṭippik kaṟpikkum paṇiyi vaṭṭa, oṉṟiyak kaḻakaṅkaḷum, nakarāṇmik kaḻakaṅkaḷum, taṉiyārum ceytaṉar. ivaṟṟil pala kiṟittavak kaḻakaṅkaḷāl naṭattap paṭṭaṉa. 1920 ām āṇṭil ceṉṉi mānilat toṭakkakkalvic caṭṭam koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. itaṟkuppiṉ toṭakkakkalvikkūṭaṅkaḷiyum aracē nēraṭiyākak kaṇkāṇittatu. toṭakkap paḷḷikaḷi āyvu ceyyum atikārikaḷum amarttappaṭṭaṉar. ūrāṭcik kaḻakaṅkaḷum, māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷum kalvikkeṉat taṉiyē varittaṇṭal ceyyum urimiyum peṟṟaṉa. camayac cārpil naṭattappaṭum paḷḷikaḷil camayakkalvi viruppap pāṭamākkappaṭṭatu. 
1923ām āṇṭiliruntu 500 pērukku mēl vāḻum ūrkaḷil toṭakkap paḷḷikaḷ toṭaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. toṭakkappaḷḷi uyarnilip paḷḷik kalviyiṉ toṭakkamākakkōviyākkappaṭṭatu. aracu utavi peṟṟut taṉiyārum paḷḷikaḷit toṭaṅkiṉar. tāḻttappaṭṭō rukkut toḻilāḷar nalattuṟi toṭakkappaḷḷikaḷit toṭaṅkiyatu. 1924-l paḷḷi cellum akaviyuṭiya kuḻantikaḷip paṟṟiya puḷḷi viḷakkam uruvākkappaṭṭatu. puḷḷiviḷakkaṅkaḷiṉpaṭi toṭakkakkalvi mānilam muḻuvatum paravumpaṭi paḷḷikaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa. 1927 ām āṇṭil ellā ūravikaḷilum paraviyē paḷḷikaḷi naṭattum caṭṭam koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. ittakiya muyaṟcikaḷāl toṭakkakkalvi ōraḷavu ellōrukkum eḷitil kiṭikkumāṟu amintatu. aracum āṇṭutōṟum oru kaṇicamāṉa tokiyit toṭakkappaḷḷik kalvikkeṉa otukkiyatu. poṟiyiyal kalvi 
1794-l ceṉṉic cēppākkattil toṭaṅkappeṟṟa poṟiyiyaṟ paḷḷiyil paṭittavarkaḷ poṟiyāḷarāka aracup paṇikaḷil amarntaṉar. ip paḷḷi 1859-l ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakattāl opputal vaḻaṅkap paṭṭatu, itu 1920-liruntu kiṇṭiyil māṇavar taṅkip payilum kallūri yāka māṟiyatu. 1917-lvicākappaṭṭiṉattil ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭa poṟiyiyaṟ paḷḷiyilum, 1920-l tirucciyil ēṟpaṭṭa poṟiyiyaṟ paḷḷiyilum paṭitta māṇavarkaḷum poṟiyāḷarkaḷ āyiṉar. 1930-l miṉpoṟip paṭṭap paṭippu kiṇṭiyil toṭaṅkappeṟṟatu. piṉṉar paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarntu pala tuṟikaḷilum paṭṭappaṭippukaḷum mēl paṭṭap paṭippukaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟu poṟiyiyaṟ kallūriyōṭu toḻilnuṭpak kalvik kūṭaṅkaḷ palavum uḷḷaṉa. kalvi iyakkunariṉ kīḻirunta ivi iṉṟu taṉittiyaṅkuvatōṭu ivaṟṟiṟkut taṉiyē iyakkunarkaḷum amarttappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉar.

tāynilavaralāṟu 
vaṇikaviyaṟ kalvi 
poṟiyiyaṟ kalviyip pōlavē vaṇikaviyaṟkalviyum taṉi aluvalariṉkīḻ vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. ceṉṉi, vicākappaṭṭiṉam, kōḻikkōṭu mutaliyaviṭaṅkaḷil ivi toṭaṅkappeṟṟaṉa. taṉiyārum ivi pōṉṟa kalvi niliyaṅkaḷit toṭaṅkiṉar. vēḷāṇmip paḷḷikaḷ, ceviṭar, pāmiyar paḷḷikaḷ mutaliyaṉa iyalātārukkum vāḻvaḷittaṉa. iṉṟu ivi taṉi vaḷarcci peṟṟuk kumukāyattiṉ mēṭupaḷḷaṅkaḷic camaṉ ceykiṉṟaṉa, maruttuvakkalvi 
tañcāvūr, irāyapuram ākiyaviṭaṅkaḷil iraṇṭu maruttuvap paḷḷikaḷ toṭaṅkappeṟṟaṉa. peṇkaḷukkeṉak kiṟittavak kaḻakattār vēlūril oru maruttuvap paḷḷiṉayat toṭaṅkiṉar. 1975-l nāṭṭu mūliki maruttuvak kalvikkūṭam ceṉṉiyil toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ceṉṉi maruttuvak kallūriyum, vicākappaṭṭiṉam maruttuvakkallūri yum toṭaṅkappeṟṟaṉa. ivi paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarntu ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakattālum, piṉṉar intiyap palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷālum ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭum paṭṭa, mēṟpaṭṭap paṭippukaḷik kaṟpittaṉa, iṉṟu ūrāṭcitōṟum maruttuvamaṉikaḷ perukiyuḷḷaṉa. maruttuvak kalvi mikavum muṉṉēṟiyuḷḷatu. 
vēlūril oru ciṟu paḷḷiyākat toṭaṅkappaṭṭa kiṟittavak kaḻaka maruttuvappaḷḷi iṉṟu ulakap pukaḻpeṟṟa kallūri maruttuvapāṉiyāka vaḷarntuḷḷatu. tamiḻakattil ceṉṉiyil nāṉku maruttuvak kallūri kaḷum, tañci, kōvi, ceṅkaḻunīrppaṭṭu, maturi, cēlam, pāḷi maruttuvak kallūrikaḷum ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ceṉṉiyil nāṭṭu mūliki kaḷip payaṉpaṭuttum 'aṇṇā maruttuvamaṉi' amintakariyi yaṭutta aṇṇānakaril ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi paḻaṉi ākiyaviṭaṅkaḷil tamiḻ - aṟiva (citta maruttuvak kallūrikaḷ ceyalpaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. moḻi vaḷarcci 
kipi. 19, 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ vaḷarcciyiṉi āyumpōtu ataṉ palvēṟu vaṇṇaṅkaḷik kāṇkiṟōm. ciṟappāka viṭutali iyakkattiṟku atu ciṟanta vēlākiyatu. āṅkilak kalvi muṟiyum, accup poṟiyum tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarccikkup peritum tuṇipurintaṉa. ōliccuvaṭikaḷil eḻutivanta muṟi māṟi, tāḷil (kākitattil) eḻutum mēṟkōḷ kuṟikaḷ, aṭippuk kuṟikaḷ, patti pirittu eḻututal, talippukaḷ, tuṇit talippukaḷ koṭuttu eḻututal mutaliya putiya muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟi acciṭappaṭṭa tamiḻ nūlkaḷ perukiṉa. mēlināṭṭu muṟikaḷit taḻuvi varipaṭaṅkaḷ, vaṇṇappaṭaṅkaḷ, oḷippaṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉavum vāḻkki varalāṟukaḷ, 
ōlicāmiyum tāmatitta vēḷi 
kipi.19, 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tamiḻakam 
140 
uyirōṭṭamāṉa urinaṭikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum tamiḻil tōṉṟiṉa. puṉikatikaḷ, putiṉaṅkaḷ, nāṭakaṅkaḷ putumuṟiyil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, itikācaṅkaḷ, purāṇaṅkaḷ, mēlināṭṭuk katikaḷ mutaliyavi makkaḷ pēcum naṭiyil nāṭṭu moḻikaḷiluḷḷa nūlkaḷ tamiḻākkam ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. talapurāṇaṅkaḷum, catakaṅkaḷum, vāḻviyal tattuva nūlkaḷum tamiḻil tōṉṟiṉa. 
moḻiyārāycci, oppilakkaṇam, akaramutali mutaliyaṉa vaḷarntaṉa. kaṇitam, nimittam cōtiṭam, varalāṟṟārāycci, tami ḻilakkiya ārāycci, aṟiviyal nūlkaḷ mutaliyaṉavum tamiḻil vaḷarntaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkellām mēlākac ceytit tāḷkaḷ moḻi vaḷarccikkum, makkaḷiṉ aṟivu vaḷarccikkum peritum utaviṉa. - camaṟkirutattiyum, tamiḻiyum cērttu maṇippiravāḷa naṭiyil pēciyum, eḻutiyum vanta pulavarkaḷ taṉittamiḻ naṭiyi vaḷarttaṉar. itu oru puṉita iyakkamākavē ceyalpaṭṭatu. aracē muṉvantu tamiḻ akaramutali, tamiḻ ārāycci mutaliyavaṟṟi kaṇakkuvittatu. viṭutalip pōril tamiḻmoḻi viṭutaliyum iṭampeṟṟatu. itaṉāl, moḻivaḻi mānilaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ittaṉiyum inta 19, 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil naṭantaṉa. viṭutalikkup piṉ tamiḻ ariyaṇi ēṟi āṭcipuriyat toṭaṅkiyatu. 
- kipi18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya pulavarkaḷil perumpālōr matattavarkaḷāyiruntaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷ moḻi vaḷarccikkup peritum utaviṉa, pērūrc cāntaliṅka aṭikaḷār, maṟiñāṉatēcikar, - cuṇṇaṉvaḷḷal, civañāṉa vaḷḷal, maturakavirāyar, cuppiratīpak kavirāyar, aṭṭāvatāṉam cīraṅkam pulavar nallāppiḷḷi , namaccivāya mutaliyār, oppilāmaṇip pulavar mutaliyōr pala ariya ilakkiyaṅ cuḷiyum, pāviyaṅkaḷiyum, ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷiyum camittaṉar. aruṇācalak kavirāyar irāmanāṭakam eḻutiṉār. upēntira āciriyar, kumāracāmi tēcakir mutaliyōrnimitta nālkaḷi eḻutiṉar.cāntaliṅkak kavirāyar, ampalavāṇak kavirāyar, nārāyaṇa pārati, nārāyaṇatācar, kōpālakiruṭṭiṇatācar mutaliyōr catakaṅkaḷ ceytaṉar. antāti, piḷḷittamiḻ, kōvi mutaliyaṉavum eḻuntaṉa.cokkappa nāvalariṉ tañcivāṇaṉ kōviyum, mārimuttuppiḷḷiyiṉ cittirak kaviti kaḷum, muttutācariṉ kayilimali varalāṟum kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa. uri naṭiyum, nāṭakaṅkaḷum, ciṟukāppiyaṅkaḷum innūṟṟāṇṭil ciṟantu 
vaḷarnta ṉa, 
19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil talapurāṇaṅkaḷ, vaḻuttup pāṭalkaḷ, nimitta nūlkaḷ, kiṟittava camayanālkaḷ, akaramutalikaḷ, oppilakkaṇaṅ cuḷ ciṟappāka vaḷarntaṉa. irāmāṉucak kavirāyar ciṟanta urinaṭikkum, caravaṇap perumāḷ kavirāyar cerukku pāp pāṭuvatilum vallavarkaḷ.

tāynila varalāṟu 
vīrarākava mutaliyār antakakkavi', 'muttamiḻ vittakar' eṉap pukaḻap paṭṭār, yāḻppāṇam cēṉātirāyar tamiḻakaramutali tokuttār. āṟumuka nāvalariṉ tanti kantappiṉṉaḷa oru ciṟanta nāṭaka āciriyar. ivari yaṭuttu irāmaccantirak kavirāyar, paracurāmak kavirāyar mutaliyōr nāṭakaṅkaḷ iyaṟṟiṉar. āṟumukanāvalar ilakkaṇa nūl eḻutuvatilum, civa nūlkaḷip paṭippatilum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉār. tāṇṭavarāya mutaliyār marāttiya moḻiyiliruntu 'pañcatantirak katikaḷi moḻipeyarttār, 
in nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiyavarkaḷil irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷār taliyāya ciṟappuṭiyavar. aruḷneṟi perukki, tēṉ tamiḻi vaḷarttavar ivarē. culneñcaṅkaḷiyum urukkum pāppāṭṭa vallavar, potunili naṉvaḻi (camaraca caṉmārkkam) kaṇṭavar. ivaruṭiya tiruvaruṭpākkaḷik kēṭṭu urukātār illi. makāvittuvāṉ mīṉāṭcicuntarampiḷḷi nāṟkavi vallavar. ivaruṭiya māṇavartāṉ par.uvēcāminātiyar. ipperumakkaḷiṉ paṇiyāl tamiḻ vāṉaḷāva vaḷarntatu. uvē.cāminātiyariṉ perumuyaṟciyāl cellerittup pōkavirunta cuḻacu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ accēṟiṉa. avaṟṟi accēṟṟi nammuṭiya celvamākkiyap perumi avariyē cārum. mīṉāṭci cuntarampiḷḷi patiṉāṟu tala purāṇaṅkaḷiyum, patiṉāṟu antāti kaḷiyum, piṟa nūlkaḷiyum paḻamivaḻiyil yāttār. 
19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḷiyōrum iṟivaḻipāṭṭil īṭupaṭum vaṇṇam 'kīrttaṉikaḷ' vaḷarntaṉa. kōpālakiruṭṭiṇa pāratiyiṉ nantaṉār kīrttaṉi, mutturāvamutaliyāriṉ pārat kīrttaṉimutaliyaṉa kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa. māyūram(muṉcīp vētanāyakam piḷḷi puṉikatikaḷ puṉintu putumiyi uṇṭākkiṉār. ivarkaḷēyaṉṟi par kālṭuvel, ci.yūpōppiyar mutaliya mēlināṭṭavarkaḷum tamiḻ kaṟṟu, pala nūlkaḷi eḻuti, tirukkuṟaḷ pōṉṟa nūlkaḷi āṅkilam, ḵpireñcu 
mutaliya mēli moḻikaḷil ākkiyuḷḷaṉar. 
ki.pi.20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ mēli nāṭṭu muṟiyil varalāṟṟu aṭippaṭiyil vaḷarntatu. accukkūṭaṅkaḷ tamiḻ vaḷarccik kup peritum utaviṉa.teṉṉintiyac civakkoṇṭu muṭivu civa cittāntam nāṟpatippuk kaḻakam tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭatu. kaḻaka nūlkaḷi āyntu ik kaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭatu. tamiḻk kaḻakaṅkaḷ pala tōṉṟip palar tamiḻk kalvi kaṟṟuttēṟa utaviṉa. īḻattilum, kaṭal kaṭanta piṟa nāṭukaḷilum ik kaḻakaṅkaḷāl tamiḻ vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. tamiḻi maṭṭumē cuṟṟōrum aracup paṇikaḷilum, araciyal talivarkaḷiṉ variciyilum iṭam peṟṟaṉar. 
vi.mu.kōpālakiruṭṭiṉar (āccāriyār, pāṇṭitturi tēvar toṭaṅkiya maturit tamiḻc caṅkattār mutaliyōr civa 
atika tākkattār mukām

151 
cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattip pōlavē pala tamiḻ nūlkaḷip patippittum uriyuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭṭum pukaḻaṭintaṉar. mu. irāka viyaṅkār, irā.irākaviyaṅkār mutaliya tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ ilakkiya, varalāṟṟu ārāyccik kaṭṭurikaḷi eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟukaḷ āṅkilattilum veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. ittuṟiyil paṇiyāṟṟiya em. ecu.pūrṇaliṅkam piḷḷi ), vi.kaṉakacapi (piḷḷi ) mutaliyōri maṟuttalākātu. paṇṭitamaṇi mu.katirēcaṉ ceṭṭiyār, kā.cuppiramaṇiyam (piḷḷi ), na.mu.vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭār) mutali yōriṉ tamiḻppaṇiyum cālac ciṟantaviyē, 
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiya pāvalar mikac cilarēyāvar. karuppiyāppāvalar pala ciṟu kāppiyaṅkaḷi iyaṟṟiṉār. "vikṭōriyā pēraraciyār varalāṟu' eṉṟa nūliyum eḻuti iṉṟiya taṉippaṭṭār varalāṟṟip paṭikkum muyaṟciyil ivar veṟṟikaṇṭār. ivarit toṭarntu palariṉ varalāṟukaḷ veḷivantaṉa. itaṉāl, vaḷḷalkaḷ, tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ, pēraṟiñarkaḷ mutaliyōriṉ vāḻkki varalāṟukaḷum tamiḻil vaḷarntaṉa. kāntipurāṇam, tilakar māṉmiyam, vaḷḷalār carittiram ākiya nūlkaḷi eḻutiyavar paṇṭiti acalāmpiki yammiyār āvār. taṉit tamiḻiyakkam 
taṇittamiḻiyakkam uruvāvataṟkum tamiḻiṉa uṇarvu iyakkamāka vaṭiveṭuppataṟkum kāraṇamāṉavar 'tamiḻaṉ' eṉṉum tāḷikiyiṉ āciriyarcu ayōttitācap paṇṭitar āvār. ēṟkeṉavē taṉittu tamiḻ iyaṅkavallatu eṉpatik kaṇṭuṇarntavar kālṭuvelār.taṉ peyarip paritimāṟkaliñar eṉat taṉittamiḻil māṟṟikkoṇṭa cūriya nārāyaṇ cāttiriyār 'taṉittamiḻ iyakkam' toṭaṅki vittār. maṟimali yaṭikaḷ taṉittamiḻ iyakkatti makkaḷ cārnta pēriyakkamāka vaḷarttār. iṉṟu ulaka moḻiyārāycciyāḷarkaḷil talimi cāṉṟavarum, taṉittamiḻiyē taṉ uyirppākavum, uṭalākavum koṇṭa varumāṉa moḻiñāyiṟu ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇar ivviyakkattiṉ iṉṟiya mukaṭākat tikaḻkiṟār. avariṉ tamiḻ varalāṟu, vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu, mutaṟṟāymoḻi, tiraviṭattāy, tamiḻar varalāṟu, tamiḻar matam, paṇṭit tamiḻanākarikamum paṇpāṭum mutalāṉa taraya tamiḻ nūlkaḷum Primary clas sical Language of the World mutalāṉa āṅkila nūlkaḷum pāvāṇarit talivarākak koṇṭu iyaṅkum makkaḷ iyakkamāṉa tamiḻartam paṇpāṭṭut toṉmiyi mēliyarum viyakkumvaṇṇam ulakukku uṇartti niṟkiṉṟaṉa. pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē nāṭakam naṉku vaḷarcci peṟṟu viṭṭatu eṉalām, āṉāl, avi marapu vaḻip paṇpukaḷit taḻuviyē vaḷarntaṉa, nāṭakaṅkaḷ naṭattuvataṟkeṉat taṉik kaḻakaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ araciyal, kumukāyac cīrtirutta nāṭakaṅkaḷum, varalāṟṟu nāṭakaṅkaḷum vaḷarntaṉa. pammal campanta mutaliyāriṉ nāṭakappaṇi pōṟṟaṟkuriyatu. nāṭakattip pōlavē nāṭṭiyamum, iciyum tamiḻil vaḷarcci yaṭintaṉa. kālappōkkil aracu ātaravuṭaṉ ivi vaḷarntu, iṉṟu muṟippaṭi ivaṟṟik kaṟṟukkoḷvataṟkuk kallūrikaḷum ēṟpaṭuttap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. toṭakkattil ivaṟṟiṉ vaḷarccikku utaviya puravalar aṇṇāmali ceṭṭiyāriyum, aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakattiyum uccimēl vittup pōṟṟa vēṇṭum. nāṭakattiṉ mutirnta vaḷarcciyil iṉṟu pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkum tirippaṭaṅkaḷum, vāṉoli * yum tamiḻ vaḷarccikku oruvakiyil utavukiṉṟaṉa, 
tamiḻ vaḷarccikkum, potumakkaḷiṉ moḻi, araciyal, aṟiviyal mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vaḷarccikkum uṟutuṇiyāy niṟpaṉa tamiḻttāḷikika ḷākum. viṭutalip pōrāṭṭak kālattil cutēcamittiraṉ, tiṉamaṇi, āṉantapōtiṉi, āṉantavikaṭaṉ, kalki mutaliya ēṭukaḷ araciyal, moḻi, paṇpāṭṭu neṟikaḷi vaḷarttaṉa. pala ariya varalāṟṟuk katikaḷik kalkiyum, āṉantavikaṭaṉum veḷiyiṭṭaṉa. āṉāl, ivi yāvum ak kālattil vaḻaṅkappeṟṟa maṇippiravāḷa naṭiyik kaṭippiṭittaṉa. taṉittamiḻ vaḷarccikkum tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarccikkum pāṭupaṭap pala ēṭukaḷ toṭaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. curantit tamiḻc caṅkattār veḷiyiṭṭa 'tamiḻppoḻil', civa cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭa 'centamiḻc celvi', maṟimaliyaṭikaḷāriṉ aṟivukkaṭal' (ñāṉa cākaram mutaliya ēṭukaḷ ittakiyap paṇikaḷi mēṟkoṇṭaṉa.

'lōkōpakāri', irā.cokkaliṅkattiṉ ' 
caḻiyaṉ', pacīvāṉantattiṉ 'caṉacakti', nāraṇaturikkaṇṇaṉiṉ 'piracaṇṭa vikaṭaṉ', ē.kē.ceṭṭi yāriṉ 'kumariḻalar', na.cōmayāculuviṉ iḷantamiḻaṉ', cīṉivācaṉiṉ 'maṇikkoṭi", perṇāṇṭōviṉ 'tamiḻmaṇi' mutaliya eṇṇiṟanta ēṭukaḷ ivvakiyil paṇipurintaṉa. aṟiviyal ēṭukaḷ tōṉṟit tamiḻ vaḷarccikku utaviṉa. 
mēṭip pēccilum, pakuttaṟivu vaḷarcciyilum tamiḻil oru puttiyalip paṭittavarkaḷ tanti periyārum pēraṟiñar aṇṇāvum āvar. periyār eḷiya tamiḻil pēcik kōpurattiṉ ucciyilirunta araciyal karuttukaḷik kuppi mēṭṭukkuk koṇṭuvantār. aṇṇā aṭukku moḻiyāl āyiramāyiram makkaḷik kavarntār. pēccuk kaliyē oruputu merukuṭaṉ tikaḻvataṟku aṇṇāvum avarip piṉpaṟṟiyōrum vaḻivakuttaṉar. āṅkilattil pēcuvatutāṉ ciṟappāṉa teṉṟa nilimāṟi tamiḻil pēcuvatutāṉ paṇpuṭiyatu eṉṟa kaṭṭāyam iṉṟu ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatu. tamiḻ ariyaṇi ēṟiviṭṭatu. 
kaṭiciyāka āciriyar kaḻakaṅkaḷum, maṉṟaṅkaḷum, toḻilāḷar iyakkaṅkaḷum, accukkūṭaṅkaḷum tamiḻ vaḷarccikkuc ceyta paṇikaḷi maṟukkamuṭiyātu. ci.ē.naṭēcaṉ accukkaḻakam, tamiḻ vaḷarppup paṇṇi , cātu accukkūṭam mutaliya pala accukkūṭaṅkaḷum, patippakaṅkaḷum tamiḻ vaḷarccikku utaviṉa. pāratiyārum, pāratitācaṉum āṟṟiya tamiḻppaṇi viyakkattakkavi. ivarkaḷ pāṭal (kaviti), ilakkiyam, tāḷiki mutaliya pala tuṟikaḷilum paṇiyāṟṟit tamiḻ moḻikkut tāḷāṇmi tantaṉar. 
īḻam, malināṭu (malēyā mutaliya ayalnāṭukaḷilum tamiḻc caṅkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟit tamiḻp paṇiyāṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. kolkattā (kalkattā) mumpāy (pampāy), tilli, peṅkaḷur mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa tamiḻc caṅkaṅkaḷiṉ tamiḻp paṇiyālum, ulakat tamiḻk kaḻakattālum, ulakat tamiḻārāyccik kaḻakattālum iviyāvaṟṟiṟkum mēlāka iṉṟiya āṭciyāḷarālum tamiḻ poṟkālattip paṭittuviṭṭatu. intiyetirppu tamiḻukku uramiṭṭatu .

tāṉ mēṟkūṟiya 'taṉittamiḻ iyakkam vaḷarntatu. camaṟkirutattit tēvamoḻiyāka matittu vaḻipaṭṭa maṉappāṇmi māṟiyatu. tamiḻariṉ perumiyip palarum pōṟṟip parappiṉar. nāṭṭiṉappāvalar cuppira maṇiyapārati, pāratitācaṉ mutaliya taliyāya pāvalarkaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭu araciyalāḷarkaḷum, tamiḻ vaḷarap pāṭupaṭṭaṉar. pala tāḷikikaḷum, naralkaḷum tamiḻil vantaṉa. tamiḻicic caṅkam teruvellām tamiḻ muḻaṅka vaḻivakuttatu. in niliyil tamiḻariṉ araciyal viḻippuṇarccik kākat tōṉṟiya nayaṉmik kaṭci irupatāṇṭukaḷip patavi vēṭṭi yilēyēvīṇaṭittatu. itaṉ karaṇiyamākat tamakkuḷḷēyē piḷavupaṭṭuc citaṟiyatu. 1937-l naṭanta tērtalil pērāyak kaṭci veṉṟatu. 'ēka intiyā', 'inti' ākiyavi tamiḻarmēl niṉṟaṉa. mīṇṭum camaṟkirutam āḷumipeṟṟatu. inti etirppu tamiḻakameṅkum tōṉṟiyatu. paḷḷi kaḷil tiṇikkappaṭṭa intiyiyetirttu mānāṭukaḷ kūṭiṉa.tāḷamuttu, naṭarācaṉ pōṉṟa iḷiñarkaḷ taṅkaḷ iṉṉuyiri īkam tantaṉar. 
pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷ naṭantaṉa. kaṭṭāya intit tiṇippu nīkkappaṭṭatu. 
pērāyak kaṭci tēciya uṇarcci', 'viṭutalip pōr pōṉṟa vaṟṟāl untappaṭṭu perum vaḷarcci peṟṟapōtu, kāntiyār ataṉ oḷiviḷakkāyt tikaḻntār orē intiyar' eṉṉum kōṭpāṭum tēciya moḻik kōṭpāṭum paravalākap pōṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. āyiṉum, tamiḻar viḻippōṭiruntu tamiḻnāṭu tamiḻarukkē' eṉṟu muḻaṅkiṉar. mapociva ñāṉam pērāyak kaṭciyiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭut tamiḻaracuk kaḻakatti ēṟpaṭuttiṉār. tamiḻē tamiḻakattil āṭci moḻiyāka vēṇṭum. āṅkilamō, intiyō iṭam peṟak kūṭātu eṉṟār. moḻi ārāycci kaḷum, mānāṭukaḷum naṭantaṉa. tamiḻar oṉṟupaṭalāyiṉar. moḻivaḻi mānilaṅkaḷ pirintaṉa. viṭutalikkup piṉṉum, tamiḻariṉ viḻippukkup piṉṉum naṭuvaṇ āṭci oru moḻikkōṭpāṭṭik kiviṭavilli, itaṟkāka āṭcimoḻic caṭṭam vantatu. 1965 ām āṇṭu caṉavari - pippiravarit tiṅkaḷkaḷil āṭci moḻic caṭṭattiyetirttut tamiḻaka meṅkilum māṇavarkaḷ pōrkkoṭi uyarttiṉar, potumakkaḷum kalantu koṇṭaṉar. tamiḻaka aracāl im moḻip pōrāṭṭatti aṭakka muṭiyavilli. naṭuvaṇ āṭciyiṉ paṭikoṇṭutāṉ im moḻip pōrāṭṭam aṭakkappaṭṭatu. palar māṇṭaṉar. palar taṭiyaṭipaṭṭaṉar. palar tīkkuḷittaṉar. ittakiya moḻip pōrāṭṭam ituvari ulakil enta nāṭṭilum inta aḷavu muṭukkamāka naṭakka villi. ip pōrāṭṭam tamiḻakatti moḻiyāl oṉṟiṇiyac ceytatu. tamiḻmoḻi kāppōrukkē oppōliyaḷikkappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, tamiḻaka araciṉ varalāṟē māṟiyatu. pērāyak kaṭcipaṭutōlvi kaṇṭatu. tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam āḷum kaṭciyākit tamiḻi ariyaṇiyēṟṟiyatu. inti aṭiyōṭu nīkkappaṭṭatu. eṅkum etilum tamiḻ malarntu maṇam parappattoṭaṅkiyatu.

(e) viṭutalip pōril tamiḻakam tamiḻarkaḷ iyaṟkiyākavē vīrap paṇpum, viṭutaliyuṇarvum koṇṭavarkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ yāvum vīrap peṭṭakaṅkaḷē. avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟup pōkkil aṭimi vāḻvit toṭarntu kāṇa muṭiyātu. avarkaḷ poṟāmikku aṭimiyāṉa pōtellām taṅkaḷukkuḷ kāḻppuṇarcci poṅkiya pōtellām māṟṟāṉukku iṭam koṭuttut taṅkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟil pulluruvi pukumpaṭi ceytu viṭṭaṉar. attakiya pulluruvikaḷtām āriyarum, iculāmiya rum, marāttiyarum, piṉṉar vanta airōppiyarum āvar. tamiḻarkaḷ oṉṟutiraṇṭu muṉippāka etirttiruntāl muculīmkaḷiṉ 
āṭci maturiyilēyē tōṉṟiyirātu. marāttiyar tamiḻ maṇṇil kālaṭi vittirukka māṭṭārkaḷ; airōppiya vaṇikarum tamiḻakattil vaṇikarākavē iruntu akaṉṟiruppar. āṉāl, oḷaṉṟi nōkkumpōtu 
āriyap puyalum, muculīm cūṟāvaḷiyum, airōppiyap pēykkāṟṟum intiyat tuṇik kaṇṭattiyē acittuviṭṭapōtu tamiḻakam maṭṭum taṉiyē tappiyirukka muṭiyātu eṉpatum varalāṟṟu uṇmi yākiṟatu. eppaṭiyō māṟṟāṉukku aṭimiyākiviṭṭa nāṭṭil tamiḻar taṉṉuṇarvu peṟum pōtellām māṟṟāṉi maṇṇiviṭṭu akaṟṟac ceyta viṭutalip pōrkaḷit toṭarntu kāṇkiṟōm. kuṟippāka 19, 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil ittakiya pōrkaḷ tamiḻ maṇṇil muṭukkamāy, muṉippuṭaṉ naṭanta varalāṟṟip pūlittēvaṉ, vīrapāṇṭiyak kaṭṭapommaṉ, ūmitturi, marutupāṇṭiyarkaḷ ākiyōri varalāṟṟil kaṇṭōm. itē kālattil micūr vīraṉ tippucultāṉum, āṅkilari aṭiyōṭu viraṭṭap pōrkkoṭi uyarttip poṅki eḻuntatiyum pārttōm. ivarkaḷ yāvarum maṉṉarkaḷāy nāṭṭiyāṇṭavarkaḷ. eṉavē, māṟṟāṉic cāyti toḻikka ivarkaḷ mēṟkoṇṭa pōrkaḷi "viṭutalip pōrkaḷ' eṉṟu oppukkoḷḷac cilar maṟukkiṉṟaṉar. 'pōr' maṉṉariṉ vāḻvōṭu kalanta paṇpu. ākavē, atu nāṭṭu viṭutalip pōrākātu eṉpatu ivartam karuttākum. 
aṭuttup paṭivīrarkaḷ āṅkilari etirttu eḻunta nikaḻcciyum tamiḻaka varalāṟṟil uṇṭu. atutāṉ ki.pi1806-l vēlūril naṭanta paṭivīrarkaḷiṉ culkamākum. itip paṟṟiyum iruvēṟu karuttukaḷ nilavukiṉṟaṉa. āṅkilap paṭivīrarkaḷ peṟṟirunta taṉic calukikaḷi yum, uyarviyum kaṇṭu maṉam poṟāt tāḻniliyil paṇiyāṟṟiya intiya vīrarkaḷ kāḻppuṇarcciyāl ceyta kalakamē vēlūrk kalakam eṉpatu oru cārār karuttu. āṉāl, āṅkilariṭam paṇiyāṟṟum vīrarum viṭutali vēṭkiyuṭaṉ eḻuntaṉar eṉpatu maṟucārār karuttu. in nikaḻcciyik kāṇpōm.

vēlūrk kalakam (ki.pi.1806) 
vayavar. ciyārcu (car. jārj pārlō ki.pi1805-1807 āṅkila vāṭciyiṉ talimiyāḷunarāka iruntapōtu ceṉṉi yāḷunarāka iruntavar kiḻāṉ villiyam peṇṭiṅku āvāṉ. ivaṉ ceṉṉi yāḷunarākak ki.pi.1801 mutal 1807 variyilum iruntāṉ. ivaṉē piṟkālattil talimi yāḷunarāka kipi1828 mutal 1835vari iruntāṉ.) āṅkilat taḷapati vayavar cāṇkirētāk eṉpavaṉ ceṉṉi yāḷunariṭam opputal peṟṟukkoṇṭu oru cila paṭic cīrtiruttaṅkaḷik koṇṭu vantāṉ. ataṉpaṭi intiyap paṭiñarkaḷum cippāykaḷum paṭi irāṇuvac cīruṭiyilirukkum pōtu 'tilakam', 'nāmam, tirunīṟu' mutaliya camayac ciṉṉaṅkaḷi neṟṟiyil iṭak kūṭāteṉṟum, orē vakiyākat talimayiri veṭṭik koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉṟum, mīciyi miṭukkāka muṟukkiviṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟum kātil kaṭukkaṇ pōṭakkūṭā teṉṟum āṇiyiṭappaṭṭatu. orē vakiyāṉa paṭic cīruṭi kaḷiyum, toppiyiyum aṇiyavum āṇiyiṭappaṭṭaṉar. ittakiya āṇikaḷi intiyap paṭivīrarkaḷ aiyattuṭaṉ nōkkiṉar. āṅkilar taṅkaḷik kiṟittavarākkavē iccūḻcci ceykiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu nampiṉar. taṅkaḷ camayattiṟkum paṇpukaḷukkum ētam vantuviṭṭatākak kataṟiṉar. 
1806 ām āṇṭil vēlūrkkōṭṭiyilirunta paṭiyil 370 āṅkila vīrarkaḷum, 1500 intiya vīrarkaḷum iruntaṉar. intiyap paṭivīrarkaḷ ittakiyap paṭiāṇikaḷit takarkkavum, āṅkilari oḻikkavum taṅkaḷukkuḷ kamukkamāka oru catittiṭṭattit tīṭṭiṉar. cūlit tiṅkaḷ pattām nāḷ kāliyil ēḻumaṇikku tiṭīreṉap puraṭci ceytaṉar. āṅkila vīrarkaḷum atikārikaḷum taṅkiyirunta pācaṟi (mukāmkaḷi nōkkic caramāriyākak kuṇṭumaḻi peytaṉar. veḷḷi yar palar māṇṭaṉar. palar tappiyōṭiṉar. intiyap paṭivīrarkaḷ veḷḷiyari viraṭṭiviṭṭōm eṉṟu veṟṟimuracu koṭṭiṉar. kōṭṭiyil paṟanta āṅkilak koṭiyi iṟakkiviṭṭut taṅkaḷ koṭiyip paṟakkaviṭṭaṉar. vēlūrkkōṭṭiyil ciṟi vikkappaṭ ṭirunta tippucultāṉiṉ iraṇṭu piḷḷikaḷil oruvaṉi maṉṉaṉ eṉap 'pirakaṭaṉam' ceytaṉar. makiḻcciyāravārattil āṭikkaḷittuk 
kūttāṭiṉar. 
in niliyil tappiyōṭiya veḷḷiyaril oruvaṉāṉa taḷapati kūṭcu (mējar kūṭs) eṉpavaṉ orē mūccāka irāṇippēṭṭi variyil ōṭi aṅkirunta paṭiyi aḻittukkoṇṭu vēlūr tirumpiṉāṉ. (karṉal) vellaculi eṉpavaṉ talimiyil vanta ip paṭi muṉṉūṟu intiyap paṭikaḷic cuṭṭukkoṉṟu kalakatti aṭakkiyatu. ik kalkattil māṇṭa āṅkila vīrarkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki nūṟu ākum. eñciya intiyap paṭivīrarkaḷ kaṭuntaṇṭaṉikaḷip peṟṟaṉar. ikkalkattāl viḷinta iḻappukaḷiyum, itaṉ karariyatti

yum ucāviya āṅkila mēliṭam kirētākkiyum, āḷunariyum pataviyi liruntu nīkkiyatu. tippuviṉ makkaḷ kalkattāl ciṟikku aṉuppappaṭṭa ṉar. putitākak koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa paṭic cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ nīkkappaṭṭaṉa. 
vēlūril naṭanta intap paṭiñar cippāyk kalakam cipi.1857-l naṭanta māperum mutal intiyap pōrukku vaḻikāṭṭiyākum. cilar itaṉic 'cātāraṇak kalakameṉṟum, itaṟkum 1857 ām āṇṭup puraṭcikkum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu eṉṟum kūṟuvar. 1808 ām āṇṭil mīṇṭum veḷḷiyari veḷiyēṟṟa oru tiṭīrppuraṭci vēlūril tōṉṟiyatu. atuvum veṟṟi peṟavilli. atil paṅkēṟṟa intiyap paṭiñarkaḷi āṅkilar, potumakkaḷ muṉṉiliyil tūkkiliṭṭaṉar. tuvarkaḷ toṅkiya tākkumaramkōṭṭikkuḷ iṉṟum kāvaltuṟip payiṟci niliyattiṟkuḷ uḷḷatu. vēluār makkaḷ aṉṟum, ataṟkuppiṉ naṭanta pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷilum, pōrkaḷilum paṅkēṟṟa vīrarkaḷ. ākavē, vēlūrk kalakamē intiyāvil veḷḷiyari etirttu naṭanta mutal viṭutalip puraṭciyākum, 
vēlūrk kalakattil ak kālattil irumput tirikaḷāyirunta cāti, camayat tirikaḷik kiḻittukkoṇṭu intu, muculīmkaḷ mēl cāti, kīḻccātiyiṉar yāvarum paṭiyiṉar īṭupaṭṭaṉar. eṉavētāṉ ki.pi.1857-l vaṭanāṭeṅkilum paraviya intiya viṭutalip pōrukku cippāyk kalakamiḵtōr ottiki' eṉṟaṉar, 
itu oru cippāyk kalakamā? illavē illi. vañcam tīrkkavē mūṇṭa pōr; āṅkilariṉ aṭimittaḷiyi uṭitteṟiyum tēciyap pōr; nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ nallāṭcikku vittiṭṭa pōr; karupparāṉa aṭimikaḷ taṅkaḷi aṭimippaṭuttiyāṇṭāṅkilarikkoṉṟu kuvitta vintippōrēyaṉṟi vēṟilli ' eṉṟu iracal (Russel) eṉṉum āṅkilar vēlūrk kalakattip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. 
āyiṉum, vēlūrk kalakam tōṟṟataṟku mucutāpā pēk eṉpavariṉ iraṇṭakac ceyalē itaṟkuk karaṇiyamākum. ivaṉ tippucultāṉiṉ makkaḷiṭamiruntu paṇattiyum peṟṟu, culakattiyum tūṇṭiviṭṭu, āṅkilāriṭamum kalakattiṉ kamukkattik kūṟiviṭṭāṉ. āṅkilar ivaṉuṭiya kāṭṭik koṭutta parikkaḷitta kikkūli iraṇṭāyiram varākaṉ paṇamākum. ākavē, vēlūr, viṭutalippōr mukatāpāpēk kiṉ caṇṭāḷattaṉattiṟkum paliyāki viṭṭatu. "kalakam" eṉum kaḷaṅka mākiviṭṭatu. 
mēṟkūṟiyavi maṉṉarkaḷālum, paṭivīrarkaḷālum mēṟ : koḷḷappaṭṭa viṭutalippōrkaḷeṉṟataṉāl cilar potu makkaḷ paṅkēṟkāta pōrkaḷ viṭutalip pōrkaḷ ākāveṉpar. paṭivīrar kaḷum, maṉṉarkaḷum, makkaḷtām, tamiḻ maṟavartām eṉpatiyum, maṉṉarkaḷ āṇṭumāṉattil potu makkaḷ, atuvum tamiḻp paṇpil

urupā avar āṉāṉ. avaṉ aḷikkum pātukāp 
nilikkum cātaṉāṉa palvēṟu āṇi 
tōynta potu makkaḷ kiḷartteḻa vāyppilli, avarkaḷiṉ uṇarvu kaḷittāṉ pūlittēvaṉum, kaṭṭapommaṉum, marutu pāṇṭiyarum, vēlūr vīrarkaḷum veḷippaṭuttiṉar. veḷḷiyar kalakam (1809) 
vēlūrk kalakattiṉ karaṇiyamākac ceṉṉiyāḷunar villiyam peṇṭiṅ pataviyiḻantu iṅkilāntu ceṉṟuviṭṭāṉ. avaṉukkuppiṉ vayavar ciyārcu pārlō (1807-13) ceṉṉiyāḷunar āṉāṉ. ceṉṉip paṭiyilirunta veḷḷiyarkaḷ 'kūṭārappaṭi ' kēṭṭuk kalakam ceytaṉar. veḷḷiyari veṟutta intiyap paṭiyiṉar ivarkaḷuṭaṉ cērntu kalakam ceyyāmal vāḷāviruntuviṭṭaṉar. itilum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ uṇarvi aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. vēluttampikalakam (1809-1810) 
1795-l āṅkilar tiruvitāṅkūril taṅkaḷ paṭiyoṉṟi niṟutti ataṟkākum paṭiccelavi aḷikkumpaṭi uṭaṉpāṭu ceytukoṇṭa ṉar. ataṉpaṭi tiruvitāṅkūr aracar āṇṭoṉṟukku eṭṭilakkam urupā aḷittu vantār. 1809 -l vēluttampi eṉpāṉ tiruvitāṅkūr tivāṉ amiccar āṉāṉ. avaṉ āṅkilariyum, ayalār āḷumiyi yum veṟukkum taṉmāṉa vīraṉ. paṭikkaḷikkum pātukāppup paṇatti niṟuttiṉāṉ. āṅkilari yetirttup pōrkkoṭi uyarttiṉāṉ. 1810-l ālappuḻiyil muppatu āṅkilap paṭiyiṉariyum, oru paṭi atikāriyiyum koli ceytāṉ. itaṉiyaṟinta ceṉṉiyāḷunar vayavarciyārcu pārlōperumpaṭiyaṇuppittiruvitāṅkūrit tākkip piṭittukkoṇṭāṉ. taṉ māṉavīraṉ vēluttampi pakivariṉ kiyil cikkāmal taṟkoli ceytukoṇṭāṉ. 
intiyāvil viṭutali iyakkam tōṉṟuvataṟkāṉa karaṇiyāṅkaḷ palavāyiṉum ayalavarāṉa āṅkilariṉ aṭimittaḷiyi aṟukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa viṭutali vēṭkiyēmutukantaṇṭākum. ivviṭutali vēṭki āṅkilavāṭci ēṟpaṭṭu irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷ vari ili maṟi kāy pōliruntu 19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷippaṭṭu, irupatām nāṟṟāṇṭil palaṉ tantatu. iṉattāl, moḻiyāl, camayattāl, cātiyāl vēṟupaṭṭup pirintuk kiṭanta intiyar oṉṟupaṭṭu āṅkilari akaṟṟa muṭiyavilli. kālappōkkil ivarkaḷiṭiyē eḻunta inta viṭutali vēṭki ittakiya vēṟupāṭukaḷi uḷḷaḻutti mēleḻuntu veṟṟi peṟṟatu. avvāṟu eḻuntu pala cōtaṉikaḷit tāṇṭi veṟṟipeṟṟa vīravaralāṟṟil tamiḻakam paṭitta iyali īṇṭuc curuṅkak kāṇalām. 
vēlūr vīrarkaḷiṉ viṭutalik kiḷarcci aṭakkappaṭṭa piṉṉum tamiḻakattil puraṭcik kaṉal nīṟupūttu niṉṟatu. 1851 ām āṇṭil ceṉṉi nakareṅkilum āṅkilarukku etirāṉa cuvaroṭṭikaḷ kāṇap paṭṭaṉa. makkaḷ oru perum kiḷarccikkut taṅkaḷi aṇiyappaṭuttik 
cipi. 19, 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tamiḻakam 
150 
koḷḷumpaṭiyāṉa āṇikaḷāka avi kāṭciyaḷittaṉa. viṭutalip pōrukkut tamiḻarkaḷi aṇariyappaṭuttiya cuvaroṭṭikaḷukkup piṉṉar naṭimuṟiyil tamiḻakap pāḷiyaṅkaḷil puraṭcik kaṉal veṭittatu. pāḷiyaṅkaḷil eḻunta puraṭcikaḷi navāpu vayavar cālarcaṅ aṭakki ṉāṉ, 
1857-l vaṭanāṭeṅkilum intiyappaṭivīrarkaḷ āṅkilari yetirttup pōriṭṭapōtu tamiḻakam amitiyākavē iruntatu. itaṟku ak kālattil pōkkuvarattu, ceytit toṭarpu mutaliya ēntukaḷ ērāḷamāka illātatē karaṇiyamākum. mēlum, nāṭu muḻuvatilum tiraṇṭu makkaḷ oru mukamākap pōriṭum pāṅkum ak kālattil illi. āyiṉum, ip pōril viṭutali vīrarkaḷi māyttup pukaḻpeṟṟu, piṉṉar 1897 cepṭampar 20 ām nāḷ lakṉōvil māṇṭa paṭiñaṉ (karṉal nīl eṉpavaṉukkuc ceṉṉiyil vitta cili 'avamāṉac ciṉṉam' eṉak karutiya tamiḻarkaḷ kotittu eḻuntaṉar. āṉāl, neṭunāḷkaḷukkup piṉṉarē ci irācakōpālāccāriyār mutalamicca rāyiruntapōtu ac cili akaṟṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṉi aruṅkāṭcic cāliyil paṭikkaḷap pirivil vikkap peṟṟatu. 
1857-kkuppiṉ āṅkilavaṇikakkuḻuvār āṭci oḻintatu. āṅkila varaci intiyāvukkum araci āṉāḷ. itaṟkup piṉṉum pēraraciyāriṉ pēraṟikkikku māṟāka intiyariṉ aṭippaṭiyurimikaḷi āṅkila āṭciyāḷarkaḷ nakakki vantaṉar. intiyā piriṭṭāṉiyariṉ viṟpaṉic cantiyākavē tikaḻntatu. intiyac celvam paṟaṅkiyarāl koḷḷiya ṭikkappaṭṭatāl nāṭṭil pañcaṅkaḷum paṭṭiṉiccāvukaḷum atikarit taṉa. āṉāl, ivaṟṟippaṟṟiyē kavalippaṭāta aracap paṭinikarāḷikaḷ 'āṭampara vāḻkkiyilum, vēṭikki viḻākkaḷilum kaḷippuṟṟu niṉṟaṉar. 1870 ām āṇṭu mutal toṭarntu muppatāṇṭukaḷukku mēl nāṭṭil pala pañcaṅkaḷum, koḷḷi nōykaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. pala ilakkam makkaḷ paṭṭiṉiyāl māṇṭaṉar. pala ilakkam tamiḻarkaḷ tōṭṭakkūlikaḷāy ayalnāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟaṉar. in niliyil aracappaṭinikarāḷi yākavirunta kiḻāṉ iliṭṭaṉ (7876-18801-1-1877-l tilliyil oru kumpali tarparik kūṭṭi vikṭōriyā intiyāviṉ *pēraraci' eṉṟu aṟivittāṉ. itaṟku ērāḷamāṉa paṇattic celaviṭ ṭāṉ. aṭuttu, vaṭamēṟku ellac ceykiyil taliyiṭṭu āpkāṉiyap pōril īṭupaṭṭup peruntokiyic celaviṭṭāṉ. ittakiya ceyalkaḷāl pañcamum, paṭṭiṉiyum atikarittaṉa. 1876 mutal 1878 vari nāṭē pañcattil mūḻkip paṭṭiṉic cāvukaḷ atikarittaṉa. ivvāṇṭu kaḷil pañcattāl iraṇṭu ilakkam makkaḷ māṇṭaṉar. in niliyil tāymoḻit tāḷikikaḷit taṭippaṭuttum caṭṭam oṉṟi (The Ner nacular Pr&s Act) iliṭṭaṉ koṇṭuvantu intiyariṉ ceytittāḷ urimiyiyum paṟittāṉ. avaṉ koṇṭuvanta "paṭikkalac caṭṭam"

icākēṟṟāṟiyum avaṉ cukki intiyā 
[ArIIIs Act) intiyar ettakiya paṭikaḷiyum (āyutam ēntic celvat taṭivitittatu. intiya vaṇikatti muṭamākki intiyari vaṟiyarākkum - iṟakkumatic caṭṭam oṉṟiyum avaṉ koṇṭuvantāṉ. intiyāviṉ viruntu pañcācu ēṟṟumatiyāki, iṅkilāntil āṭiyāka neyyappaṭṭu iṟakkumatiyākum paruttiyāṭikaḷ mītirunta varikaḷi nīkki āṅkila vaṇikarukkup perum ūtiyam tēṭik koṭuttāṉ. ivaṉuṭiya ittakiya ceyalkaḷālum iyaṟkik kēṭukaḷālum intiyariṉ uṇarvukaḷ oṉṟupaṭṭu āṅkilavāṭciyi aṭiyōṭu oḻikka vēṇṭu meṉṟa viṭutali vēṭki mēliṭṭatu. nayaṉmi vaḻaṅkuvatilum āṅkivarukkum intiyarukkumiṭiyē ēṟṟattāḻvukaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. paṟaṅkik kuṟṟavāḷikaḷi intiya nayaṉmiyar ucāvutaṟkut takutiyaṟṟavar reṉṟu karutiṉar. itaṉip pōkka, kiḻāṉ rippaṉ (78800-1884) kālattil koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa "ilparṭ' caṭṭavarivu macōtāvum payaṉaṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. kiḻāṉ rippaṉiṉ kalvic cīrtiruttam, uḷḷāṭciyil intiyar peṟṟa paṅku mutaliyavaṟṟāl intiyar mēlum orumip pāṭṭuṇarvi yum, viṭutaliyuṇarviyum peṟṟaṉar. tāḷikikaḷ (pattiriki mītirunta taṭikaḷ nīkkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl, intiyar taṅkaḷiṉ karuttukaḷi veḷiyiṭum urimi peṟṟaṉar. attōṭu taṅkaḷiṉ pātukāppukkeṉap pala caṅkaṅkaḷiyum ēṟpaṭuttikkoṇṭaṉar. 1876-l intiyakkaḻakam (Ini diain League), intiyac caṅkam (Iridian Association) mutaliyaṉa ēṟpaṭṭa ṉa. 1852-l ceṉṉiyil , ceṉṉivāṇar caṅkam' ēṟpaṭṭatu. tēciya uṇarvit tūṇṭum kūṭṭaṅkaḷ, mānāṭukaḷ ic caṅkaṅkaḷāl naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. palatiṟappaṭṭa moḻivaḻi nāḷēṭukaḷum, kiḻami, tiṅkaḷēṭukaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. pala periya niliyaṅkaḷāl veḷiyiṭap paṭṭa āṅkila ēṭukaḷiṉ vāyilāka nāṭu muḻuvatilum orē mātiriyāṉa tēciya uṇarcci' paraviyatu. 1883l kalkattāvil "paṉṉāṭṭukkaṇkāṭci' oṉṟu toṭaṅkiyatu. atil 'tēciya mānāṭu' oṉṟik kūṭṭi, nāṭu muḻuvatum pala kaḻakaṅkaḷit toṭaṅki tēciya uṇarvi vaḷarkkum vaḻivakikaḷi āyntaṉar. itil intu, iculāmiyat talivarkaḷ palarum vēṟupāṭiṉṟip paṅkēṟṟaṉar. pērāyattiṉ (kāṅkiracu) tōṟṟam (1885) 
intiya tēciyappērāyam mutaṉ mutalil tamiḻakattiṉ talinakarāṉa ceṉṉi nakariltāṉ tōṉṟiyatu. 1884-l tivāṉpakatūr irakunātarāv eṉpavar vīṭṭil patiṉēḻu pērkūṭi 'pērāyam" ēṟpaṭuttuvatu paṟṟik pēciṉar. ivarkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavar aṉantāccārlu, araṅkayyā fnāyuṭu, ci.cuppiramaṇiya ayyar, irakunātarāṉ ākiya tamiḻnāṭṭavarkaḷ ḷāvar. ivarkaḷiṉ muṭivuppaṭi 1885-l pūṉāvil mutal pērāya mānāṭu kūṭuvatā yiruntatu. āṉāl, pūṉāvil 'piḷēk nōy ēṟpaṭṭatāl pampāyil mutal pērāyak kaṭci mānāṭu kūṭiyatu. intap pērāyak kaṭci tōṉṟuvataṟku muṉpē ceṉṉiyil *makācaṉa cavi' eṉṟa tēciya

niṟuvaṉattit tōṟṟuvittavarkaḷum itil paṅkēṟṟa mēṟkaṇṭa tamiḻarkaḷēyāvar. itil intu muculīm uṟuppiṉar palarumiruntaṉar. * 07ām āṇṭu pālakaṅkātara tilakar ceṉṉi vanta pōtu ic cavi pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ kōṭpāṭukaḷi appaṭiyē ēṟṟuc ceyal paṭṭatu. piṉṉar ic cavi pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ oru kiḷiyākavum, pala tiṟappaṭṭa intiyarip paṇpāṭṭiṉ aṭippaṭiyil oṉṟupaṭuttum niliyamākavum māṟiyatu. ilaṇṭaṉ mānakarilum intiyar caṅkam' oṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu. 
tamiḻakattil nāṭṭuppaṟṟum, viṭutali vēṭkiyum ēṟpaṭa mēṟkaṇṭa caṅkaṅkaḷēyaṉṟit tamiḻmoḻi oru ciṟanta karuviyāka amintatu. nām kaṇṭa tamiḻ maṟavarkaḷāṉa pūlittēvaṉ kaṭṭa pommaṉ mutaliyōriṉ vāḻkki varalāṟukaḷ vīrappāviyaṅkaḷākavum, nāṭakaṅkaḷākavum parappappeṟṟu makkaḷit taṭṭiyeḻuppiṉa. 'kaṭṭa pommaṉ caṇṭikkum nāṭṭuppuṟa makkaḷiyum taṭṭiyeḻuppiyatu. ittakiyavīrappāviyaṅkaḷēyaṉṟi * āṉmikattuṟiyilum tamiḻarkaḷi oṉṟupaṭac ceyyum pala nūlkaḷum tamiḻil tōṉṟiṉa. 'nāmārkkum kuṭiyallōm, namaṉi añcōm' eṉpatē tamiḻar camayam. itaṉi viṭutali ūḻiyilum kāṇkiṟōm. vaṭakkē irācārām mōkaṉrāy, tayāṉanta caracuvati, irāmakiruṭṭiṇar tōṉṟi āṉmanēya orumip pāṭṭi ēṟpaṭuttiyatip pōlavē irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷār tamiḻakat til āṉma nēya uṭaṉpāṭṭi uṟutippaṭuttiṉār. ivariyaṭuttu māyūram vētanāyakaṉār kōpālakiruṭṭiṇa pāratiyār, u.vē.cāminā tiyar, paritimāṟ kaliñar (cūriyanārāyaṇa cāttiri, pi. cuntaram (piṉṉaḷ) mutaliyōriṉ arumpaṇikaḷ tamiḻiyum, tēciya uṇarvi yum āṉmanēya muṟiyil vaḷarttaṉa. par.ci.yū. pōp, kālṭuvel mutaliyōriṉ arumpaṇiyāl tiraviṭarkaḷ "orutāy makkaḷ' eṉa aṟiyappaṭṭut teṉṉavar', 'tiraviṭar' eṉṟa uṇarvu koṇṭu iṇin taṉar. ākavē, teṉṉāṭṭil kuṟippākat tamiḻakattil tōṉṟiya viṭutali vēṭkikkum, orumippāṭṭiṟkum tamiḻ moḻiyē aṭippaṭiyāka amintatu. muṟpōkkāḷar 
pālakaṅkātara tilakar pērāyak kaṭciyil muṭukkamākap paṅkēṟkumuṉ atu oru maṉuppōṭum kaṭciyākavum, paṭittavarukku vēli tēṭit tarum niliyamākavumē iruntatu. 1905-l kiḻāṉ karcaṉ pirapu (789-1905) vaṅkāḷatti iraṇṭākap pirittut tēciyavātikaḷiṉ etirppukku āḷāṉāṉ. itaṉiyetirttu eḻunta kiḷarcciyil intuk cuḷum, muslīmkaḷum orumaṉatāyk kalantukoṇṭaṉar. piṉṉar, itu tēciyak kiḷarcciyāka vaḷarntatu. aravintar vaṅka ēṭukaḷilum tilakar marāttiyēṭukaḷilum, tēciya uṇarccikaḷit tāṇṭum pala ceytikaḷi

tāynilavaralāṟu 
veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. tamiḻakattilum pala tēciya ēṭukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. "taṉ ṉurimi (cutantiram) ematu piṟappurimi' eṉṟa tilakariṉ kural intiya nāṭṭuēṭukaḷilellām olittatu. 
1906ām āṇṭu mutal 1920 ām āṇṭu vari tilakar pērāyak kaṭciyiṉiṉṟu vilaki, 'tīviravātiyākit" tēciyappōri muṭukkit tiṟampaṭa ceyalpaṭumpaṭic ceytār. paṭṭatārikaḷum mēṭṭukkuṭiyiṉa rum taṉṉalattiṟkākap payaṉpaṭuttum kūṭāramāka irunta pērāyakkaṭci ivaruṭiya talimiyil ēḻiyeḷiyavariṉ (pāmarariṉ) pācaṟiyā yiṟṟu. tāymoḻi maṭṭumē kaṟṟavarkaḷum tēciya uṇarcciyit tūṇṭum valimipeṟṟaṉar. tamiḻakattil eṭṭayapuram ci. cuppiramaṇiya pāratiyār attakiyavaril oruvar, tamiḻāl taraṇiyit taṭṭiyeḻuppi, viṭutalip pāṭalkaḷ puṉinta nikaraṟṟa tēciyavātiyāṉa pāratiyār tamiḻariṉ paṇpiyum, vīrattiyum, iṟi neṟiyiyum, iyaṟkiyiyum 'tēciyam' eṉṟa tēṉil kuḻittu icittavar. muppatu kōṭi muka muṭiya intiyattāy patiṉeṭṭu mukāmi moḻikaḷiyum, palvēṟu moḻikaḷiyum pēciṉālum avaḷ peṟṟa kuḻantikaḷ "orutāy makkaḷē' eṉakkūṟi intiya orumippāṭṭiṉik kaṇṭa pārati. iṅku āyiram cātikaḷiruppiṉum intiyar orē kuṭumpattiṉarē; itil ayalavaṉāṉa āṅkilaṉ taliyiṭa urimi illi, eṉṟum, 'vīṭṭuk kuḷḷē caṇṭi pōṭṭuk koḷvōm. vīṭu tīppaṟṟi eriyum pōtu oṉṟukūṭit tīyi aṇikkap pōrāṭuvōm' eṉa āṅkilat tīyi aṇikka intiyari aḻittavar pārati, pāratiyāriṉ naṇparāṉa cuppiramaṇiya civam oru muṟpōkkāṉa tēciyavātiyum, tamiḻt toṇṭarum āvār. ivarukku āṅkila aṭakkumuṟiyāṭci pattāṇṭu ciṟit taṇṭaṉi vaḻaṅkiyatu. ciṟimīṇṭa piṉṉarum ivar pala nūlkaḷi eḻutiṉār. 'ñāṉapānu' eṉṟa tiṅkaḷēṭṭi naṭattit tēciya uṇarvi vaḷarttār. ivar ciṟik kūṭattilirunta pōtu 'caccitāṉanta civam' eṉṟa nūli eḻuti vaṟumiyil vāṭiya taṉ maṉivikku aḷittārām. itit tavira ivar pala nūlkaḷiyum eḻutiṉār. vivēkāṉantariṉ āṅkila nūlkaḷil cilavaṟṟiyum tamiḻil ākkiṉār. kaḻaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷukku vaḻi nūlkaḷi eḻutit tamiḻarvīram maṟumalarcci yaṭiyac ceytār. tām naṭattiya tiṅkaḷēṭṭiṉ mūlam 'intunēcaṉ", 'cutēcamittiraṉ' ākiya ēṭukaḷ taṉmāṉa uṇarvum, taṉittamiḻ uṇarvu miṉṟik kiṭappati veṟuttār. 
ci. cuppiramaṇiyacivam naṭattiya "ñāṉapānu' ēṭum kappalōṭṭiya tamiḻar vana. citamparaṉār naṭattiya 'pārati' eṉṟa tiṅkaḷēṭum tēciya vātikaḷi ulakaṟiyac ceytaṉa. va.u.citamparaṉār oṭṭappiṭārattil piṟantavar kaṭṭapommaṉi viṭutali vīrar eṉap pāviyam pāṭiyavar; tēciya iyakkattil īṭupaṭṭu nāṟpatu āṇṭukkālam ciṟittaṇṭaṉi aṭinta ivar pāratiyāriṉ uyirttōḻar āṅkilarukku etirākak

kappalōṭṭiya tamiḻar; ciṟit taṇṭaṉip peṟṟuc ciṟiyilēyē cekkiḻutta pōtum āṉma nalac cintaṉiyilum, tamiḻ moḻiyilum aṟupaṭā cintaṉi koṇṭavar. 
va.vē. cuppiramaṇiya aiyar vaḻakkaṟiñar {pārisṭar paṭippukkāka ilaṇṭaṉ ceṉṟavar. nāṭṭuppaṟṟāl veḷḷiyarukku etirākac ceyalpaṭṭu avarkaḷiṉ taṇṭaṉiyiliruntu tappi māṟuvēṭam pūṇṭu tamiḻakampōntu tilakariṉ muṭukkamāṉa iyakkattil īṭupaṭṭavar. ivar, 'tēcapaktaṉ' eṉṟa kiḻamiyitaḻil āṅkila āṭciyic cāṭiyatāl oṉpatu tiṅkaḷ ciṟiyil vatintār. ciṟiyiliruntapōtu kamparip paṟṟiya ārāycci nūli āṅkilattil eḻutiṉār; ivar oru tamiḻ ilakkiya mēti, āṅkilam, ḵpireñcu, arapu, intucutāṉi ākiya moḻikaḷilum vallavar. ivaruṭiya paṉmoḻi aṟivum, 'pāriṉpaṭar' paṭippum, muṭukkamāṉa tēcappaṟṟum tamiḻakattil pala viṭutali vīrarkaḷi vaḷarttaṉar. ivar uruvākkiya tamiḻkkurukula vittiyālayam' iḷiñarkaḷ, nāṭṭu viṭutalip pōrukkup payiṟci aḷikkum niliya mākiyatu. 
tirunelvēli māvaṭṭam kaṭṭapommaṉ kālattiliruntē vīrattiṉ viḷikaḷamāy viḷaṅkiyatākum. vu.ciyum piṟarum tōṉṟiya im māvaṭṭattil parali nelliyappar tōṉṟiṉār. ivaruṭiya aṇṇaṉ parali cu. caṇmukacuntaram piḷḷiyi makkaḷ "vantē mātaram' piḷḷiyeṉṟē aḻittaṉar. tēciyakkavi cuppiramaṇiya pāratiyāriṉ uṭaṉpiṟavāt tampiyākat tikaḻnta parali cu. nelliyappar tamiḻit taṉ paṭiyākak koṇṭu viṭutalip pōril īṭupaṭṭār. pāratiyār naṭattiya 'intiyā' eṉṉum kiḻami ēṭṭilum, cuppiramaṇiya civam naṭattiya tiṅkaḷēṭāṉa "ñāṉapānu'vilum, vacecuaiyar naṭattiya pāla pārati'yilum, tiru.vi.ka. naṭattiya tēcapaktaṉ', 'navacakti' ākiya nāḷēṭu kaḷilum nelliyappar viṭutalip pāṭalkaḷiyum, kaṭṭurikaḷiyum eḻutiṉār.tāmē lōkōpakāri* eṉṟa kiḻami itaḻiyum naṭattiṉār. 
ivvāṟu tamiḻarkaḷ tamiḻvaḻi viṭutaliyi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. nāṭeṅkilum viḻippuṇarcciyum, viṭutali vēṭkiyum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. tilakariṉ muṭukkamāṉa pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷ tamiḻakattilum veṭittaṉa. tēciyavātikaḷ putuccēriyip pācaṟiyākak koṇṭu ceyalpaṭṭaṉar. 1911 ām āṇṭutilliyil aintām ciyārcu maṉṉaṉiṉ muṭicūṭṭu viḻā tarpār naṭantatu. itaṟkup patiṉāṉku kōṭi urupā celaviṭappaṭṭatu. in nikaḻcci muṟpōkkāḷarkaḷi veṟipiṭikkac ceytatu. makkaḷ paṭṭiṉiyāl vāṭumpōtu paṭāṭōpac celvā eṉakkotittaṉar. nāṭeṅkilum āṅkilarip palavakikaḷil kollum catit tiṭṭaṅkaḷ uruvāyiṉa. tamiḻakattil tirunelvēli māvaṭṭa āṭciyāḷarāyirunta ācu turi eṉṟa veḷḷiyaṉi vāñcinātaṉ eṉṟa iḷiñar cuṭṭuk koṉṟār. itaṟkuk karaṇiyam kuṟṟāla aruviyil veḷḷiyar kuḷikkum

ācu turiyic cuṭṭukkoṉṟa vāñcinātaṉ piṉṉar taṉṉit tāṉē cuṭṭu koṇṭāṉ. ivaruṭaṉ cērntu ic catiyil īṭupaṭṭa patiṉālvarmītu cativaḻakkut toṭarappaṭṭatu. caṅkarakiruṭṭiṇa ( ayyar, maṭattuk kaṭi citamparam piḷḷi ), muttukkumāracāmi (ppiḷḷi), cuppiyā piḷḷi), cakannāta ayyaṅkār, piccumaṇi (ayyar, vēmpu, arikaraṉ, tēcikāccāri, aḻakap piḷḷi , cāvaṭi 
aruṇācalam, pāṭippiḷḷi , vantē mātaram cuppiramaṇiya ayyar ākiyōr kuṟṟavāḷik kūṇṭilē niṟuttappaṭṭaṉar. nīlakaṇṭa pirammac cāri eṉṟa iḷiñar mutal kuṟṟavāḷiyāṉār. ivarkaḷ yāvarum iḷi ñarkaḷē. tīrppil ivarkaḷukkuc ciṟittaṇṭaṉi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. ittōṭu tamiḻakattil viṭutalippōr vērūṉṟiya āḻamum āṅkila rukku aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl tamiḻakattil viṭutalip pōr mēlum muṭukkamaṭintatu. 
talimik kāvalarākap paṇiyāṟṟiya kurunāta ayyar eṉpavar veḷḷiyar viṭutali maṟavarkaḷi oṭukka mēṟkoḷḷum tiṭṭaṅ kaḷit tōlviyaṭiyac ceytu, viṭutali maṟavarukkup paṭikaṉik koṭuttutaviṉār. itaṉāl ivar aṟāṇṭuk kaṭuṅkāval taṇṭaṉiyum peṟṟār. tilakar toṭaṅkiya tīvira iyakkattil paṅkēṟṟuc cutēci iyakkattip parappiya "cutēci ayyaṅkār' eṉappaṭum patmanāp (ayyaṅkār aṭakkumuṟikku āḷāṉār, cutēciyak kappalkaḻakam

āyiram urupāpit tēciyak kappal kaḻakattiṟku aḷittār. makkaḷ talikku nāṉku aṇāmēriporuntaviyaḷittaṉar. ivvāṟu cērtta paṇattiliruntu iraṇṭu kappalkaḷ vāṅkappeṟṟaṉa. va.u.ci. tamiḻil naralkaḷi eḻuti viṟṟu varum paṇatti ik kappalkaḻaka nitiyākka muyaṉṟār. ak kālattil tamiḻ nālkaḷi yārum vāṅkuvatilli, irācāci ivaṟṟit tāmē vāṅkikkoṇṭār. ap paṇam cutēcik kappal nitiyuṭaṉ cērntatu. cutēcik kappal kaḻakam ulakap pukaḻpeṟṟa, kaṭalāḷumi paṭitta veḷḷiyari yetirttu veṟṟi peṟuva teṉpatu vīṇkaṉavu. āyiṉum, viṭutaliyuṇarviṉ aṭippaṭiyil lēyē vuci kappali alikaṭal naṭuvuḷ celuttiṉār. 
vuciyiṉ muyaṟcikku uṟutuṇiyāy niṉṟavar parali cu. caṇmuka cuntaram piḷḷi āvār. vuci. vantē mātaram piḷḷi (parali cu. caṇmuka cuntarampiḷḷi), cuppiramaṇiyacivam ākiyōr tirunelvēlic cīmiyil viṭutali vēṭkiyip perukkeṭuttu ōṭac ceytaṉar. citamparaṉār ōṭṭiya kappal muṭavariyum uṇarvu peṟṟu viḻitteḻac ceytatu. tūttukkuṭiyil vāḻnta veḷḷiyarkaḷ iravil nakarukkuḷ tūṅkuvataṟku añci iravu muḻuvatum paṭakukaḷilēṟi kaṭalil mitantu tūṅkiṉareṉṟāl ivarkaḷiṉ viṭutalippōriṉ vēkatti ūkikka pām, 
1908-l tūttukkuṭiyil 'kōral' ālit toḻilāḷarkaḷ vēliniṟuttam ceytaṉar. tēciya viṭutali varalāṟṟil araciyal kalappuṭiya mutal vēliniṟuttam ituvēyākum. itaṟkut talimi tāṅki naṭattiyavar vuciyāvār. itaṉiyum oru kuṟṟamākakkoṇṭē avarukku vāḻnāḷtaṇṭaṉi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. intiyāvilēyē mutaṉ mutal toḻilāḷarkaḷi oṉṟupaṭuttit toḻiṟkaḻakattit tōṟṟu vittavarkaḷum viṭutali maṟavarē. 1918-l ceṉṉit toḻilāḷar kaḻakam" niṟuvappaṭṭatu. itaṉi niṟuviyavarkaḷ tiru. vi. kaliyāṇacuntara ṉār kuttikēcavap piḷḷi , ci. celvapati ceṭṭiyār, irāmaṉucalu (nāyuṭu, vāṭiyā ākiyōrāvar,

(visrāyṭacaviyil uṟuppiṉarāyirunta vayavar, ci.caṅkaraṉ (nāyar tam pataviyit tuṟantār. pākkuyil rōciṉi nāyuṭu taṉatu keyccar-i inta viruti vīciyeṟintār. nāṭṭiṉap pāvalar tākūr 'car' paṭṭatti utaṟiṉār. innikaḻcciyip paṟṟi eḻutiyatāḷikikaḷiṉ āciriyarkaḷ taṇṭaṉip peṟṟaṉar. ceṉṉiyil naṭattappaṭṭa 'intu' nāḷēṭ ṭiṟku īrāyiram urupā piṇiyattoki vitikkappaṭṭatu. viṭutalik kōruvatē kuṟṟam eṉṟu aracu caṭṭam koṇṭu vantatu. itaṉ kīḻ palar kuṟṟam cāṭṭappaṭṭut taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭaṉar. itaṟku em. ār. ceyakar,tilakar, kāntiyaṭikaḷ mutaliyōrum iriyāṉārkaḷ. ceṉṉi yil 'pāratatēvi' āciriyarukkāka allāṭi kiruṭṭiṇacāmi ayyar vaḻakkāṭi veṟṟi kaṇṭār. kānti āṇṭumāṉam (1920 - 1947) 
1918 ām āṇṭil pērāyak kaṭci tilliyil kūṭi mānilat taṉṉāṭci (Provincial Autonomy) vēṇṭumeṉṟu kōriyatu. mēlum ceytit tāḷkaḷ caṭṭam, kuṟṟaviyal tiruttac caṭṭam, araca iraṇṭakac caṭṭam, intiyap pātukāppuc caṭṭam mutaliyavaṟṟi nīkkumpaṭiyum kōriyatu. mānilaṅkaḷukku muḻumiyākat taṉṉāṭci (mānila cuyāṭci! vēṇṭumeṉṟu cilar vātiṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl pērāyak kaṭciyiṉar, meṉpōkkiṉar (mitavātikaḷ), vaṉpōkkiṉar (tīviravātikaḷ eṉṟu iraṇṭākap pirintaṉar. 
inniliyil kāntiyaṭikaḷ teṉṉāppirikkāviliruntu intiyā vukku vantār. pīkāril camparāṉ māvaṭṭattil avuri payiriṭuvōruk kum, airōppiya mutalāḷikaḷukkum iṭiyē pūcal ēṟpaṭṭatum, pampāy mānilattil ēṟpaṭṭa pañcattāl vāṭiya makkaḷi varutti āṅkila aracu varit taṇṭal ceytatum, kāntiyār pāṭukiṭappu cattiyār kirakap pōrilīṭupaṭak karaṇiyaṅkaḷāyiṉa. vallapapāy paṭṭēl ivaruṭiya pōrukkut tuṇiniṉṟār. mutal ulakappōrum muṭintatu. kāntiyārum tamatu amiti (cātvīkap pōril veṟṟi kaṇṭār, itutāṉ pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ veṟṟip pātiyiṉ toṭakkaviyal. 1919 ām āṇṭu aracuc caṭṭam mānilaṅkaḷil iraṭṭiyāṭci muṟiyik koṇṭuvantatu. viṭutali maṟavarukku itu oru periya ēmāṟṟamākiyatu. viṭutali maṟavariṉit taṉṉāṭciyum, poṟuppāṭci yum kōruvati viṭuttu muḻu viṭutali (pūraṇa cutantiram peṟa muṭivu ceytaṉar. in niliyil kāntiyaṭikaḷ pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ talivar āṉār. pāṭukiṭappu cattiyākkirakam, amiti (cātvīkap pōrāṭṭam, vaṉmuṟiyaṟṟa ottuḻiyāmi , caṭṭamaṟuppu, uṇṇā nōṉpu mutaliya putiya paṭikaḷik' āyutaṅkaḷ koṇṭut taṉatu viṭutalip pōrit toṭaṅkiṉār. ēṟkaṉavē, cāliyaṉvālāp paṭukoli, evariyum ucāvaliṉṟic ciṟiyilaṭikkum reṉalaṭ' caṭṭam mutaliyavaṟṟāl makkaḷ kotittuk koṇṭiruntaṉar. piriṭṭāṉiya āḷumiyi

etirttu eḻunta aṉittulaka iculāmiyariṉ kilāpat iyakkam oru payaṅkarap paṭiyāyiṟṟu. ic cūḻalil kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ talimiyil pērāyakkaṭci aṭakkumuṟikaḷukku iṭiyil meyyāyvu (cattiya cōtaṉikaḷit tāṇṭi valuppeṟṟatu. 1908 mutal 1920 vari tilakar kālam paṭitta 'tīvira iyalic cātvīta' iyalāka māṟṟiyavar kānti yaṭikaḷ āvar. ākavē, 1920 ām āṇṭu mutal viṭutaliyaṭiyum vari intac cātvīka muṟiyilāṉa "kāntiya cakāptam' tōṉṟiyatu. itil tamiḻarkaḷiṉ paṅkic curuṅkak kāṇalām. 
kāntiyaṭikaḷi 1920 ām āṇṭu nākapuriyil naṭanta mānāṭu pērāyakkaṭciyiṉ talivarāka ēṟṟatu. ottuḻiyāmi iyakkam paṟṟi im mānāṭu muṭivuceytu ataṉic ceyalpaṭuttat tīrmāṉittatu. moḻivaḻi mānilaṅkaḷip pirikkum kōṭpāṭum uṟuticeyyap paṭṭatu. itaṉāl, āṅkila moḻiyiṉ ātikkattiyakaṟṟit tāymoḻi kaḷ intiya moḻikaḷ vaḷarum vāyppum, itaṉ vaḻi āṅkila mēlāṇmikku aṭippaṭi uṟutiyaṟṟup pōkum tiṭṭamum oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu. āṅkilak kalviccālikaḷi maṟuppatu, nāṭṭiṉak kalviccālikaḷiyamippatu, nāṭṭiṉ moḻiyākat tēvanākari eḻuttil eḻutappaṭṭa intiyi ēṟpatu mutaliya tīrmāṉaṅkaḷum kāntiyaṭikaḷ talimiyil niṟivēṟiṉa. in niliyiltāṉ 'tāy moḻippaṟṟētāynāṭṭup paṟṟu' eṉṟa uṇmi yuṇarvum tōṉṟiyatu. nāṭṭiṉap pāvalar cuppiramaṇiya pāratiyārum centamiḻ nāṭṭiyum, tantiyar nāṭṭiyum iṇittup pāṭiṉār. "vāḻiya centamiḻ vāḻka naṟṟamiḻar, vāḻiya pārata maṇittirunāṭu' eṉat tāymoḻiyiyum, tamiḻariyum, intiya nāṭṭiyum vāḻttiṉār. tēciyam immuṟiyil puttuṇarcciyuṭaṉ vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. viṭutalip pōrukkut tāy moḻikaḷum, tāymoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷum makkaḷit taṭṭiyeḻuppa utaviṉa. āṅkila mōkam kuṟintatu. 
kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ ottuḻiyāmi iyakkattit tamiḻakattil toṭaṅkiyavar irācāciyē. itil kilāpat iyakkattiṉarum muculīm kaḷum paṅkēṟṟaṉar. itaṉāl palar muḻunēramum nāṭṭuppaṇiyil īṭupaṭṭaṉar, paṭṭitoṭṭikaḷilellām viṭutali iyakkam paraviyatu. tīṇṭāmiyi oḻikkum iyakkam mummuramaṭintatu. caṭṭamaṟuppu, ottuḻiyāmi mutaliyavaṟṟil īṭupaṭṭuc ciṟipukuvatip puṉitap paṇiyāka makkaḷ karutiṉar. nayaṉmi maṉṟaṅkaḷiyum, piṟa āṅkila niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷiyum, vaḻakkuriñar mutal kuṭiyāṉavar vari yāvarum puṟakkaṇittaṉar. pērāyakkaṭciyiṉ potucceyalāḷarkaḷil oruvarāṉa irācāci 192ām āṇṭil tamiḻakameṅkum ottuḻiyāmi iyakkat tip parappiṉār. ivvāṇṭu ṭicampar 2ām nāḷ irācāci, ī. vē. irāmacāmi (tanti periyār), āraṇi cuppiramaṇiya cāttiri mutaliyōr taṭi uttaravi mīṟi vēlūril ottuḻiyāmi iyakkattip paṟṟiya
