miēḻakamuētal rakam), 
kūṭṭattil pēciṉar. irācāci kitu ceyyappaṭṭu mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷ ciṟit taṇṭaṉip peṟṟār. pāṭukiṭappuc cattiyākirakam ceytu, ottuḻiyāmi iyakkattit toṭaṅki mutaṉmutal ciṟi ceṉṟavar irācāciyē. itaṟku vaḻikāṭṭiyatum tamiḻakamē. kaḷḷukkaṭi maṟiyal 
kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ āṇiyi ēṟṟu ottuḻiyāmi iyakkatti mēṟkoṇṭa tamiḻakamē avaruṭiyakumukāyac cīrtiruttamāṉa kaḷḷuk kaṭikaḷi mūṭutal, tīṇṭāmi oḻippu, kinnūral katar), kittoḻil kaḷip parapputal mutaliyavaṟṟilum vaḻikāṭṭiyatu. 1921-l īrōṭṭil ī. vē. irāmacāmi (tanti periyār taṉakkuc contamāṉa teṉṉi, paṉi maraṅkaḷi veṭṭi vīḻtti matuvilakkut tiṭṭattit toṭaṅkiṉār. kaḷḷukkaṭikaḷiṉ muṉ maṟiyal ceytu ciṟippaṭṭār. ivaruṭaṉ ivaruṭiya maṉiviyār nākammiyum, taṅki kaṇṇammāḷum ciṟippaṭṭaṉar. ciṟappākap peṇkaḷ, atuvum celvak kuṭumpattup peṇkaḷ kāntiyiṉ kaṭṭaḷippaṭi maṟiyali naṭattic ciṟipukunta varalāṟu tamiḻakattil ituvē mutalākum. kāntiyār, tamiḻakattil kaḷḷukkaṭi maṟiyalukku vittiṭṭu vaḷarttavarkaḷ ip peṇkaḷē' eṉṟār. tamiḻakattil 'kaḷḷukkaṭi maṟiyal' iyalit toṭaṅkiyavarē periyārtāṉ. ivarip piṉpaṟṟit tamiḻakameṅkum kaḷḷukkaṭikaḷiṉ muṉ toṇṭarkaḷ amiti neṟiyil maṟiyal ceytaṉar. maturiyil vittiyanāta ayyar eṉpār maṟiyalil īṭupaṭṭapōtu ciṉaṅkoṇṭa kaṭikkāraṉ kaḷ kuṭatti avar talimītu kaviḻttāṉām, arakkō caṇattil ākkūr āṉantāccāri, camatakkaṉi ākiyōr maṟiyal ceyta pōtu kaṭikkāraṉ camatakkaṉiyik kattiyāl kuttiṉāṉ. tiruvaṇṇā mali māvaṭṭattil kaḷḷukkaṭi maṟiyalilīṭupaṭṭut toṇṭarkaḷ ciṟiceyyappaṭṭup paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷilum, cāvaṭikaḷilum aṭikkap paṭuvatum, piṉṉar, ciṟikku aṉuppappaṭuvatum ak kāla vaḻakkam. avvāṟu irācāci oru paḷḷikkūṭattil aṭikkappaṭṭirunta pōtutāṉ cakacāṉanta aṭikaḷ eṉṟa tuṟavikkum, avarukkum naṭpuṟavu ēṟpaṭṭatu. aṉṟu mutal cakcāṉantarum muṟpōkkāṉa viṭutali maṟavarāṉār. tiruvaṇṇāmali māvaṭṭam mēlputuppākkattil piṟanta ivvaṭikaḷē piṉṉar citamparam nantaṉār kalviccāliyi ēṟpaṭuttiya var. ivaruṭaṉ irācāci camapantiyil uṇṭataṟkākap pirāmaṇar palarum avaric cātiyiviṭṭu nīkkiṉar. ēṉeṉil, cakacāṉanta aṭikaḷār oru tīṇṭattakāttāḻkulattavar.kaḷḷukkaṭi maṟiyalil īṭupaṭṭataṟkākac cilampuc celvar ma. po. civa ñāṉatti avar iṉattārē cātiyi viṭṭu nīkkiṉar. avar iṉattār kaḷ iṟakkum toḻilil īṭupaṭṭavarkaḷ, āṉāl, mapociyō kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ koḷkiyāl īrkkappaṭṭavar. tīṇṭāmi oḻippu 
pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ maṟṟoru koḷki tīṇṭāmiyi oḻippatu. itaṟkum tamiḻakamē vaḻikāṭṭiyāka amintatu. irācāci tirucceṅkōṭṭil putuppāḷiyam eṉṟa ūril “kānti āciramam' oṉṟi 1924l toṭaṅkiṉār. itil carkkāvik koṇṭu nūl nūṟṟal, puṟa oḻukkam, nāṭṭup paṟṟu, tīṇṭāmi oḻippu mutaliyavaṟṟil payiṟciyaḷikkappaṭṭatu. cēlam nakaracavit talivarāyirukkumpōtu irācāci kuḻāy paḻutu pārkkum paṇiyil tāḻttappaṭṭavari amarttit taṉ iṉattavarukkup pakivarāṉār. itippōlavē ip pāḻiyilum āciramattilum tāḻttappaṭṭavarkaḷiyē paṇiyil amarttiṉār. avar tāṉē cakacāṉanta aṭikaḷuṭaṉ campantiyil uṇṭu, tam iṉattavariṉ paḻikku āḷāṉatip pārttōm. ivvāṟu, irācāci tam vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum tāḻttappaṭṭavariṉ nalattiṟkup pāṭupaṭṭati yārum maṟukkamuṭiyātu. 
1923-l tanti periyār pērāyakkaṭciyiṉ tamiḻnāṭṭut talivar āṉār. īrōṭṭilirunta avar vīṭēkaṭciyaluvalakamākavum, toṇṭar kaḷukku aṟaccāliyākavum māṟiyatu. tāḻttappaṭṭa makkaḷ tamiḻaka meṅkilum kēvalamāy naṭattappaṭṭaṉar. kōyilkaḷil nuḻiya aṉumatikkappaṭavilli . 1919-l tiruvitāṅkūril kōyilukkuc cellum urimikōri kiḷarcci naṭattiṉar. ataṟku ṭi. kē. mātavaṉ eṉpār talimitāṅkiṉār. 23-l kākkiṉāṭāvil mulāṉā mukamatali talimiyil pērāyak kaṭci tāḻttappaṭṭavarkaḷik kōyilukkuḷ aṉumatikkumpaṭi tīrmāṉam pōṭṭatu. 1974-l kēraḷattiluḷḷa vikkam eṉṟa ūril kōyiluḷḷa teruvilkūṭat tāḻttappaṭṭa varkaḷ pōkakkūṭāteṉṟa taṭiyi etirttup pōrāṭṭam toṭaṅkap peṟṟatu. itaṉit talimitāṅki naṭattiyavar tanti periyār āvār.taṭiyaṭikkup piṉṉum uṟutiyuṭaṉiruntu itil veṟṟi kaṇṭa periyār vikkam vīrar' āṉār. 1932-l kuruvāyūr kōyilukkuḷ tāḻttappaṭṭōr aṉumatikkappaṭavēṇṭumeṉak kēḷappaṉ eṉpār uṇṇānōṉpu nōṟṟār. āṉāl, 1936-l tāṉ vayavar cipi. irāmacāmi ayyar) tiruvitāṅkūr tivāṉāka iruntapōtu kēraḷak kōyilkaḷ tāḻttappaṭṭōrukku tiṟantu viṭappaṭṭaṉa. 4-8 1939-l irācāci ceṉṉi maṇṭil mutalamiccarāyirunta pōtu tāḻttappaṭṭōriṉ kuṟi kaḷiyum muyaṟciyil kōyil nuḻivu' mutal kaṭṭamākat toṭaṅkap peṟṟatu. ivvāṟu tīṇṭāmiyi oḻikkak kāntiyārum pērāyak kaṭciyum mummuramāka muṉintum ip pērāyak kaṭciyilum tīṇṭāmi iruntatu. pāḻikaḷilum itu kaṭip piṭikkappaṭṭatu. pirāmaṇar uyantavarkaḷākak karutappaṭṭaṉar. in niliyil ceṉṉi māṇṭilatti āṇṭa nayaṉmik kaṭci (nītikkaṭci) tāḻttappaṭṭavarkaḷi vañcittatu. pērāyak kaṭciyil cāti vēṟupāṭu

iruppati etirttuttāṉ, periyār pērāyak kaṭciyiviṭṭu 1925-l nīṅkiṉār. 
kirāṭṭi 
kāntiyiṉ uyirnāṭiyāṉa kirāṭṭiyāl nūṟṟuk katarip parappum paṇiyil tamiḻakattil mutaliṭam peṟṟavar tanti periyār āvār. celvarāṉa avar kinnūṟṟuṇi mūṭṭiyit tōḷmītu cumantu terutteruvāy alintukinnūṟṟuṇi (cutar) viṟṟār. tilakar kinnūl toḻilip parapputaṟkut tamiḻakattil oru kōṭi urupā poruḷtiraṭṭavēṇṭum eṉṟār. āṉāl, aṉṟu tamiḻakattilirunta perumpaṇakkārarkaḷ nayaṉmik kaṭciyic cērntavarkaḷāyum, kāntiyiyum, pērāyak kaṭciyiyum etirppavarkaḷāyumiruntatāl ēḻi makkaḷē īrilakkam vari poruḷ , aḷittaṉar. kinnāltuṇi uṟpatti yāl kānti makiḻntār. 'cēvākirāmam" piṟantatu; 'carvōtayam" tōṉṟiyatu. 
ivvāṟu kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ tiruppaṇikaḷil īṭupaṭṭat tamiḻakat toṇṭarkaḷi ilēcāka eṇṇiviṭamuṭiyātu. 
1921-l virutunakaril tanti periyār pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ kōṭpāṭukaḷiyum kāntiyāriṉ kumukāyap paṇikaḷiyum viḷakkip pēciya pēccukaḷ pattoṉpatu akaviyē nirampiya iḷiñar ku. kām rācikcuvarntaṉa. erimalipa. cīvāṉantam(mūkkāṇṭi mutaliya ilakkak kaṇakkāṉa tamiḻ maṟavarkaḷ ip paṇikaḷil īṭupaṭṭaṉar. catāvatāṉam te.po.kiruṭṭiṇacāmippāvalariṉ nāṭakak kuḻuviṉar katariṉ veṟṟi" eṉṟa nāṭakatti naṭattiṉar. itil ṭikē. ecu. uṭaṉpiṟappār (cakō tararkaḷ paṅkēṟṟaṉar. itu pōṉṟa pala nāṭakaṅkaḷum, nūlkaḷum, tāḷikikaḷum matuvilakku, tīṇṭāmi, kinnūl mutaliya iyakkaṅkaḷi vaḷarkka utaviṉa. cimaṉ āṇikkuḻu 
1927-l intiyarukku viṭutaliyaḷippatippaṟṟi nēraṭiyāka ucāval naṭattak kiḻāṉ cimaṉ talimiyil oru kuḻu intiyāvukku vantatu. "iḵtu oru kaṇ tuṭippu" eṉa aṟinta viṭutali maṟavar itaṉi yetirppateṉa muṭivu ceytaṉar. nāṭeṅkilum itaṟku etirpput terivikkappaṭṭatu. "veḷḷi āṇikkuḻuvē tirumpippō' eṉṟu makkaṉ āravāram ceytaṉar. itaṉāl, nāṭeṅkilum taṭiyaṭiyum, tumukkiccūṭum naṭantaṉa. pañcāpu arimā ilālā lacapatirāy taṭiyaṭi paṭṭu māṇṭār. veḷḷiyarikkollum muyaṟcikaḷ nāṭeṅkilum taṭantaṉa. toṇṭarkaḷmītu poy vaḻakkukaḷ cōṭikkappaṭṭu, vaḻakkut toṭarappaṭṭut taṇṭikkappaṭṭaṉar, palar tākkiliṭappaṭṭaṉar. ceṉṉiyil āntirakēcari ṭi. pirakācam talimi tāṅki cimaṉ āṇik kuḻuvi etirttār. tumukkiyik kāṭṭi ōṭippōkumpaṭi 
kāṉa tamiḻ maṟivatu. caṉavariṉ nāṭakak kuḻu 
cāppār (cakō 
kipi. 19, 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tamiḻakam

eccaritta veḷḷik kāval atikārikku "cuṭṭā tuṇiviruntāl' eṉṟu caṭṭiyik kiḻittu mārpit tiṟantu kāṭṭiṉār pirakācam. itaṉik kaṇṭu acantupōṉa anta atikāri tumukkiyik kīḻē pōṭṭuviṭṭu kinaṭuṅka vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ. anta iṭattiltāṉ (=aṇṇāmali maṉṟat tiṟketiril pirakācattiṟkuc cili vikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu). pirakācam accameṉpatiyē aṟiyāta nāṭṭuppaṟṟāḷar. ilākūrc cativaḻakkil īṭupaṭuttic ciṟit taṇṭaṉi peṟṟavarkaḷil tamiḻakattil kāmarācarum oruvar, uppup pāṭukiṭappu(cattiyākirakam) 
1930 jaṉavari mutal pakkalukkuḷ intiyāvukku 'ṭomiṉiyaṉ' kuṭiyēṟṟa nili uyarvu koṭuttuviṭṭu, intiyarē āḷumpaṭi viṭṭuviṭṭu āṅkilar veḷiyēṟa vēṇṭumeṉa mōtilāl talimi yil kūṭiya aṉittuk kaṭcikaḷiṉ mānāṭṭil tīrmāṉikkappaṭṭup piriṭṭaṉukku aṟivikkappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, kāntiyaṭikaḷuṭaṉ pēccukaḷ naṭattiya aracappaṭinikarāḷi viṭutali maṟṟavarukkuk kaṇtuṭippākac cila calukikaḷi maṭṭumē aḷittār. roṭṭi kēṭkappaṭṭatu, kaltāṉ koṭukkappaṭṭatu' eṉṟār aṭikaḷār. eṉavē, kāntiyār caṭṭatti mīṟi ovvoru intiyaṉukkum urimiyāṉa uppeṭukkum pōrāṭ ṭattit toṭaṅkiṉār. "cey allatu cettumaṭi' eṉṟu āṇiyiṭ ṭār. nāṭeṅkilum talivarkaḷiṉ talimiyil toṇṭarkaḷ kaṭaliliruntu uppeṭukkum urimip pōrāṭṭattit toṭaṅkiṉar. kāntiyaṭikaḷ talimiyil 1930 mārccu 12 ām nāḷ toṇṭarkaḷ tāṇṭikkup puṟappaṭṭaṉar. mēttiṅkaḷ nāṉkām nāḷ kānti caṭṭatti mīṟi uppu eṭuttataṟkākap pūṉāviluḷḷa eravāṭā ciṟiyil aṭikkap paṭṭār. kāntiyārit toṭarntu ēppiral patiṉāṉkām nāḷil tamiḻakattil irācāciyiṉ talimiyil nūṟu toṇṭarkaḷ tirucciyil liruntu tirumaṟikkāṭṭiṟku vētāraṇyam uppeṭukkum puṉitap payaṇatti mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. tirucci, ṭi ecu.ecu. irācaṉ avarkaḷ vaḻiyaṉuppi vittār. civakurunātaṉ eṉṟa yāḻppāṇattut toṇṭar caṅkukati muṉ cellat toṇṭarkaḷ iṟineṟip pāṭalkaḷip pāṭikkoṇṭē piṉ naṭantaṉar. toṇṭarkaḷukku vaḻiyil nīr koṭuppavarum taṇṭikkappaṭuvareṉṟu aracu āṇi piṟappittiruntatu. toṇṭarkaḷ pāratiyāriṉ vīramikka viṭutalip pāṭalkaḷiyum, ciṟappāka nāmakkal pāvalar irāmaliṅkam piḷḷi ) avarkaḷiṉ 'kattiyiṉṟi irattamiṉṟi yuttam oṉṟu varukutu; cattiyattiṉ nittiyatti nampum yārum cēruvīr' eṉṟa pāṭaliyum vaḻineṭukap pāṭic ceṉṟaṉar. 
toṇṭarkaḷ tiruttuṟippūṇṭiyil nilakkiḻār irāmaccantira nāyuṭuvāl varavēṟkappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl, nāyuṭu kitu ceyyappaṭṭār. paṭi tirumaṟikkāṭṭi neruṅkum pōtu cartār vētarattiṉam (piḷḷi) taṉ pukiyilit tōṭṭattiyē aḻittut toṇṭarka 
176 
tāynilavaralāṟu 
ḷukkuk kūṭāramiṭṭut taṅkavum, uṇavu mutaliya ēntukaḷiyum ceytu koṭuttār. irācāci, kē, cantāṉam, maṭṭappāṟi vēṅkaṭarāmiyar, kē. ecu. cuppiramaṇiyam, ci. irāmaccantiraṉ, urukkumaṇi laṭcumipati ākiyōriṉ talimiyil ovvoru nāḷum toṭarntu toṇṭarkaḷ maṟiyal ceytaṉar. kāvaltuṟiyiṉarum toṭarntu ivarkaḷic ciṟip paṭuttiṉar. 
ceṉṉik kaṭaṟkariyil car, naṭarācaṉ eṉpār talimiyil toṇṭarkaḷ uppup pāṭuṭippuc ceytaṉar. ivar cattiyamūrttiyip pōl āṅkilattil pēcum āṟṟal paṭittavar. itil turkkāpāy ammiyār, camatakkaṉi mutaliyōrum paṅkēṟṟaṉar. kāvaltuṟiyiṉar taṭiyaṭi ceytatil palariṉ ki kālkaḷ muṟintaṉa. 
uppup pāṭukiṭappiṉpōtu aṟappōrāṭṭa vīrarkaḷit tākkiya aracik kaṇṭittuc ceṉṉit tilakar kaṭṭaṭattil naṭanta kūṭṭattiṉmītu kāvalar tumukkic cūṭu naṭattiṉar. ī.kē.kōvintacāmi eṉṟatoṇṭar kuṇṭukku iriyāṉār. 
1932 ām āṇṭu caṉavari 24-l tamiḻakameṅkilum taṭiyi mīṟi 'viṭutalināḷ' koṇṭāppaṭṭatu. ceṉṉi ūrvalattil em. paktavaccalamum, mutturaṅka mutaliyārum, ātikēcavalunāyuṭuvum piṟarum kalantu koṇṭaṉar. im mūvariyum kāvalar taṭiyāl aṭittuc ciṟiyil aṭittaṉar. 
1932-l taṭiyuttaravi mīṟi ūrvalam ceṉṟatāl ma.po. civañāṉamum ciṟit taṇṭaṉi peṟṟār. itaṉāl, ivar ceytuvanta accuk kōkkum vēliyum pōyiṟṟu. ivarippōl nāṭṭup paṟṟāḷar palarum vāḻviḻantaṉar. itil kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyum, kuṭumpa mum, kuṭumpaṅkaḷum, ōr ūrumkūṭa oṭṭumottamākap paṅkēṟṟaṉar. toṇṭarkaḷ orupuṟam perukum pōtu, taṭi uttaravukaḷum, aṭakku muṟikaḷum maṟupuṟam atikarittaṉa. 'cutantirac caṅku' tāḷikiyiṉ āciriyar caṅku kaṇēcaṉ, nāṭṭuppaṟṟit tūṇṭum nūlkaḷi malivup patippil veḷiyiṭṭa ci.ē.naṭēcaṉ, cuppiramaṇiyacivam, ṭi. ecu. cokka liṅkam, cīṉivāca varataṉ, kalki catācivam mutaliyōriṉ paṇikaḷ pōṟṟat takkaṉa. ikkālattil eḻuccimiku pēccukkuc cattiya mūrttiyē mukaṭāṉa var. ivaruṭiya araciyal māṇavartāṉ peruntalivar kāmarācar, uppuppāṭukiṭappil īṭupaṭṭa kāmarācarukku iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ ciṟittaṇṭaṉi kiṭittatu. ivarit talivar cattiyamūrtti tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ taliciṟanta ūḻiyar, eṉatu arumit tōḻar, ālōcakar' eṉṟu kūṟuvār. pēṭṭi mutturaṅka mutaliyārukku em. paktavaccalam araciyal māṇākkar, kāntiyārukkup pala kōṭi māṇākkarkaḷ iruntaṉar. avarkaḷil taliciṟantavar nāttikar cūttirattic cērnta pārppaṉarāṉa 'kōrā' (irāmacantirarāv) eṉpārum oruvar.ākak kānti

'taṉi vākkāḷar paṭṭiyal taṅkaḷukkeṉa uruvākka vēṇṭumeṉṟum kōriṉar. cimaṉ āṇik kuḻuvum ivarkaḷiṉ kōrikkikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil taṉippaṭinikarāḷiyam' otukkiyatu.tāḻttappaṭṭōr nalaṉkaḷik kavaṉikkat toḻilāḷar tuṟi ēṟpaṭṭatu. innērattil tāṉ ivarkaḷukkuk kōyil nuḻivu kōri kiḷarccikaḷ naṭantaṉa.

. 1937-l naṭanta potuttērtalil pērāyakkaṭci 25 iṭaṅkaḷil 150 iṭaṅkaḷip peṟṟatu. irācāciyum, cattiya mūrttiyum ceyta coṟ poḻivukaḷē inta veṟṟiyit tēṭittantaṉa. āḷunar taṉatu taṉiyati kāraṅkaḷik kiyāḷāviṭṭāl tāmē amiccaraviyi amip patāy irācāci kūṟiṉār. avvāṟu āḷunar vākkaḷittapiṉ pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ amiccaravi (1937-39) irācāci talimiyil amintatu. āṉāl, iraṇṭām ulakappōril pērāyakkaṭciyik kalakkāmalēyē piriṭṭaṉ intiyarippōril īṭupaṭac ceytatiyetirttu, irācāci amiccaraviyik kalittār. 
1940-l 'taṉippaṭṭavar pāṭukiṭappu' (taṉinapar cattiyākkirakam naṭantatu. atil paṅkeṭutta irācāci kitu ceyyappaṭṭār. avari ucāvic ciṟit taṇṭaṉi vaḻaṅkiya nayaṉmiyar caṉāp appācu ali taṉ kaṭamiyic ceyvatāyppacuvatkītiyiliruntu mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭit tīrppuk kūṟiṉār. toṭarntu pala toṇṭarkaḷ ciṟippaṭṭaṉar. 
1947-l kiripcu eṉpavar talimiyil oru kuḻu piriṭṭāṉiyap pēraracāl intiyāvukku aṉuppappaṭṭatu. pōr muṭittavuṭaṉ intiyāvukku viṭutali vaḻaṅkuvatip paṟṟiya aṟikkiyi ak kuḻu veḷiyiṭṭatu. atil pākicuttāṉ piriviṉi paṟṟiyum kāṇappaṭṭatu. kāntiyaṭikaḷ pākicutāṉ pirivati virumpavilli . irācāciyum, kāmarācarum maṟṟum tamiḻacut talivarkaḷum pākistāṉ pirivati virumpiṉar. mukamatali ciṉṉā 'eṅkaḷiṉ piṇaṅkaḷiṉ mēl pākicu tāṉik kaṭṭuvōm' eṉṟār. in niliyil kāntiyaṭikaḷ pampāy mānāṭṭil veḷḷiyaṉē veḷiyēṟu' tīrmāṉattik koṇṭu vantār. nēruvē itaṉi muṉmoḻintār. ciṉṉā intiyāvip pirittuviṭṭu veḷiyēṟu' eṉṟu muḻaṅkiṉār. kānti tāḻttappaṭṭa makkaḷi oṭukki aḻuttiyatip pōl muslīmkaḷi aḻutta muṭiya villi. pākistāṉ pirivatu uṟutiyāyiṟṟu. ākacṭṭu puraṭci (1942)

"yātum ūrē yāvarum kēḷir" eṉpatu tamiḻariṉ cālpu. ākavē, intu iculāmiyar pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷ iṅkut talikāṭṭavilli. āyiṉum, uyarcāti intukkaḷ paṉṉeṭuṅkālamāka aḻuttappaṭṭuk kiṭakkum tāḻkulattavarkaḷiṉ mītu aṭikkaṭi cātip pōrkaḷit toṭuttut tāḻkulattōr talitūkka muṭiyāmal ceytaṉar. av vēḻikaḷ, etirkkat teriyāta kōḻikaḷ, nellikkāy muṭṭiyeṉac citaṟic cīraḻintu kiṭantaṉar. āka, tamiḻakattil uyarntōr, tāḻntōr eṉṟa iru piriviṉarē iruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟutaṟkumilli. orē cātiyil pēcum moḻi, vāḻum iṭam, uṇṇum uṇavu, vaṇaṅkum kaṭavuḷ mutaliyavaṟ ṟālum vēṟupaṭṭuk kulappūcalkaḷil īṭupaṭṭuk kutti, veṭṭi māyntaṉar. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, koḷḷēkālil vāḻnta ceṭṭikkulatta var kaṉṉaṭamum, vēlūril vāḻntavar kaṉṉaṭamum tamiḻum pēciṉar.

moḻiyālum iṭattālum ivarkaḷukkuḷḷē pala uṭpirivukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟē piṉpaṟṟum camayattāl kōmuṭṭicceṭṭikaḷ, viṇava ceṭṭikaḷ mutaliya pirivukaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. pirāmaṇaruk kuḷḷum ittakiya pirivukaḷ uṇṭu. ip pirivukaḷāl tamiḻaka makkaḷ oṉṟiṇintu vāḻum vāyppaṟṟup pōyviṭṭatu. tāḻttappaṭṭa varkaḷ allatu tīṇṭappaṭātavarkaḷiṭattilum paḷḷar, paṟiyar, taṅkalāṉ, cāmpuvāṉ, aruntatiyaṉ mutaliya palvēṟu pirivukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. paḷḷar, paṟiyarukkuḷḷēyum ēṟṟattāḻvukaḷ nilavi varukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉ viḷivāl iṉṟiya tamiḻaka āṭciyil paḷḷaruk keṉavum, paṟiyarukkeṉavum taṉittaṉi amiccar patavi aḷikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl, tāḻttappaṭṭa ivviṉattār mēlum pakimi uṇarvu koṇṭu pirintu vaḷarkiṉṟaṉar. itaṟku ivviṉattiluḷḷa paṭittavarkaḷum talivarkaḷum uyar atikārikaḷum uramiṭukiṉṟaṉar. 
ivvāṟu tamiḻnāṭṭuk kumukāyam paṭikkaṭṭukaḷip pōlum kuṟukku neṭukkuc caṭṭaṅkaḷippōlum pala cātikaḷāyp pirintu kiṭantatu kiṭakkiṉṟatu. eṉavē, ivarkaḷiṉ tirumaṇam maṟumaṇam, maṟṟum vāḻviṉ kūṟupāṭukaḷil orē vakiyāṉa paṇpukkūṟukaḷik kāṇamuṭiyavilli. cila cātiyiṉar kuḻanti maṇattiyum, cilar maṟumaṇattiyum kaṭippiṭittaṉar. camayattiṉ aṭippaṭiyilum paṇpāṭukaḷ māṟupaṭṭaṉa.tāḻttappaṭṭavarkaḷ kiṟittuvarāka māṟit tāṅkaḷ tuyttu vanta koṭumikaḷiṉiṉṟu viṭutali peṟṟuk kalviyil muṉṉēṟiṉar. kiṟittuvam māṟiyōriṉ poruḷiyal uyarntatōṭu kumukāyattilum uyar cātiyiṉar avarkaḷi matittaṉar. aṉṟiya āṭciyāḷarāṉa āṅkilariṉ camayamum kiṟittavamātalāl tāḻttappaṭ ṭōriṉ camayamāṟṟam iṉṟiya kaṭcimāṟṟam pōlāyiṟṟu! āṅkivaruk kum, tamiḻarkaḷukkumiṭiyē ēṟpaṭṭa maṇa uṟavāl piṟanta putiya iṉattavar āṅkilō intiyar allatu caṭṭikkārar' eṉappaṭṭaṉar, necavuttoḻil ceytōrkikkōlar allatu ceṅkuntar eṉappaṭṭa ṉar. ki.pi. 12 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilēṟpaṭṭa valaṅki, iṭaṅkic cātip pirivukaḷ 19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum toṭarntaṉa. ivviru piriviṉarukku miṭiyē aṭikkaṭi pūcalkaḷ ēṟpaṭuvatum avi koliyilum koḷḷiyilum muṭivatum ik kālak kāṭcikaḷāyiṉa. kāñci viṇavar vaṭakali, teṉkali eṉṉum cikkalil māṭṭikkoṇṭu caṇṭiyiṭṭaṉar. tilliyilēvaḻipāṭu ceyta pirāmaṇar (pārppaṉar) tīṭcatarkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ vēṟu entap pārppaṉarōṭum maṇa uṟavukoḷḷamāṭṭārkaḷ. tirumaṇamāṉa pārppaṉa iḷiñaṉē pūci ceyvataṟku uriyavaṉāvāṉ. ātalāl ivarkaḷiṭiyē iḷamit tirumaṇap paḻakkam kāṇappaṭṭatu. pārppaṉarallāta āticivar, vīracivar mutaliyōrum pārppaṉarippōlavē kumukāyat toṭar piṉṟi, otuṅki, oḻukka ( ācāraṅkaḷik kaṭippiṭittu vāḻntaṉar.

karuṇīkar (cuṇakkar iṉattilum pala pirivukaḷ iruntaṉa. vēḷāḷarkaḷi lum, mutaliyārilum pala pirivukaḷum ēṟṟattāḻvukaḷum iruntaṉa. ākap pārppaṉar, pārppaṉarallātār eṉṟu tīviramākap pirikkappaṭṭa iruvakip pirivukaḷuḷḷum, valaṅki - iṭaṅki eṉṟu pirikkappaṭṭa pirivukaḷuḷḷum eṇṇat toliyāk kiḷiccātikaḷ muḷittu viṭṭaṉa. ākavētāṉ 'tamiḻakattil tamiḻarik kāṇa muṭiyavilli ; āṉāl ceṭṭiyār mutaliyār,nāyuṭukaḷik kāṇamuṭintatu' eṉṟāṉ ōr āṅkila ōrmiyāḷaṉ (cintaṉiyāḷaṉ). 
ittakiya cātip pirivukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyiltāṉ kalvi, poruḷiyal, araciyal celvākku mutaliyaṉavum amintaṉa. orupuṟam palavāyiram vēli nilaṅkaḷi uṭiyavarum, maṭavaḷākattār (ātiṉak karttākkaḷum, maṭattalivarkaḷum celva vāḻkkiyil tiḷittu makiḻntaṉar. maṟupuṟam pallāyirak kaṇakkāṉa ēḻimakkaḷ aṉṟāṭam kañcikkaḻum niliyil iruntaṉar. intiyap paṇittuṟit {I. tērvil tēṟiyavarum, āṅkilarum veṭkit talikuṉiyumpaṭi vaḷamāka āṅkilak kalvi kaṟṟavarum iruntaṉar. tiṇṇip paḷḷikkūṭattiyē aṟiyāta āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kuṭumpaṅkaḷum, cilakulaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. araciyalilē celvākkup peṟṟut tivāṉpakatūr', 'irāvpakatūr', 'car', 'niṭvuṭ' mutaliya virutukaḷuṭaṉ tikaḻntavarum iruntaṉar; ulakiyē aṟiyātavarum iruntaṉar. ūmiyarāyc ceviṭarkaḷāyk kuruṭarkaḷāy vāḻntavarē ērāḷam. 
cēvakaṉik kaṇṭañcum tamiḻaṉē mikuti. mūṭappaḻakkaṅkaḷilē mūḻki, vitiyi nontu vāḻntavarē mikuti, cērikaḷilē cakatikaḷilē uḻaṉṟavariṉ eṇṇikki mika atikam. in niliyiltāṉ vētānta - cittāntap pūcalkaḷum, civa-viṇavavāta etirvātaṅkaḷum tōṉṟit tamiḻarkaḷi mēlum piḷavupaṭuttiṉa. āṅlak kalvi payiṉṟu uyartara aracuppaṇikaḷil amarntōr inta nāṭṭiyē veṟuttaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ pēccum mūccum mēli nāṭṭilēyē uḻaṉṟaṉa. ic cūḻalil tōṉṟiya aṉittuc camayaṅkaḷukkum 'camaraca caṉmārkkam' kāṇavum, āṇṭavaṉi aruṭcuṭar' vaṭivil kāṭṭavum tamiḻarkaḷit tamiḻāl, aruḷāl kaṭṭippiṇittu oṟṟumikkāṇa muyaṉṟa irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷār in nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṇiyilāt tēciya- āṉmīkavātiyāvār. tattuvattil ārvamum ārāycciyum ēṟpaṭavum, āṉmīkavaḻi makkaḷ oṉṟupaṭavum ceṉṉi aṭiyāṟṟil pirammañāṉa cavi' tōṟṟu vikkappaṭṭatu. itil intu, muculīm, kiṟittavar, pārcikaḷ yāvarum iṇintu iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu orumippāṭṭi vaḷarttaṉar. matiyam oṉṟiyē peritākak karutiya veḷḷit taṉiyāḷumi intiyarik kacakkip piḻintu varivāṅkum pala muṟikaḷik kiyāṇṭatu, itaṉi orumukamāyk kaṇṭikkum pakkuvamaṟṟu intiyar vēṟupaṭṭu niṉṟakōr, tamiḻakattil tōṉṟiya nayaṉmik kaṭci pārppaṉarip paḻittu

kurukulaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉ tamiḻakattil potu makkaḷiṉ naṉkoṭi caḷikkoṇṭum, kōyiṟ cottukaḷik koṇṭum, aṟakkaṭṭaḷi kaḷik koṇṭum naṭattappaṭṭa 'āciriya māṇavar illaṅkaḷ' ivaṟṟil kalvi, uṇavu, uṭi, uṟiviṭam yāvum ilavacamāy vaḻaṅkap paṭṭaṉa. pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ cārpilum ittakiya "āciramaṅkaḷ' pala naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟil pārppaṉa māṇavarkaḷē iṭampeṟṟaṉar. pārppaṉarallāta māṇavarkaḷ puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭaṉareṉṟāl avarkaḷukkum kīḻirunta toltamiḻa (ātit tirāviṭa māṇavarkaḷukku itu oru kaṉavu ulakamē! pukaḻpeṟṟa nāṭṭup paṟṟāḷar va.ve.cuppira maṇiya aiyarē pārppaṉarallāta māṇavarit tām cēraṉmātēviyil naṭatti vanta kānti cēvā āciramattil cērkka maṟuttu āciramattiyē mūṭi viṭṭārām ittakiya kēṭukaḷi etirttuttāṉ tanti periyār 1929-lpērāyak kaṭciyiviṭṭē veḷiyēṟit taṉmāṉa (cuyamariyāti 
iyakkattiyum, kuṭiyaracu' itaḻiyum toṭaṅkiṉār. itaṉāl pārppaṉarallātāriṭiyē viḻippuṇarcci ēṟpaṭṭatu. pārpaṉiyatti yum, kumukāyakkēṭukaḷiyum etirkkum maṉappakkuvam tamiḻariṭiyē tōṉṟiyatu. mārṭṭiṉ lūtarāl kiṟittuvam putumiyaṭintatu pōlavē periyārāl intuc camayamum kaḻuvāykaḷit tēṭikkoṇṭu pārppaṉarallātāriyum matikkumpaṭi ceytatu, aṟaniliyap pātu kāppuc caṭṭamum niṟivēṟiyatu, tamiḻaka varalāṟṟil ciṟappākak kumukāya māṟṟattil tanti periyār oru viṭiveḷḷi: atirveṭiyuṅkūṭa - ivvāṟu pārppaṉari etirttuppārppaṉarallātār kumukāyap "pōṭṭiyil palavakiyilum muṉṉēṟumpōtu tāḻttappaṭṭavarka

oḻukkam', 'pūlōkaviyācaṉ', 'tamiḻaṉ', 'āti tirāviṭa mittiraṉ'. *puttuyir', 'jeypīm', 'camattuvam', toṇṭu', mutaliya ēṭukaḷum im makkaḷit taṭṭiyeḻuppiṉa. āṅkila atikārikaḷiṭam camiyaṟkārar (paṭlar kaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟiya ātit tirāviṭar cilariṉ piḷḷikaḷ āṅkilariṉ utaviyāl kalviyil muṉṉēṟiṉar. kiṟittuvak kurukulaṅkaḷ tāḻttappaṭṭōriṉ kuḻantikaḷi āḷākkiṉa. ivaṟṟil kaṟṟut teriyavarkaḷē piṟkālattil inta iṉattavariṉ talivarkaḷāki āṅkila aracuṭaṉ pōriṭṭuc caṭṭattāl urimikaḷip peṟac ceytaṉar. ivarkaḷil peruñcelvarkaḷāyt tikaḻnta kappal maturip piḷḷi, naṟukāy cakkaravartti pi. vi. cuppiramaṇiyam piḷḷi , irāvpakatūrar iraṭṭimali cīṉivācaṉ, em.ci.irācā mutaliyavarkaḷum, kaṟṟaṟinta varkaḷāyt tikaḻnta cē. civacaṇmukam piḷḷi , eṉ, civarācu mutaliya varkaḷum, ''tamiḻaṉ' ayōttitācap paṇṭitarum araciyal, camaya, kumukāya eḻuccikaḷil im makkaḷi vaḻippaṭuttiṉar. lōttiyaṉ kuḻu', cimaṉ āṇikkuḻu' mutaliya kuḻukkaḷilum, vaṭṭamēci mānāṭukaḷi lum ivarkaḷ paṅkēṟṟuttaṅkaḷiṉ kuṟikaḷi muṉvittu vaḻakkāṭit taṅkaḷukku viṭivu kaṇṭati yaṟikiṟōm. eṉavētāṉ viṭutalikku muṉṉum, viṭutalikkup piṉṉum, pūṉā oppantappaṭiyum ivarkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi urimikaḷ kākkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. caṭṭappaṭi iṉṟu tīṇ ṭāmi akaṟṟappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, uṇmiyil pēyāka ulavukiṟatu; araciyal iruṭṭil āḷik kollukiṟatu. 
iṉṟiya tamiḻakattil kumukāya nalam peritum vaḷamākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. makkaḷiṉ nalaṉkaruti palvēṟu caṭṭaṅkaḷum, tiṭṭaṅ cukaḷum koṇṭu varappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kalvi, potunalam, poruḷiyal mutaliyavaṟṟilum, ēṟṟat tāḻvukaḷi akaṟṟum vakiyilum tamiḻakam aṇṭi mānilaṅkaḷiviṭa muṉṉaṇiyil niṟkiṟatu. teluṅkar, maliyāḷiyar, kaṉṉaṭar ākiya aṇṭi mānilattārum, vaṭa intiyar kaḷum tamiḻakattil kuṭiyēṟit tamiḻarkaḷōṭu uṟavāṭi, oru kuṭumpatti ṉarip pōl vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. piṟa mānilaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum cēṉi kaḷum' pūcalkaḷum iṅku illi. "yātum ūrē yāvarum kēḷireṉṟa muntiyar moḻiyi iṉṟum kāppavar tamiḻarē! iṉit tamiḻarukkuḷ cātip pirivukaḷ illi ; tamiḻaṉ ulakak kuṭimakaṉ: itu naṉavāka vēṇṭumē! 
(ai) araciyal kipi. 19 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ceṉṉi maṇṭilattil tiruvitāṅkūr putukkōṭṭi mutaliya oru cila iṭaṅkaḷit tavira maṟṟavi āṅkila aracuṭaṉ iṇikkappaṭṭaṉa.kipi.1799-lkiḻāṉ velleli tañci maṉṉaruṭaṉ ōr uṭaṉpaṭikki ceytu koṇṭu am maṉṉar vāḻntu vanta kōṭṭi oṉṟit tavira tañci nāṭu muḻuvatiyum āḷum

ki.pi.18 ām āṭcimuṟi am kipi.1773l : 
poṟuppi ēṟṟatip pārttōm. oru cila 'jākīrtārkaḷum', jamīṉtārkaḷum', āṇṭa iṭaṅkaḷit tavirtta piṟapakutikaḷ āṅkila āṭciyiṉ nēraṭip pārviyiṉkīḻ vantaṉa. inta 'jākīrtārkaḷum', 'jamīṉtārkaḷum' āṅkila āṭciyiṉ mēlāṇmiyi yēṟṟavarkaḷē. kipi.18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil piriṭṭāṉiya nāṭāḷum maṉṟam āṅkila vaṇicukkuḻu āṭcimuṟiyi oḻuṅkupaṭuttavum. ataṉmītu nēraṭip pārviyiṉic celuttavum kipi.1773l oḻuṅkumuṟic caṭṭattiyum, ki.pi.1784-l 'piṭ' intiyac caṭṭattiyum, paṭṭiyac caṭṭaṅkaḷiyum koṇṭuvantatu. maṉṟattiṉ nēraṭi āḷukiyiṉkīḻ vantatu. ceṉṉi, pampāy mutaliya mānila āḷunarkaḷukku mēlākat talimi āḷunari' (Governer General) amarttappaṭṭār, 'piṭ' intiyac caṭṭappaṭi 'kaṭṭuppaṭuttum kaḻakam' oṉṟu ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. kuḻuvāṭciyiṉ poṟuppukaḷiyēṟṟa talimiyāḷunarum, avaruṭiya caviyārum ik kuḻuvāl kaṭṭuppaṭuttappaṭṭatōṭu āṇṭutōṟum kuḻuvāṭci yiṉ mukāmic ceytikaḷiyum, varavu-celavut tiṭṭaṅkaḷiyum ic cavikku aṉuppa vēṇṭum. 'piṭ' intiyac caṭṭappaṭi ceṉṉiyāḷunar caviyil āḷunaruṭaṉ cērntu nāṉku uṟuppiṉarkaḷ amarttappaṭṭa ṉar. āḷunar, cutēca maṉṉarkaḷiṉ ceytikaḷil taliyiṭutal, varit taṇṭal, paṭiyamippu, pōr, camiti (camātāṉam) mutaliyavaṟṟil talimi yāḷunariṉ icivu aṉumati peṟa vēṇṭumeṉa ic caṭṭam valiyuṟuttiyatu. 
itaṟkuppiṉ paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarnta āṅkila āṭciyāl ceṉṉi mānila araciyalilum vaḷarcci kāṇappaṭṭatu. mānilam nilavarit taṇṭuvataṟkum nayaṉmi nīti, paṭi, kāval muṟikaḷi valuppaṭuttuvataṟkum ēṟpap pala māvaṭṭaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭatu, nilavarit tuṟiyum, nilavari muṟiyum kiḻāṉ kāraṉvālicu (1786-93) kālattil cīruṭaṉ amittuc ceyalpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. kāraṉvālicu, caṭṭam, nayaṉmi , nilavari ākiya tuṟikaḷi virivākkic ciṟanta caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭu vantāṉ. itaṉāl, ivaṉi 'intiya āṭcimuṟiyiṉ tanti' eṉṟaṉar. ivaṉ koṇṭuvanta kāraṉvālicu caṭṭat tokuppu, niliyāṉa nilavarit tiṭṭam ākiyavi ciṟappākak kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa. mānilaṅkaḷil taṇṭalarkaḷi (Collector) amarttiyum, māvaṭṭanaṭuvar nayaṉmiyari amarttiyum āḷum neṟiyikkoṇṭu vantavaṉ ivaṉē, 'cutēca' maṉṉarkaḷiṉ ceytikaḷil taliyiṭāk koḷkiyi ivaṉ kiyāṇṭāṉ, āyiṉum, avarkaḷiṉ āṭcimuṟi cīr keṭṭa pōtu nalleṇṇattuṭaṉ taliyiṭṭuc cīrpaṭuttumpaṭi āḷunarkaḷik kēṭṭukkoṇṭāṉ. itaṉpaṭi ceṉṉiyāḷunar 1787 l tañcaciyaracar iṟantavuṭaṉ ēṟpaṭṭatañci ariyaṇi aracurimip pōrāṭṭattil taliyiṭṭu araṇmaṉip paṇṭitarkaḷiṉ vēta vallunarkaḷ karutturippaṭi iṟantupaṭṭa aracariṉ iḷaval

tāynilavaralāṟu 
amarciṅki nīkki makaṉmip putalvaṉ (cuvikārap puttiraṉ) carapōciyit tañcikku aracaṉākkiṉāṉ. itippōlavē ārkkāṭṭu navāpu mukamataviyiṉ pātukāppukkāka 1787, 1792 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil ceṉṉiyāḷunar pātukāppu oppanta uṭaṉpaṭikki' ceytukoṇṭu ōr āṅkilap paṭiyi ārkkāṭṭil niṟuttiṉāṉ. itaṟkāka oru kuṟippiṭṭa tokiyiyum āḷunar peṟṟāṉ. piriṭṭāṉiya nāṭāḷumaṉṟam vaṇikak kuḻuvukku vaṇikam ceyyum urimiyip putuppikkum paṭṭiyac caṭṭaṅkaḷik ki.pi.1793-liruntu toṭarntu irupatāṇṭukku orumuṟi vaḻaṅkiyatu. ovvoru muṟiyum attakiya caṭṭatti vaḻaṅkum pōtu āṭcimuṟiyilum niruvākatti lum cīrum ciṟappum vaḷarcciyum virivum ēṟpaṭa vaḻi ceyyappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, mānila āṭcimuṟiyum vaḷarntatu. 1793 ām āṇṭup paṭṭiyac caṭṭappaṭi ceṉṉi āḷunar taṉ capiyil taṉi urimiyum, mēlatikāramum peṟṟāṉ. ceṉṉiyāḷunar vaṅkāḷattilirunta talimiyāḷunarukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭu āḷa vēṇṭu meṉa ic caṭṭam valiyuṟuttiyatu. mēlum, potut (Civil) tuṟi atikārikaḷi amartta vum, amitikāval āya naṭuvarkaḷi (jarikaḷ) amarttavum, teruk kūṭṭuvōri amarttit tuppuravu ceyyavum ic caṭṭam āḷunarukku atikāramaḷittatu. tuppuravukkāka nakaril taṉiyāka varittaṇṭal ceyyappaṭṭatu. 
1873 ām āṇṭup paṭṭiyac caṭṭam kalvi, caṭṭam, nayaṉmi , varittaṇṭal ākiyavaṟṟil āḷunariṉ poṟuppukaḷi variyaṟuttatu. aṟiviyaṟ kalvikkut taṉittoki otukkiyum, kalvikkūṭaṅkaḷi mēṟpārviyiṭṭum, kalvittuṟiyi oḻuṅkupaṭuttum atikāram āḷunarukku vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. immuṟiyil kiṟittavap pātirikaḷ kalvik kūṭaṅkaḷi amikkavum, camayap parappuri ceyyavum vāyppaḷikkap peṟṟaṉar. 1833 ām āṇṭu paṭṭiyaccaṭṭam ceṉṉi maṇṭilattil nilavivanta caṭṭaṅkaḷi oḻittu, nāṭu muḻuvatiṟkum potuvākat talimiyāḷunar (naṭuvaṇ āṭci) caviyil iyaṟṟappaṭum caṭṭaṅkaḷi yēṟkumpaṭi ceytatu. āḷunar aviyiṉ uṟuppiṉar toki iraṇṭākiyatu. taṉi mānilap paṭiyiyum, paṭit talivariyum ēṟpaṭuttik koḷḷum urimi āḷunarukku vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. cāti, camaya aṭippaṭi vēṟupāṭukaḷiṉṟi aracuppaṇiyil yāvariyum amarttavum, aṭimittaṉatti oḻittu aṭimikaḷi muṉṉēṟṟavum ic caṭṭam vaḻiceytatu. curuṅkakkūṟiṉ camattuvamum, aṭippaṭi urimiyum ōraḷavu ic caṭṭattāl valiyuṟuttappaṭṭaṉa. 1853 ām āṇṭu paṭṭiyac caṭṭam naṭuvaṇ aracil mēlavi (kavuṉcil) ēṟpaṭa vaḻi ceytatu. ituvari oṟṟi maṉṟamē iruntu vantatu). ic caṭṭamaṉṟat tiṟku mānila aracukaḷiṉ paṭinikarāḷikaḷ aṉuppappaṭṭaṉar. in nikarāḷikaḷ āṇṭukku aiyāyiram poṟkācukaḷ campaḷamākap peṟṟaṉar.

ic caṭṭamaṉṟam ceyyum caṭṭaṅkaḷi mānilaṅkaḷum ēṟṟu naṭi muṟippaṭuttiṉa. paṭiñar cippāy kalakattiṟkup piṉ kuḻuvāṭci oḻikkappaṭṭu intiyā, piriṭṭāṉiya araciṉ nēraṭiyāṭcikkuḷ koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu, 
1858 ām āṇṭil koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa intiya aracuc caṭṭappaṭi ivvāṇṭu ākcaṭut tiṅkaḷiliruntu intiyā muḻuvatum iṅkilāntu nāṭṭi āṇṭa vikṭōriyāp pēraraciyiṉ nēraṭi āṭciyiṉkīḻ vantatu. talimiyāḷunar aracappaṭinikarāḷi' aracap piratiniti eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭṭāṉ. mānila āḷunarkaḷ iṅkilāntu araciyārāl amarttappaṭṭaṉar. māvaṭṭa atikārikaḷip paṇittuṟi atikārikaḷ (I.C.S. mānilatti āṇṭaṉar. 
intiyac caṭṭacavikaḷiṉ vaḷarccikku uṟutuṇiyāyiruntavi 'intiya mēlavic caṭṭaṅkaḷ' ākum. ivikipi18, 1897 ākiya āṇṭu kaḷil niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa, 1861 ām āṇṭu intiya mēlavic caṭṭappaṭi ceṉṉi āḷunar mānilattiṟkāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷi iyaṟṟum oru caṭṭamaṉṟatti ēṟpaṭuttum atikārattip peṟṟār. ataṉpaṭi nāṉkiṟkuk kuṟiyāmalum eṭṭukku mēṟpaṭāmalum uṟuppiṉari yamartti avarkaḷukkuc caṭṭamiyaṟṟum paṇiyiṉi vaḻaṅkum atikāram peṟṟār. avvāṟu ceyyappaṭum caṭṭaṅkaḷit tāṉum opputal aḷittu aracappaṭi nikarāḷiyiṉ opputaliyum peṟṟa piṉpē avaṟṟi avar tam mānilattil naṭimuṟippaṭuttavēṇṭum. ic caṭṭamaṉṟat tiṟkuc caṭṭamiyaṟṟuvatil utavi puriyat talimi vaḻakku aṟiñarum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭār. ivar mānilat talimi vaḻakkaṟiñarākavum iruntār. potukkaṭaṉ pokkacam, kācu, añcal, tolivari(tanti), camayam, patippurimi, urimip pativukaḷ mutaliyavi tavirappiṟa tuṟikaḷ mānila araciṉ kīḻ vantaṉa. putiya mānilaṅkaḷip paṭikkavum, mānilaṅ kaḷik kaṭṭuppaṭuttavum, mānilaṅkaḷukkiṭiyē ellik kōṭukaḷi uṟutippaṭuttavum, tuṇiyāḷunarkaḷi amarttavum talimiyāḷunar atikāram peṟṟār. 
1880 ām āṇṭil ceṉṉi mānila āḷunar caviyil āḷunaruṭaṉ iraṇṭu potuttuṟi atikārikaḷum ceṉṉi mānilap paṭittuṟit talivarum (Commander-in-Chict) cērttukkoḷḷap paṭṭaṉar. āṭcimuṟi nalaṉukkāka mēlum uṟuppiṉar eṇmar amart tappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl, āḷunar caviyiṉ eṇṇikki āḷunariyum cērttup paṉṉiraṇṭu āyiṟṟu. putitāka amarttappaṭṭa uṟuppiṉar eṇmaril iruvar aracu aluvalarkaḷākavum, aṟuvar aluvalarallāta varākavum iruntaṉar.

1892 ām āṇṭu intiya mēlaviccaṭṭam makkaḷāṭci muṟikku vaḻivakuttatu eṉalām. ēṉeṉil, makkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ paṭinikarāḷikaḷit tērnteṭukkum urimi peṟṟaṉar. avvāṟu tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa uṟuppiṉarkaḷum āḷunarāl mīṇṭum amarttappaṭṭa piṉpē uṟuppiṉarāyiṉar. ivarkaḷukkuk kēḷvikaḷ kēṭkum urimi vaḻaṅkap paṭṭatu. attakiya kēḷvikaḷi āṟu nāḷkaḷukku muṉpē eḻutik koṭuttuviṭa vēṇṭum. āṉāl, avaṟṟiṟku viṭiyaḷippatu āḷunariṉ viruppam. varavu, celavu, nayaṉṟikkoḷki ākiyavaṟṟip paṟṟi ivarkaḷ vaḻakkiṭalām. āṉāl, avaṟṟiṟku oppōliyiyaḷikkum urimi ivarkaḷukkilli. ic caṭṭappaṭi caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉariṉ eṇṇikki iruppatāka uyarttappaṭṭatu. talimi vaḻakkuriñaruṭaṉ cērttu irupattōr uṟuppiṉar caṭṭamaṉṟattil iṭam peṟṟaṉar. ivarkaḷil pātikkum mēṟpaṭṭa uṟuppiṉar aracu aluvalarallātār potu makkaḷ āvar. ivarkaḷ vari celuttuvōr, ūrāṇmi , nakarāṇmik kaḻakaṅkaḷ, palkalikkaḻakam, vaṇika maṉṟaṅkaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ paṭinikarāḷikaḷāvar.1895l ceṉṉip paṭittuṟiyi naṭuvaṇ āṭci ēṟṟu at tuṟiyi naṭuvaṇ tuṟiyākkik koṇṭatāl ceṉṉip paṭittuṟit talivar caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarāka muṭiya villi. ākavē, uṟuppiṉar eṇṇikki 2-liruntu 20 ākakkuṟintatu. ic camiyattil ceṉṉi mānilattil 26 māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa, ceṉṉi maṇṭil māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ

koḷvar. ākavē, tērnteṭukkappaṭṭavarkaḷiviṭa iṉṉamum caṭṭamaṉṟattil peruvārivalimiyuṭaṉ ceyalpaṭṭavarkaḷ arakaccār puṭiyavarē! (aluvalar 21 + amarttap peṟṟavar 5 = 26. eṉavē, ic caṭṭam urimi tantatēyoḻiya ataṉip payaṉpaṭutta vaḻi ceyya villi. ic caṭṭam cātivaḻip paṭinikarāṇmikku vakuppuvārip paṭinikarāḷiyam) vaḻi ceytatu. caṭṭamaṉṟattilum nakarāṭci, nakarāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷilum vakuppuvārip paṭinikarāḷiyam piratinitittuvam) kaṭip piṭikkappaṭṭatu. iculāmiyar taṅkaḷ paṭinikarāḷikaḷit tāṅkaḷē tērnteṭuttuk koṇṭaṉar. ceṉṉic caṭṭacavikkut tērn teṭukkappaṭṭa motta caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉar irupattoru pēril muculīmkaḷ iruvar: 'jamīṉtār' iruvar; perunilakkiḻār mūvar; ceṉṉi mānakarāṭciyiṉ paṭinikarāḷi oruvar; vaṇikarmaṉṟap paṭinikarāḷi oruvar; uḻavar kaḻakap paṭinikarāḷi oruvar'. mītiyirunta patiṉoru pērum nakarāṇmikkaḻakaṅkaḷ, māvaṭṭakkaḻakaṅkaḷ, vaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷ, oṉṟiyaṅkaḷ ākiya uḷḷāṭci amippukaḷiṉ paṭinikarāḷi caḷākat tērnteṭukkappeṟṟārkaḷ. 
uṟuppiṉarkaḷ pala uṭkuḻukkaḷākap (Sub-Corrmittees) pirittu, palatarappaṭṭa ceytikaḷiyum uṟaḻntu vivātittu, āyntu muṭi veṭuttaṉar. ettakiya kēḷvikaḷiyum kēṭkum urimiyiṉiyum, caṭṭa varivukaḷik macōtā koṇṭuvarum urimiyiṉiyum peṟṟaṉar. āka, mutaṉmuṟiyākac caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarukku ittakiya urimikaḷ ic caṭṭattāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉavāyiṉum ivaṟṟi āḷunar taḷḷiviṭṭāl avarik kaṭṭuppaṭuttum urimi uṟuppiṉarukkilli." āḷunariṉ ceyaṟkuḻuvil mutaṉmuṟiyāka ōr intiyar amarttap peṟṟār. avar irācārāmarāyaḷiṅkar eṉpavar āvār. ivari makkaḷ curukkamākap paṉakal aracar eṉṟē aṟivar. ivarit toṭarntu ip patavi peṟṟōr vayavar pi.ecucīṉuvāciyar, vayavar pi.irācakōpālāc cārimutaliyōr āvar. 
1909 ām āṇṭu aracuc caṭṭam uṇmiyāṉa paṭinikarāḷiyatti vaḻaṅkavilli. paṭinikarāḷiyam peṟṟavarkaḷum makkaḷukkāka muyaṉṟu ceyalpaṭa iyalavilli. eṉavē, itaṉi yāvarum veṟuttaṉar. iculāmiyar taṉip paṭinikarāḷiyam peṟṟatāl 'intiyā' eṉṉum uṇarvukuṉṟi intu, muculīm eṉṉum iraṇṭupaṭṭa uṇarvukaḷ vaḷarntaṉa. intiyar taṉṉāṭcikkut takutiyaṟṟavar eṉa miṇṭōvum mārliyum veḷippaṭiyākavē kūṟiṉar. āḷunarum avaruṭiya caviyi lirunta uṟuppiṉarum mottam nālvar) ceyaṟkuḻu eṉṟa peyaril ellāp paṇikaḷiyum ceytaṉar. ākavē, uṟuppiṉarkaḷ peyarukkē caṭṭamaṉṟattil iruntaṉar,

āṇṭu kāntiyaṭikaḷ teṉṉāppirikkāviliruntu intiyāvukkut tirumpi intiya araciyalil aṭiyeṭuttu vitta piṉṉar pērāya (kāṅkiracuk kaṭcikku putiya polivum valiyum ēṟpaṭṭatu. kaṭciyiṉ vaḷarcciyik kaṇṭa āṅkilar ataṉ kōrikkikaḷiyēṟṟu II-l 'māṇṭēku cemcupōrṭu cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ' eṉṟa peyaril 1919 ām āṇṭu aracuc caṭṭattik koṇṭu vantaṉar. itaṉpaṭi, caṭṭamaṉṟam virivupaṭuttappaṭṭu mottam 32 uṟuppiṉar itil iṭam peṟṟaṉar. ivarkaḷil 98 pēr makkaḷāl tērnteṭukkap peṟṟaṉar, 23 pēr āḷunarāl amarttap peṟṟaṉar. mīti 11pēr aluvalar tērnteṭukkap peṟṟa caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉaril mūvari āḷunar tam karutturikkuḻuvil cērttukkoṇṭāṉ. inta mūvarum āṅkilareṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. taṉatu ceyaṟkuḻuvil maṟṟumōr intiyaric cērttukkoṇṭāṉ, anta intiyar vayavar. mukamatu apipullā cāyapu āvār. ivarukkup piṉ ip pataviyiliruntavarkaḷ vayavar kē.cīṉuvāṉa cāyaṅkār, vayavar ci.pi.irāmacāmi ayyar, vayavar. mukamatu ucumāṉ cāyapu, vayavar. em.kiruṭṭiṇaṉ nāyar mutaliyōrāvar. iraṭṭiyāṭci 
1919 ām āṇṭuc caṭṭattāl ceṉṉi maṇṭalattil iraṭṭi yāṭci muṟi ēṟpaṭṭatu. āḷunariṉ karutturikkuḻuvil avarāl amarttappaṭṭa uṟuppiṉar orupuṟamum, makkaḷāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa uṟuppiṉarkaḷil āḷunarāl amarttappaṭṭa amiccarkaḷ oru puṟamum niruvākap poṟuppi iraṇṭākap pirittāṇṭaṉar. āṉāl, āḷunarē araciṉ talivaṉāka iruntāṉ. karutturik kuḻuviṉariṭam nayaṉmi , (nīti), ciṟi, kāval, pokkacam(niti), nīrppācaṉam, varittaṇṭal, kāṭu, pañca nīkkam, toḻiṟcālikaḷ mutaliya poṟuppāṉa tuṟikaḷ oppaṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ it tuṟikaḷi avarkaḷē taṅkaḷukkeṉa niṟuttikkoṇṭaṉar. ivi makkaḷ paṭinikarāḷiyarkku amiccarkaḷukku māṟṟamuṭiyāta tuṟikaḷāyiṉa. eṉavē, ivaṟṟi *niṟuttikkoṇṭa tuṟikaḷ' allatu otukkik koḷḷappaṭṭa tuṟikaḷ (Reserved Subjects)eṉṟum, amiccarkaḷukku māṟṟappaṭāta tuṟikaḷ allatu otukkappaṭāta tuṟikaḷ (Non-Transferred Subjects) eṉṟum aḻittaṉar. makkaḷāl tērnteṭukkappeṟṟa intiya uṟuppiṉarkaḷil oru cilari āḷunar amiccarkaḷāka amartti avarkaḷiṭam cila tuṟikaḷi oppaṭittāṉ. avarkaḷukku ūrāṭci, kalvi (airōppiyar kalvi nīṅkalāka) potunalam, tuppuravu, maruttuvam, potuppaṇi, vēḷāṇmi, cuṅkam, kūṭṭuṟavuc caṅkaṅkaḷ, toḻil vaḷarcci mutaliya tuṟikaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivi māṟṟappaṭṭa tuṟikaḷ (Transferred Subjects) eṉappaṭṭaṉa. āḷunarāl ivarkaḷukku ivi otukkappaṭṭaṉa.

mutaliyāri nīkki ivariyē karutturiccavi mēlaviyil cērttatu, nayaṉmik kaṭcikku naṉmi pukaṭṭi em.ci.irācā uṟuppiṉarāṉār. piṉṉar amiccarākavum ceyalpaṭṭār. caṭṭamaṉṟattilum tāḻttappaṭṭōr aivar iṭam peṟṟaṉar. 
1923-l naṭanta potut tērtalil nayaṉmik kaṭciyē amic caraviya mittatu. itilum paṉakal aracarē mutalamiccar āṉār. ēvi. pātrē ṭieṉ. civañāṉam (piḷḷi ) mutaliyōr amiccarkaḷāyiṉar. āḷunar tāḻttappaṭṭōrukku atika iṭaṅkaḷi otukkiṉār. itaṉāl oṉpatu tāḻttappaṭṭa uṟuppiṉarkaḷ caṭṭamaṉṟattil iṭampeṟṟaṉar. inta amiccaravi 1926vari ceyalpaṭṭatu. iccamiyattil tāḻttap paṭṭōr nalaṉ toḻilāḷar nalattuṟi' (Labour Welfare Department) yāl kavaṉikkappaṭṭatu. at tuṟiyi yoḻittu, ataṟkāṉa māṉiyaṅ kaḷiveṭṭit tāḻttappaṭṭōrukku iṭukkaṇ ceyyum paṇiyil ik kaṭciyum iṟaṅkiyatu. itaṉiyetirttu em.ci. irācā pōrk kural eḻuppiṉār, 1923, cūli 21, 22 ām pakkalkaḷil kōvilpaṭṭiyil 'teṉṉintiya ātittirāviṭar iraṇṭām mānāṭu' oṉṟik kūṭṭi tam iṉattukku vanta kēṭukaḷi viḷakkiṉār. aracu āvaṇaṅkaḷil "paṟiyar' eṉṟirunta collimāṟṟi "atittirāviṭar' eṉṟu tirutti eḻutac ceytār. atu āṇiyāka veḷiyiṭap peṟavum ēṟpāṭu ceytār. pārppaṉarkaḷiṭam cama urimi kōrum pārppaṉarallātār tammi yotta intu tutittirāviṭarkaḷukkuc camarṭariṉamkoṭukka maṟuppatik kaṇṭittār. 
nayaṉmikkaṭci 1920iliruntu 1926vari āṟāṇṭukaḷ ceṉṉi mānilattiyāṇṭatu. itil aintu āṇṭukaḷ paṉakal aracar mutalamicca rāka iruntār. inta āṇṭukaḷil ivvamiccaraviyāl paṉṉiraṇṭu caṭṭaṅkaḷi niṟivēṟṟa muṭintatu. avaṟṟuḷ perumpālāṉavi ōṉarāṭcik kaḻakaṅkaḷ, toṭakkakkalvi paṟṟariyaviyākum. 1923-l ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakac caṭṭamum, 1925l āntirap palkalikkaḻakam caṭṭamum niṟivēṟiṉa. 1923-l koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa maṟṟoru caṭṭam intu camaya aṟaniliyac caṭṭamākum. itaṉpaṭi intu aṟa niliyaṅ cuḷip pātukākka oru kuḻu amikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ talivarāka vayavar, ṭicatāciva ayyar eṉpār amarttappaṭṭār. 
1926-l naṭanta potuttērtalil nayaṉmik kaṭci tōlviyuṟṟatu. pērāyakkaṭci (kāṅkiracu) veṟṟi peṟṟatu. āyiṉum, kaṭci cārpaṟṟa par, cupparāyaṉ mutalamiccar āṉār. ivaruṭiya amiccaraviyil ārōkkiyacāmi mutaliyār, araṅkanātarīmutaliyār ākiyōr iṭam peṟṟaṉar. tāḻttappaṭṭōrukku inta amiccaravi pala calukikaḷ ceytatu. pattut tāḻttappaṭṭa uṟuppiṉar caṭṭamaṉṟattil iṭam peṟṟaṉar. vēlcu iḷavaracar ceṉṉi vantapōtu par, cupparāyaṉ amiccaravi avarukku varavēṟpaḷittatu. 1919 ām āṇṭuc caṭṭattiṉ

paṭi aminta iraṭṭiyāṭci muṟiyi etirttu, mēlum viṭutali urimikaḷik kōri kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ talimiyil kiḷarcci ceytuvanta pērāyak kaṭci iḷavaracar vantapōtu etirppik kāṭṭippalakalavaraṅ kaḷic ceytatu. ākavē, taṅkaḷ kaṭciyil iṭam peṟṟa cuppārāyaṉ taṅkaḷiṉ kōṭpāṭṭukku etirāka iḷavaracari varavēṟṟataṟkup pērāyak kaṭci amiccaravikkut tollikaḷ koṭuttatu. 1928-l pērāyak kaṭci amiccarkaḷāṉa ārōkkiyacāmi mutaliyārum, araṅkanāta fmutaliyārum kaṭcik kaṭṭupāṭṭiṟkiṇaṅka amiccar pataviyiliruntu nīṅkiṉar. maṉantaḷarāta par. cupparāyaṉ paṉakal aracariṉ aṟivurippaṭi muttiyā mutaliyār, ceturām (ayyar ākiyōri amiccarka ḷākkit tiṟampaṭa āṇṭār. 
par. cupparāyaṉ amiccaravi cāti aṭippaṭikkēṟpap paṇiyā ḷarkaḷi aracup paṇiyil amarttappaṭum aracāṇi (CornumunalG.pi.) oṉṟi niṟivēṟṟiyatu. nayaṉmik kaṭci ceyyat tavaṟiyatic ceyya muṭiyātatiyellām ivar ceytu kāṭṭiṉār. pārppaṉarallātār iv vāṇiyāl perum payaṉ peṟṟaṉar. tāḻttappaṭṭōrukkum 'camanīti kiṭittatu. 1928-l aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakac caṭṭam niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭatu. 1978l aracup paṇikaḷukkup potuttērvu mūlam aluvalarkaḷit tērnteṭukkum ceṉṉit tērvāṇik kaḻakac caṭṭam niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭatu. itē āṇṭil ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakat tiruttac caṭṭam, kāval tuṟic caṭṭam mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉa. 
itaṟkiṭiyil intiya araciyali maṟuvoḻuṅku paricīlaṉi ceytu araciyal caṭṭattip paṭikka cimaṉ āṇikkuḻu 1929-l vantatu. itaṉi etirttut tamiḻakattil cakkaravartti irācakōpālāc cāriyār talimiyil maṟikkāṭṭil vētāraṇyattil taṭiyi mīṟi uppuk kāyccutal pōṉṟa etirppukaḷil īṭupaṭṭaṉar. irācakōpālāc cāriyārum, kāntiyaṭikaḷum uṭpaṭa pala ilakkam pēr ciṟippaṭṭaṉar, vaṭṭa mēci mānāṭukaḷ tōlviyuṟṟaṉa. kaṭiciyāka 1935 ām āṇṭu intiya aracuc caṭṭam vantatu. 
ivvāṟu nāṭṭil muṭukkamāka viṭutalip pōr naṭantu koṇ ṭirukkumpōtu 1930 ām āṇṭup potut tērtalum vantatu. itil nayaṉmik kaṭci ciṟupāṉmi valimi peṟṟu veṟṟi peṟṟatu. pērāyakkaṭci veḷḷiyaṉi veḷiyēṟṟum paṇiyil muḻumūccuṭaṉ īṭupaṭṭiruntatāl tērtalippaṟṟi atu cintikkavilli, eṉavē, ciṟupāṉmiyiṉarāṉa nayaṉmik kaṭciyiṉar amiccaraviyi amikkum vāyppip peṟṟaṉar. inta amiccaraviyil pi.muṉucāmi (nāyuṭu mutalamiccarākavum, piṭi irācaṉ, ecu, kumāracāmi reṭṭiyār

puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭaṉar. oru vaṭṭak kaḻakattil tāḻttappaṭṭavarukku iṭam koṭuttatālēyē aluvalakak kaṭṭaṭatti vāṭakikku viṭṭiruntavar ataṉiviṭa maṟuttuviṭṭār. pērāyak kaṭci tīṇṭāmiyoḻippu, matuvilakkiṟ kākak kural eḻuppiyatē oḻiya cātiyoḻippukku evvaki muyaṟciyum mēṟkoḷḷavilli. aṅkum cātiveṟiyum, tīṇṭāmiyum tāṇṭava. māṭiṉa. ivarkaḷ amitta pāḻ āciramamyil pārppaṉarallā tārukkeṉat taṉiyē kuṭinīrp pāṉi vikkappaṭṭatu. itaṉi yetirttu aṉṟiya tamiḻnāṭu pērāyakkaṭciyiṉ talivarāyirunta tanti periyār kaṭciyi viṭṭē (1975l veḷiyēṟiṉāreṉil ik kālac cātiveṟiyip paṟṟi iṉi viḷakkattēviyilli. itaṉāltāṉ 1930-32-l naṭanta ilaṇṭaṉ vaṭṭamēci mānāṭṭil pēraṟiñar par.pi. ār. ampētkār tīṇṭāmiyiṉ koṭumikaḷik kēṭṭu ulakamē viyakkum vaṇṇam, aṟiyum vaṇṇam viḷakkiṉār. itil tamiḻakatti liruntu arāva aṇṇal irāvpakatarar iraṭṭimali cīṉivācaṉum kalantu koṇṭār. itaṉpiṟaku 1933-l irāmcē makataṉālṭu tāḻttap paṭṭōrukkut taṉittokutiyaḷikkum vakuppurimit tīrvi (Corrithu nal Award) aṟivittār. itaṉiyetirtta kāntiyaṭikaḷ intukkaḷit 'tīṇṭuvār-tīṇṭātār' eṉṟu iraṇṭākap pirikkat tāṉ ottuk koḷḷa muṭiyāteṉṟu kūṟic cākum vari uṇṇānōṉpirukkat toṭaṅkiṉār. appoḻutu avar pūṉāviluḷḷa eravāṭā ciṟiyil iruntār. kaṭiciyāka avarukkum tāḻttappaṭṭōriṉ talivarukkum iṭiyē ēṟpaṭṭa 'pūṉā oppantam' niṟivēṟiyatu. taṉit tokuti kiviṭappaṭṭatu. tāḻttappaṭṭōrukkuk kalvi, aracuppaṇi, caṭṭa maṉṟaṅkaḷ, nāṭāḷumaṉṟaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil vikitācārappaṭi iṭaṅkaḷ otukkavum avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkeṉat taṉittuṟi ēṟpaṭuttip pāṭupaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟum muṭivāyiṟṟu.

kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ talimiyil toṭarntu naṭanta pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷukkum, cimaṉ āṇikkuḻuvukkum, vaṭṭamēci mānāṭukaḷukkum, maktaṉālṭu aṟikkikkum, pūṉā oppantattiṟkum piṉṉar, 1935ām āṇṭu intiya aracuccaṭṭam koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. itaṉ kūṟupāṭukaḷiṉpaṭi ceṉṉi mānilattil iru caṭṭacavikaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa. iraṭṭi āṭcimuṟi oḻikkappaṭṭatu. naṭuvaṇ āṭci kūṭṭāṭci muṟiyil iyaṅka vaḻiceytatu. intiyāvil patiṉoru mānilaṅkaḷum, āṇi yāḷar āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭa āṟu pakutikaḷum kūṭṭāṭciyil aṭaṅkiṉa. ivaṟṟittavira 567 ciṟṟaracukaḷ iruntaṉa. cutēca camastāṉaṅkaḷ, periya mānilaṅkaḷ iraṭṭic caṭṭamaṉṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉ taṉic ceyalkaḷil taṉittum potuc ceyalkaḷil naṭuvaṇ āṭciyuṭaṉ iṇintum ceyalpaṭum kūṭṭāṭci muṟi ēṟpaṭṭatu. caṭṭamaṉṟamum ataṉāl amikkappaṭum amiccaraviyumē ellāp poṟuppukaḷiyum ēṟṟaṉa, āḷunar 
1919 ām āṇṭuc caṭṭattiṉpaṭi āḷunarē mānilat taliva rākavum, amiccarkaḷi amarttupavarākavum, taṉiyatikāram peṟṟava rākavum, taṉit tuṟikaḷip peṟṟavarākavum ceyalpaṭṭār. āṉāl, 1935 ām āṇṭuc caṭṭattāl iraṭṭiyāṭcimuṟi oḻikkappaṭṭu amiccaraviyē āṭcip poṟuppuḷḷatāka māṟiyatu. āyiṉum, āḷunarukkut taṉiyatikāraṅkaḷ iruntaṉa, naṭuvaṇ āṭciyil aracap paṭinikarāḷipeṟṟirunta taṉiyatikāraṅkaḷ aṉittiyum ivar mānilac caṭṭacavikaḷil peṟṟiruntār. peruvāriyāka veṟṟipeṟṟa kaṭciyē amiccaraviyi amikkumāyiṉum āḷunarē amiccarkaḷi amarttuvār. kuṟippāka, ivar ciṟupāṉmiyiṉariṉ urimikaḷukkuk kāppāḷarāka iruntār. tāmē taḷiyāḷ oruvari amiccarāka amarttum atikāramum avarukkuṇṭu, āyiṉum, avvāṟu amarttap paṭṭavar āṟu tiṅkaḷukkuḷ tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa uṟuppiṉarākiviṭa vēṇṭum. āḷunar caṭṭamaṉṟam, mēlavi iraṇṭiṟkum vikitāc cārappaṭi uṟuppiṉarkaḷi amarttuvār. nāṭṭiṉ pātukāppu, caṭṭam, oḻuṅku, amiti ivaṟṟik karuti maṉṟattik kalittut tāmē āḷum atikāramum ivarukkuṇṭu. ataṟkumuṉ ivar aracap paṭinikarāḷiyiṉ opputalip peṟavēṇṭum. kalitta āṟu tiṅkaḷukkuḷ tērtali naṭatta vēṇṭum; allatu mīṇṭum aracap paṭinikarāḷiyiṉ opputal peṟṟu mēlum āṟu tiṅkaḷ vari ivarē āḷalām. piraṭṭāṉiya nāṭāḷum maṉṟattiṭam, opputal peṟṟu āḷunarē mūṉṟāṇṭukaḷvari caṭṭamaṉṟa miṉṟiyē āḷavum ic caṭṭam vaḻi ceytatu. āṉāl, poṟuppuḷḷa amiccaraviyāl āṭci naṭattap peṟṟatāl āḷunar ittakiya urimikaḷi muḻuvatumākap payaṉpaṭuttavilli.

mēlavi (Legislative Council) 
1935 ām āṇṭu aracuc caṭṭappaṭipampāy, oṉṟiya mānilaṅkaḷ, (United Provinces), pīkār, acām, vaṅkāḷam ākiya mānilaṅkaḷil ēṟpaṭṭatip pōlavē ceṉṉi maṇṭilattilum mēlavi, kīḻavi eṉṟa iru caṭṭamaṉṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. inta mēlaviyil tērn teṭukkappeṟṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷum, amartta uṟuppiṉarcuḷum iṭam peṟṟaṉar. ic caviyil makkaḷ tokikkēṟpa mottam 5 iṭaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. itil muppattintu iṭam potuvākavum, pattu iṭam tāḻttappaṭṭavarukkum, ēḻu iṭam iculāmiyarukkum, mūṉṟu iṭam intiyak kiṟittuvarukkum, ōriṭam airōppiyarukkum otukkappaṭṭaṉa, mītiyuḷḷa pattu iṭaṅkaḷil āḷunariṉ amartta uṟuppiṉar iṭam peṟuvar. inta mēlavi niliyāṉatu. itil mūṉṟāṇṭukkorumuṟi mūṉṟiloru pakuti uṟuppiṉar vilakuvar. avarkaḷiṉ iṭaṅkaḷit takunta uṟuppiṉar nirappuvar. kīḻaviyil koṇṭuvarappaṭum ovvoru caṭṭa varivum ic caviyil uṟaḻntu vivātittu opputal peṟṟa piṉpē caṭṭamākkappaṭum. kaṭiciyāka āḷunariṉ opputalum, aracap paṭi nikarāḷiyiṉ opputalum peṟṟāka vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum niṉivukūrtal vēṇṭum. caṭṭamaṉṟam (alla tu) kīḻavi (Legislative Assembly) 
itil mottam 315 uṟuppiṉarkaḷ iṭam peṟṟaṉar. pūṉā oppantattiṉpaṭi itilum tāḻttappaṭṭōrukku 30 iṭaṅkaḷ otukkap paṭṭaṉa. mītiyil mali vāṇarukku ōriṭamum, iculāmiyarukku irupat teṭṭu iṭaṅkaḷum, āṅkilēyarukku īriṭaṅkaḷum, airōppiyarukku mūṉṟu iṭaṅkaḷum intiyak kiṟittavarukku eṭṭu iṭaṅkaḷum, toḻil vaṇika niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷukku āṟu iṭaṅkaḷum, perunilakkiḻārkaḷukku āṟu iṭaṅkaḷum, toḻilāḷikaḷukku āṟu iṭaṅkaḷum, peṇkaḷukku eṭṭu iṭaṅkaḷum, palkalikkaḻakattiṟku ōriṭamum otukkappaṭṭaṉa. peṇkaḷukku otukkappaṭṭa eṭṭu iṭaṅkaḷil oṉṟu kiṟittavap peṇṇukkum, oṉṟu iculāmiyap peṇṇukkum cērum. mītiyuḷḷa 116 iṭaṅkaḷ potu iṭaṅkaḷākavum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. itilum amartta uṟuppiṉar uṇṭu. 
ic caṭṭamaṉṟam aintāṇṭukaḷ pataviyil irukkum. muṉpē kūṟiyapaṭi iṭiyil caṭṭam, oḻuṅku, amiti, nāṭṭiṉ pātukāppu mutaliyavaṟṟik karuti āḷunarāl itu kalikkappaṭalām. uṟuppi ṉarkaḷ kēḷvikaḷ kēṭkavum caṭṭa varivukaḷik koṇṭuvaravum urimi peṟṟaṉar.

1936-l amikkappeṟṟa amiccaraviyil poppiliyaracar mutalvarākavum, pi.ṭi irācaṉ, ecu. kumāracāmi reṭṭiyār ākiyōr amiccarkaḷākavum pataviyēṟṟaṉar. inta amiccaraviyil nāṉku tiṅkaḷ piṭi irācaṉ mutalamiccarākavum ceyalpaṭṭār. piṉṉar inta amiccaraviyil em,ē, muttiyā ceṭṭiyār, ē.ṭi paṉṉīrcelvam, pi, kalipullā cākippakatūr, mayilic ciṉṉattampi irācā (em. ci. irācu), ār.em. pālaṭ ākiyōr amiccarkaḷ āyiṉar. amiccariṉ eṇṇikki itil atikamiruppati yaṟiyalām. tērtalil tōṟṟup patavi iḻantatu. ituvē nayaṉmik kaṭciyiṉ kaṭici amiccaravi.

rākak koṇṭa amiccaravi amikkappeṟṟatu. itil peyar peṟṟa patiṉmar amiccarkaḷ āyiṉar. avarkaḷ ṭi. pirakācam, yākum ācaṉ, par.pi. cupparāyaṉ, par, ṭi. ecu, ecu. irācaṉ, vi. ai. muṉucāmi piḷḷi ! vi.vi. kiri, ecu. irāmaṉ mēṉaṉ, pi. kōpāl reṭṭi, ci.cē. varkicu ākiyōr āvar. vi. ai. muṉucāmippiḷḷi tāḻttappaṭṭōr paṭinikarāḷiyāka uṟuppāṇmiyēṟṟār. inta amiccaravi aminta kālamum, cūḻalum ciṟappāṉatu, makkaḷ nāṭṭuppaṟṟil taṅkaḷi īṭupaṭuttikkoṇṭa kālattil, uyar atikārikaḷum, amiccarkaḷum ottuḻitta kālattil, makkaḷ paṇiyē makēcaṉ' paṇiyeṉa amiccar kaḷum, atikārikaḷum eṇṇic ceyalpaṭṭa kālattil irācakōpā lāccāriyār amiccaravi amittār. itaṉāl inta amiccaravi yil niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭa caṭṭaṅkaḷ yāvum kaṭavuḷ' kaṭṭaḷikaḷākap pōṟṟik kaṭippiṭikkap peṟṟaṉa. matuvilakkuc caṭṭam naṭimuṟi yākkappaṭṭatu: kuṭivārac caṭṭam koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu. ivaṟṟāl eṇṇiṟanta kuṭumpaṅkaḷ kuṭiyilum kaṭaṉilumiruntu mīṭkappeṟṟaṉa. 
āyiṉum, irācakōpālāccāriyār amiccaravi kaṭiciyil tamiḻarkaḷiṉ maṉakkacappip peṟṟatu. ataṟkuk karaṇiyam avar koṇṭuvanta kaṭṭāya intittiṇippēyākum. paḷḷikaḷilum itu kaṭṭāyap pāṭamākkap peṟṟatu. itaṉiyetirttut tamiḻnāṭu muḻuvatum celvaraṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.ī. vē. irāmacāmip periyār talimi yil 1938-l pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa toṇṭarkaḷ intiyiyetirttuc ciṟipukuntaṉar. tanti periyār tamiḻ nāṭu tamiḻarukkē' eṉa. muḻaṅkiṉār. intiyetirppil aṉṉi alumēlu appāturi, aṉṉi mīṉāmpāḷ civarācu, taḷapaticieṉ aṇṇāturi pēraṟiñar aṇṇā mutaliya eṇṇāyiram pēr paṅkēṟṟaṉar. paḷḷikaḷiṉ vāyil kaḷil niṉṟu maṟiyal ceytum, inti nūlkaḷiyerittum pōrāṭic ciṟi pukuntaṉar. ic camayattil nayaṉmik kaṭci tanti periyārit talivarāka ēṟṟatāl tanti periyār ciṟiyiliruntapōtum eṇṇat toliyāttamiḻarkaḷ intiyi ōyātu etirttup pōrāṭiṉar.tamiḻp pēraṟiñar maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ, catāṉantar, (īḻattaṭikaḷ) nāvalar cōma cuntara pāratiyār, tiru.vi.kaliyāṇacuntaraṉār ñā.tēvanēyap pāvāṇar mutaliya aṟiñar perumakkaḷum itil paṅkēṟṟaṉar. ivvāṟu tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷiṭiyēyum inti etirppu uṇarvu poṅkiyatu. irāca kōpālāccāriyār vēṟu vaḻiyiṉṟik kaṭṭāya intik kalviyi oḻittār. itip pōlavē kammāḷarkaḷ taṅkaḷi 'āccārikaḷ', eṉak kūṟāmal 'āsārikaḷ' eṉak kūṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟu irācakōpālac cāriyār koṇṭuvanta caṭṭamum etirppukku īṭukoṭukka muṭiyāmal oḻintatu. ceṉṉi mākāṇam eṉpatu tamiḻakam, āntiram, kēraḷattil iru māvaṭṭaṅkaḷ, teṉkaṉṉaṭam ākiyavaṟṟik koṇṭu tikaḻntatu. āṉāl, nāṭu viṭutaliyaṭintapiṉ makkaḷukku moḻivaḻi, iṉavaḻi uṇarvukaḷ perukiṉa. itil mikavum uṇarccivayappaṭṭa āntira makkaḷ 'vicāla āntiram kēṭṭuk kiḷarccikaḷ ceytaṉar. poṭṭi cīrāmulu fnāyuṭu) eṉpār taṉi mānilam kōri uṇṇānōṉpu nōṟṟu uyir nīttār. iccamayattil ci.irācakōpālāccāriyār talimiyi lāṉa amiccaravi ceṉṉi mānilatti yāṇṭatu. itu 1-4-1952 mutal 13.4.1954 vari pataviyil iruntatu. em. paktavaccalam, ci. cuppira maṇiyam, aṉṉi cōtiveṅkaṭācalam mutaliya patiṉēḻu amic carkaḷ itil iruntaṉar. āntirattit taṉi mānilamākka inta aviyum, intiya aracum oppukkoṇṭaṉa. itaṉpaṭi āntiram 53 ām āṇṭu taṇi mānilamāṉatu. 
āntiram taṉi mānilamāṉati eṭuttukkāṭṭākak koṇṭu moḻivaḻi mānilaṅkaḷ kōri nāṭṭil āṅkāṅkē kiḷarccikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. par. pi. ār. ampētkār moḻivaḻi mānilaṅkaḷit tōṟṟuvippatōṭu intiyāvukkut teṟkilum oru talinakari amikkavēṇṭumeṉa acikka muṭiyāta aṟiviyal uṟaḻiyaṅkaḷi (vātaṅkaḷi muṉvittup pēciṉār. tamiḻakattil taṉi nāṭu kōrum kiḷarccikaḷ eḻuntaṉa.1953-l pacalali talimiyil amikkappeṟṟa kuḻu moḻi ellikaḷik kuṟittatu. itaṟkuḷ caṅkaraliṅka nāṭār eṉṉum tamiḻar taṉit tamiḻnāṭu kōri uṇṇānōṉpu nōṟṟu uyir tuṟantār. 1956 ām āṇṭuc caṭṭappaṭi kēraḷam taṉi mānilamākkappeṟṟatu. āka, āntiramum, kēraḷamum taṉi mānilaṅkaḷāṉa piṉ tamiḻakam parappilum elliyilum curuṅkiviṭṭatu. itu moḻivaḻiyākat tamiḻakam' eṉa aḻikkap peṟṟatu. 1949-l tirāviṭar kaḻakattiliruntu pirintu aṟiñar

aṇṇā talimiyil tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam' uruvāṉatu. ik cuṭci, nammiṭamiruntu pirintu ceṉṟa āntiramum, kēraḷamum moḻi vaḻiyē amintu aḻikkappaṭumpōtu 'tamiḻakam" eṉa aḻikkap peṟāmal ceṉṉi mākāṇam' eṉa aḻikkappaṭuvati etirttup pōrāṭiyatu. kiḷarccikkup piṉ akattīcuvaram, tōvāḷi, kalkuḷam, viḷavaṅkōṭu, ceṅkōṭṭiyiṉ perum pakutiyum ceṉṉiyuṭaṉ iṇikkappeṟṟut tamiḻ mānilamāṉatu. 
1957-l naṭanta viṭutali intiyāvil iraṇṭāvatu potut tērtalilum pērāyak kaṭciyē veṟṟi peṟṟatu. kēraḷamum, āntiramum taṉi mānilaṅkaḷāṉapiṉ tamiḻakam ciṟuttuviṭṭatu. itaṉāl, amic caraviyum curuṅkiviṭṭatu. itil.ku. kāmarācar mutalamiccarākavum, em. paktavaccalam, ci. cuppiramaṇiyam mutaliya eḻuvar amiccarkaḷākavum patavi ēṟṟaṉar. tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam toṭarntu intiyiyetirt tum, ceṉṉi mānilattit 'tamiḻnāṭu' eṉṟu māṟṟumpaṭiyum pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷ naṭattiyatu. 1957l ceṉṉi mānilam tamiḻnāṭu' eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭum eṉṟum, āṅkilattil "ceṉṉi' eṉak kuṟippiṭavēṇṭum meṉṟum kāmarācar amiccaravi tīrmāṉittatu. paḷḷi iṟutip paṭippuvari ilavacak kalviyum, ēḻi māṇavarukku matiya uṇavuttiṭṭa, mum koṇṭu vantu kāmarācar tamiḻariṉ uḷḷak kōyilil iṭam peṟṟār. 
1967-l naṭanta potut tērtalilum pērāyak kaṭciyē veṟṟi peṟṟatu. kāmarācarē ceṉṉi mānila mutalvar āṉār. em. pakta vaccalam, ci. cuppiramaṇiyam mutaliya eḻuvar amiccaraviyil iṭam peṟṟaṉar. 1955-l cēlam, tarumapuri eṉa iru māvaṭṭaṅkaḷākap pirikkap peṟṟatu. cuṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭamum tōṉṟiyatu. kocciyiliruntu tiruvaṉantapuram pirikkappeṟṟatu. 1963-l kāmarācar mūtta uṟuppiṉar, cuṭcikkup paṇiyāṟṟap patavi tuṟakkum taṉ kōṭpāṭṭiṉpaṭi tāmē patavi tuṟantār. ākavē, 1963 mutal 16 vari em. paktavaccalam mutalamiccar āṉār, taṉatu tirucci mānāṭṭu muṭiviṉpaṭi it tērtalil īṭupaṭṭatirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟakkaḻakam oru cila iṭaṅkaḷip piṭittatu. 
1967-l naṭanta potut tērtalil tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam perumpāṉmiyāṉa iṭaṅkaḷip peṟṟu aṟiñar aṇṇā talimi yil amiccaravi amittatu. irā. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ, mu. karuṇāniti mutaliya oṉpatu amiccarkaḷ itil iṭam peṟṟaṉar, aṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ talimiyilāṉa tirāviṭa muṉṟēṟṟak kaḻakattiṉ inta amiccaravi makkaḷ, kaṭcikaḷ, atikārikaḷiṉ ottuḻippik kōrip peṟṟup pala putiya cīrtiruttaṅkaḷik koṇṭuvantatu. ceṉṉi mākāṇam tamiḻnāṭu' eṉṟu māṟṟappaṭum caṭṭattiyum, intiyi aṭiyōṭu nīkkit tamiḻē mānilam muḻuvatum kaṟkum kaṟpikkum āḷum 
kipi 19, 20 ām nāṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tamiḻakam 
203 
moḻiyākavum, āṅkilam tuṇi moḻiyākavum irukkum caṭṭatti yum koṇṭu vantatu. 1957-l ceṉṉi mākāṇam 'tamiḻnāṭu' eṉa aṟiyappaṭum tīrmāṉattik kāmarācar amiccaravik koṇṭuvantatu. āṉālum 1967-l aṇṇā amiccaravitāṉ caṭṭappaṭi tamiḻnāṭu eṉṟu māṟṟiyatu. 1958 mutalē tamiḻ āṭcimoḻiyākkappaṭṭatāyi ṉum aṇṇāviṉ amiccaraviyē caṭṭappaṭi ataṟku mukāmi iṭattiyum virivākkattiyum aḷittatu. inti aṭiyōṭu akaṟṟap paṭṭu, inti āciriyarkaḷ vēṟu paṇikaḷil amarttap peṟṟaṉar. 1963 54 ām āṇṭu mutalē palkalikkaḻakap paṭṭappaṭippil cila pāṭaṅkaḷ tamiḻil kaṟṟukkoṭukkap peṟṟaṉa. āyiṉum, itu caṭṭappaṭiyum, paṭippaṭiyākavum koṇṭuvarappaṭṭu iṉṟu kallūrikaḷil tamiḻ pāṭamoḻiyākiviṭṭatu. intuc camaya vitikaḷiṉpaṭi ceyyappaṭṭa tirumaṇaṅkaḷ tavira piṟa tirumaṇaṅkaḷ cellupaṭiyākāveṉṟa nilimi māṟiperiyāriṉ taṉmāṉa (cuyamariyātit tirumaṇaṅkaḷum cellupaṭi yākum caṭṭam koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu. tāḻttappaṭṭa āṇ allatu peṇ vēṟu kulattavaruṭaṉ maṇa uṟavu koṇṭāl ataṉi uṉakkuvic kum poruṭṭut taṅkap patakkam vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭamum koṇṭu varap paṭṭatu. kumukāyac cīrtiruttaṅkaḷiyē taṉ mutukantaṇṭāykkoṇṭa tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam taṉ amiccaraviyilēyē ittakiya cīrtiruttac caṭṭaṅkaḷiyum, tiṭṭaṅkaḷiyum koṇṭu vantu veṟṟi peṟṟatu. 3-2-1969-l aṟiñar aṇṇā kālamāṉār: mu. karuṇāniti mutalamiccar āṉār. 
1971-l naṭanta iṭittērtalilum tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakamē veṟṟippeṟṟatu. aṇṇāvukkup piṟaku eṭṭu nāḷkaḷ irā. neṭuñ ceḻiyaṉ taṟkālika mutalvarāka iruntār. avarukkup piṉṉum, iṭit tērtalukkup piṉṉum kaliñar mu. karuṇānitiyē toṭarntu muta lamiccarāka iruntār. 10-2-1959 mutal 31-1-197vari irunta karuṇāniti yiṉ amiccaravi piṟa mānilaṅkaḷum piṉpaṟṟumāṟu pala ariya tiṭṭaṅkaḷi vakuttuc ceyalpaṭutti vantatu. kalvi, poruḷiyal, kumukāyac cīrtiruttaṅkaḷil tamiḻakam iṉṟu mutaliṭam peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya pēruntu vaṇṭikaḷi nāṭṭuṭimiyākkiyatu; kappal vāṇikatti mēṟkoṇṭatu; vāṉvaḻip pōkkuvaratti mēṟkoḷḷaviruntatu; mānilat tiṭṭakkuḻuvi ēṟpaṭutti ataṉ karuttukaḷi ēṟṟu naṭappatu: pacumip puraṭci, veḷḷip puraṭci, uṇavup puraṭci mutaliyaṉavum, taṉi vāriyaṅkaḷi amittup poruḷiyal vaḷarcciyit tūṇṭuvatumāṉa pala ariya tiṭṭaṅkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil poruḷiyal vaḷarccikku ēṟṟam tarukiṉṟaṉa. kāviri nīrp paṅkīṭu muṟiyum tīrntu, nīrppācaṉat tiṭṭaṅ kaḷum, vēḷāṇmit tiṭṭaṅkaḷum, toḻil tiṭṭaṅkaḷum niṟivēṟi ṉāl tamiḻakam poruḷiyal niliyil taṉṉiṟivu peṟuvatōṭu piṟa mānilaṅkaḷukkum utavum niliyil amiyum.

kalviyilum, kumukāya muṉṉēṟṟattilum tamiḻnāṭu mutaliṭam peṟṟatu. kaṇṇoḷi vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭam, muṭavar, kuruṭar, iravar (piccikkārar maṟuvāḻvut tiṭṭam,ētili aṉātikaḷukku vāḻvaḷit kum tiṭṭam mutaliyaṉa karuṇānitiyiṉ ciṟanta tiṭṭaṅkaḷākum. nāṭu muḻuvatum toḻunōyāḷarkaḷukku viṭutikaḷ amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. irappatum (picci eṭuppatum, māntaṉi māntaṉ ki vaṇṭiyil vittu iḻuttuccelvatum caṭṭaṅkaḷāl kuṟṟaṅkaḷākkappaṭṭaṉa. naravaṇṭi (ki rikcāvukkuppatil cumi mitivaṇṭi cikkiḷ rikcā) koṭukkappaṭṭatu. narikkuṟavar pōṉṟa nāṭaṟṟa ētilikaḷukkum taṉiyē kuṭiyiruppukaḷum, kalvi, toḻiṟkūṭaṅkaḷum amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kuṭicimāṟṟu vāriyam, tāḻttappaṭṭōr vīṭṭu ēntu vāriyam mutaliyaṉavum pūcāriyar aruccakar) caṭṭavarivum kumukāya nalaṉukkākak koṇṭu varappaṭṭa ciṟanta tiṭṭaṅkaḷākum, piṟpaṭṭōr nalattuṟi 
nayaṉmik kaṭci 1920 ām āṇṭu amiccaraviyi amittati liruntē piṟpaṭṭōr nalattiṟkē pāṭupaṭa muṉintatu. ataṉ maṟupiṟavi yāṉa tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam piṟpaṭṭōr nalattuṟi oṉṟit taṉiyē ēṟpaṭuttip piṟpaṭṭōrukku aracup paṇikaḷilum, kalvik kūṭaṅkaḷilum atika iṭaṅkaḷi otukkiyuḷḷatu. nāṭu muḻuvatum piṟpaṭṭōr kuḻantikaḷukkāṉa viṭutikaḷ amikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa, tāḻttappaṭṭōr nalattuṟiyuṭaṉ iṇintu ceyalpaṭṭa it tuṟiyit taṉiyē pirittu ataṟku taṉiyē amiccariyum amarttic ceyalpaṭṭa perumi mu. karuṇānitiyiyē cārum. 
itaṉāl, tāḻttappaṭṭōr-piṟpaṭṭōr calukip pōrāṭ ṭaṅkaḷum, cāti uṇarvukaḷum vaḷarntu varuvatit taṭukka muṭiyavilli, itaṉip pōkkak kumukāyac cīrtiruttattiyē mūccākakkoṇṭa tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam cātiyaṭippaṭiyi oḻittupporuḷiyal aṭippaṭiyil calukikaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉāl kumukāyattil cāti uṇarvu vaḷarāmal taṭukkalām eṉa aṟiñar palarum karutukiṉṟaṉar. tāḻttappaṭ ṭōr māṇavar viṭutikaḷum, piṟpaṭṭōr māṇavar viṭutikaḷum cāti uṇarvukaḷi uruvākkum vitip paṇṇikaḷākiviṭṭaṉa. aracu itaṉit takkapaṭi āyntu ceyalpaṭa vēṇṭum. māṇavamaṇikaḷ maṉam tiruntic cātiuṇarviviṭṭoḻittu muṉṉēṟa vēṇṭum.

uḷḷāṭcimuṟi stala stāpaṉa āṭci eṉṟavuṭaṉ nām rippaṉ pirapu (1880-1884) eṉpāṉi niṉivu kūrkiṟōm. āṉāl, kipi. 1871-l ēṟpaṭṭa uḷḷāṭcic caṭṭappaṭi (Local Fund Act) ceṉṉi māvaṭṭattil māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷum (District Bards), vaṭṭak kaḻakaṅ kaḷum (Taluk Boards), karāṭci oṉṟiyaṅkaḷum (Union Boards) 
ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷ 
1871 ām āṇṭuc caṭṭappaṭi ceṉṉi maṇṭilattil māvaṭṭac kaḻakaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. ik kaḻakaṅkaḷ nilavari varuvāyttuṟiyiṉ kīḻc ceyalpaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē, ovvoru varuvāyt tuṟip piriviṉ kīḻum oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu kaḻakaṅkaḷ niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. kōṭṭa varuvāyt tuṟi atikāri kalekṭar allatu kōṭṭāṭcit talivar) itaṉ talivarāṉār. itil aracāl amarttappaṭṭa iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu uṟuppiṉar iṭam peṟṟaṉar. inta uṟuppiṉar conta nilam allatu cottuṭiyavarkaḷāyiruppar. 1884-l koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa ceṉṉi uḷḷāṭcic caṭṭam' ovvoru māvaṭṭattilum māvaṭṭakkaḻakam ēṟpaṭavum, atil 24 uṟuppiṉarkaḷ iṭam peṟavum vaki ceytatu. inta uṟuppiṉarkaḷil oru cilar āḷunarāl amarttappaṭṭaṉar. oru cilar vaṭṭa ūrāṭci oṉṟiyak kaḻakaṅkaḷālum vari koṭuppōrālum tērn teṭukkappaṭṭaṉar. oru cila varikaḷi araciṉ opputal peṟṟu vitikkum atikāratti ik kaḻakaṅkaḷ peṟṟaṉa. vaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷ (Taluk Boards) 
māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷukkukkīḻ vaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa, ovvoru vaṭṭak kaḻakattilum aracāl amarttappaṭṭum, varikoṭup pōrālum, oṉṟiyakkaḻakattālum tērnteṭukkappaṭṭum paṉṉiraṇṭu uṟuppiṉar iṭam peṟṟaṉar. māvaṭṭa nilavarittuṟi atikāri itaṉ talivar āṉār. māvaṭṭak kaḻakam taṉ muṭivukkēṟpa vaṭṭak kaḻakaṅ kaḷukkup paṇam otukkīṭu ceytatu. varumāṉattiṟkēṟpa māvaṭṭa, vaṭṭakkaḻakaṅkaḷukkuk kuṟippiṭṭa viḻukkāṭṭut tokiyiyum utavit tokiyiyum aracu otukkiyatu.

oṉṟiyak kaḻakaṅkaḷ (Union Board) 
pala ūrkaḷ oṉṟiṇikkappaṭṭu oṉṟiyamākkappaṭṭu oṉṟiyak kaḻakattāl āḷappaṭṭatu. ovvōr ūriṉ talivarum itil uṟuppiṉ rāyiruppār. kuṟintatu aintu pēr itil uṟuppiṉarāka irukka vēṇṭum. itil uṟuppāṇmi peṟṟa ōr ūrttalivarē oṉṟiyattiṉ talivar rāka (Union Chirman) iruppār. ūrttalivarkaḷit tavirtta maṟṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷ āḷunarālum, varikoṭuppōrālum amarttappaṭuvar. 
vīṭṭu variyē oṉṟiyattiṉ mukāmivaruvāyākum. itittavira vaṭṭak kaḻakamum oru tokiyi aḷikkum. taṉakkuk kīḻuḷḷa ūrkaḷiṉ potuc cālikaḷ, kuṭinīr, tuppuravu, nilaviyal mutaliyavaṟṟi oṉṟiyam 
kavaṉittatu. ūravik kaḻakaṅkaḷ 
1920 ām āṇṭu ravi (pañcāyattuc caṭṭappaṭi ūrtōṟum paravi pañcāyattu ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ovvōr ūraviyilum ēḻu mutal patiṉintukku uṭpaṭṭa uṟuppiṉar, avvūril 25 akavikku mēṟpaṭṭa āṭavarāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭu iṭampeṟṟaṉar. cottu allatu nilam uḷḷavar maṭṭumē varikoṭuppōr oppōliyurimi uṭiyavar eṉṟu irunta nilimāṟi 25 akavi nirampiya āṭavar oppōliyurimi peṟṟu vaḷaravi uṟuppiṉarit tērnteṭuttaṉar. iccaṭṭattiṉāl nilamillāta ēḻi makkaḷukku, kuṟippākat tāḻttap paṭṭa makkaḷukkum ippōvi urimi aḷikkappaṭṭatu. naṉarukku vēṇṭiya ellāc ceyalkaḷiyum ivvūraliyē ceytu koṇṭatu. vīṭṭuvari, toḻilvari, mutaliyaṉavum, potuccottukaḷiliruntu varum varumāṉaṅkaḷukkum itaṟkum varuvāyāyiṉa. mānilam muḻuvatilumuḷḷa ūravikaḷik kaṇkāṇikka mānila uṇaravip pativāḷar oruvar amarttappaṭṭār. ivarē maṟṟa ūrāṭcik kaḻakaṅkaḷiyum, nakarāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷiyum kaṇkāṇittār. 
mēṟkūṟiya ūrāṭcik kaḻakaṅkaḷ nāḷāvaṭṭattil eṇṇikki yil atikarittatōṭu, avvappoḻutu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa cīrtiruttac caṭṭaṅkaḷāl pala māṟṟaṅkaḷip peṟṟaṉa. 127-l mānila aravip pativāḷarē nēraṭiyāka ūravikaḷukku utavittoki vaḻaṅkum atikāram peṟṟār. tokiyum atikarittatu. paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷi ūrāṭcik kaḻakaṅkaḷē ēṟṟu naṭattum urimiyum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷiṉ uṟuppiṉar eṇṇikkiyum uyarntatu. kōṭṭāṭcit talivarāyirunta nilimāṟi āḷunarāl amarttap paṭṭavar talivar āṉār. varavu celavuttiṭṭattit tāmē vakuttuk koḷḷum urimiyiyum ik kaḻakam peṟṟatu. 1930 ām āṇṭiliruntu oṉṟiyaṅkaḷ cuvikkappaṭṭu paravikaḷil cērkkappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē, ūrāṭcittuṟiyiṉ kaṭici allatu toṭakka niliyāka karavikaḷ

amintaṉa, avaṟṟiṟku mēl vaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṟku mēl māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷum amintaṉa. rāṭcikku vari celuttum yāvarukkum oppōliyurimi aḷikkappaṭṭatu. aracāl uṟuppiṉar " amarttum muṟimāṟi yāvarum makkaḷāl tērnteṭukkappeṟṟaṉar. ūravi vaṭṭa, māvaṭṭakkaḻakaṅkaḷil kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ tāḻttap paṭṭōrukkum, ciṟupāṉmiyiṉarukkum otukkappaṭṭaṉa, vaṭṭa, māvaṭṭak kaḻakat talivarkaḷi tērnteṭukkappeṟṟa) uṟuppiṉarkaḷē taṅkaḷukkuḷ tērnteṭuttukkoṇṭaṉar. it talivarkaḷ uṟuppiṉa riṉ nampikki iḻantāl taḷḷappaṭalām, māvaṭṭa ūraviyatikāri cuḷum, taṇikkiyāḷarum amarttappaṭṭaṉar. varuvāy celaviṉam yāvum atikarittaṉa. aracum oru kuṟippiṭṭa tokiyi ivaṟṟiṉ vaḷarccikkeṉa āṇṭutōṟum otukkīṭu ceytatu. nalaviyal atikārikaḷ, poṟiyāḷar mutaliyōr amarttappaṭṭaṉar. ūrāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷ toṭarvaḻip pātikaḷikkūṭa kaṇkāṇittaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkuḷḷa

1871 ām āṇṭiṉ nakaravirivākkac caṭṭappaṭi tērtal āṇi yāḷar' amarttappaṭṭār. 1878-79-l mānilattiluḷḷa ellā nakaraṅkaḷi lum nakarāṇmik kaḻakaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭutta vaḻi ceyyum caṭṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭuvarappaṭṭaṉa. 1884l ceṉṉi nakarāṇmik kaḻakac caṭṭam" koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu. itaṉpaṭi nakarāṇmik kaḻaka uṟuppiṉaril mūṉṟil iru pakutiyiṉar tērnteṭukkappaṭṭaṉar. 1920l uṟuppiṉariṉ eṇṇikki 12 iliruntu 16 āka uyarttappaṭṭatu. 1930 ām āṇṭil peṇkaḷum uṟuppiṉarākum urimi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. talivari uṟuppiṉarē tērnteṭuttaṉar. it talivarkaḷi nīkkum atikāramum uṟuppiṉarukkē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. 
uḷḷāṭci muṟi vaḷarcci ceṉṉi nakaramaṉṟat tōṟṟamum vaḷarcciyum 
ki.pi. 1640]-l ceṉṉiyil puṉita cārcu kōṭṭiyikkaṭṭi ataṉic cuṟṟilum āṅkilēyarkaḷ kuṭiyamarntaṉar. ki.pi. 1687-l veḷiyāṉa paṭṭiyac caṭṭappaṭi ceṉṉi nakarip parāmarikka nakarat tanti (Mayor) amarttappaṭṭār. avarukku utaviyāka 12 nakaramutuvarkaḷ (Aldernet) ti kuṭimakaṉkaḷ ākiyōr amarttappaṭṭaṉar. nakarat tantiyiṉ patavikkālam ōrāṇṭu eṉṟum nakaramutuvarkaḷ (Aldernmen) patavikkālam āyuṭkālameṉṟum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭatu. piṉṉar nakarat tantiyi niyamaṉam ceyyāmal nakaramutuvarkaḷē (Aldernmen) tērn teṭuttaṉar. nakaramutuvarkaḷum kuṭimakkaḷāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭaṉar. kuṭimakkaḷ eṉpavar ceṉṉivācikaḷāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭavar.

aṭutta kaṭṭa vaḷarcci rippaṉ pirapu' (kipi. 1880-1888) intiyāviṉ talimi āḷunarāyiruntapōtu avar koṇṭuvanta uḷḷāṭci muṟit tīrmāṉattiliruntu toṭaṅkiyatu. * 'uḷḷāṭci muṟiyeṉpatu potu makkaḷukku araciyalil paṅkēṟkak koṭukkum aṭippaṭip payiṟci yākum" eṉpatu avaruṭiya kōṭpāṭākum.

(2) ōṉarkaḷi uṭkoṇṭa pakutikaḷil pañcāyattu oṉṟiyaṅ kaḷum, 
(3) māvaṭṭa aḷavil māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷum āka, ""mūṉṟaṭukku uḷḷāṭcimuṟi" ēṟpaṭa vaḻivakuttatu. 
ivvāṟu, paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarccip peṟṟa uḷḷāṭcimuṟi iṉṟu tamiḻakattil makkaḷiṉ aṉṟāṭa uyirt tuṭippākak kāṭci yaḷikkiṟatu. kuṟippākac ceṉṉi maṇṭilattil iraṭṭi āṭci naṭanta kālakkaṭṭattil (ki.pi. 1920-1937) tāṉ itu āḻamāka vērūṉṟat toṭaṅkiyatu. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka:- ceṉṉi uḷḷāṭci caṭṭam, ceṉṉi ūrāṭcikaḷiṉ caṭṭam mutaliyaṉa 1920-l niṟivēṟṟap paṭṭaṉa. 1930 ām āṇṭu māvaṭṭa nakaramaṉṟaṅkaḷiṉ caṭṭamum, uḷḷāṭcic caṭṭat tiruttamum koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉa. 1935lēṟpaṭṭa intiya araciyal caṭṭam mānilat taṉṉāṭci muṟiyi aṟimukap paṭuttiyatu. eṉavētāṉ ivvāṇṭiliruntu toṭaṅkum uḷḷāṭci muṟiyit ''tamiḻakattilēṟpaṭṭa uḷḷāṭci muṟiyiṉ poṟkālat toṭarcci eṉpar. 
kipi.1909-tilēṟpaṭṭa intiya araciyal caṭṭappaṭi iculāmiyar, intiyak kiṟittuvar, āṅkilō intiyar mutaliyōrukku iṭam otukkappaṭṭatu. itil cariyāṉa vikitattil iṭam peṟātavarukku ''niyamaṉamuṟi"ppaṭi iṭam otukkappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, 1920lkoṇṭu varappaṭṭa ceṉṉi maṇṭil māvaṭṭa nakaramaṉṟac caṭṭappaṭi inta niyamaṉamuṟi nīkkappaṭṭatu. 1933-l ēṟpaṭṭa caṭṭappaṭi, nakaramaṉṟa āṇiyar muḻunēra atikāriyāka amarttappaṭṭu uḷḷāṭcici-- ceyalkaḷi avarē naṭimuṟippaṭuttiṉār. viṭutalikkuppiṉ nakarmaṉṟat talivarkaḷum, āṇiyarkaḷum iṇintu ceyalpaṭṭaṉar, palvēṟu kuḻukkaḷākap pirintu uṟuppiṉarkaḷ ceyalpaṭṭaṉar. aintāṇṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷum uḷḷāṭci vaḷarcciyum 
aṭittaḷa makkaḷiliruntu, uyarniliyiluḷḷa makkaḷ variyilāṉa nāṭṭiṉ oṭṭumotta vaḷarccikkākavē, nīṇṭakāla aintāṇṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷ tayār ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aintāṇṭut tiṭṭam 1951-l toṭaṅkiyatu. aintāṇṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷāl ūrāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷil oṭṭumotta vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭṭatu. ataṟkākavē, putiya pañcāyattuc caṭṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatōṭu naṭimuṟiyilirunta caṭṭaṅ kaḷum tirutti amikkappaṭṭaṉa. ovvoru pañcāyattiṟkum vēṇṭiya

niti otukkappaṭṭatu. ovvoru pañcāyattum mānila, naṭuvaṇ 
āṭciyuṭaṉ iṇintu ceyalpaṭṭatu. 
1951 ām āṇṭu makkaḷ tokik kaṇakkuppaṭi, intiyāvil 85 viḻukkāṭu makkaḷ karppuṟaṅkaḷil tāṉiruntaṉar. eṉavētāṉ, 1952l kumukāya vaḷarccit tiṭṭamum, vaṭṭāra vaḷarccittiṭṭamum koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉa. 
vaṭṭāra vaḷarccittuṟi atikārikaḷum, kirāma cēvakarkaḷum piṟarum amarttappaṭṭaṉar. kirāma cukātāra niliyaṅkaḷ, kālnaṭi cukātāra niliyaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. kalvi vaḷarcci mika uyarntatu. vēḷāṇmi vaḷarcci, ciṟutoḻil vaḷarcci mutaliya poru ḷātāra muṉṉēṟṟamum vaḷarntaṉa. ivvāṟu mutal, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟāvatu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷilēyē muṟiyē 1951 - 55, 1956-5, 1956 ākiya kālakaṭṭaṅkaḷukkuḷ kirāmap pañcāyattukkaḷ muḻuvaḷarcci aṭintu viṭṭaṉa. 
nāṉkāvatu: intāvatu aintu āṇṭut tiṭṭakālattil (1889-197) aintāṇṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷukkuppatilāka ōrāṇṭu tiṭṭaṅkaḷi (Annual Plan) vakuttuc ceyalpaṭutta aracu muṟpaṭṭatu. itil pañcāyattu rācciyam (Panchayat Raj) eṉṟa peyaril uḷḷāṭci muṟiceyalpaṭṭatu. itil ūrāṭcikaḷi oṉṟiṇittu māvaṭṭa, vaṭṭāra vaḷarccikku ēṟpa tiṭṭaṅkaḷ ceyalpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl ovvoru vaḷarukkum tēvippaṭum kuṟinta pakka ''taṉṉiṟivu' pūrtti ceyyappaṭṭatu. 
1980-85-l āṟāvatu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭam toṭaṅkiyatu. ic kālakkaṭṭattil kumukāyap poruḷātāra mēmpāṭṭil atikak kavaṉam celuttappaṭṭatu. itaṟkākap pañcāyattuc caṭṭaṅkaḷ, uḷḷāṭci muṟi amippukaḷ mutaliyaṉa māṟṟi amikkap peṟṟaṉa. mēlum makkaḷāl, makkaḷukkāka naṭattappaṭum āṭciyāka uḷḷāṭci muṟi muṟṟilum māṟṟappaṭṭatu. ' ivvāṟu, aintu āṇṭut tiṭṭakkālaṅkaḷil uḷḷāṭciyik kaṭṭamikkattoṭaṅki, ōr āṇṭukāla tiṭṭakkālak kaṭṭamippāka vum ceyalpaṭṭa uḷḷāṭci muṟi vaḷarcci kālantōṟum āṇṭa araciyal kaṭcikaḷāl valuppeṟṟut tēciyattōṭu iṇintu oru mukamāyc ceyalpaṭṭatu. ki.pi. 1850-liruntu ceṉṉi mānakarāṭciyiṉ vaḷarcci 
ceṉṉi nakaramaṉṟattiṉ tōṟṟamum vaḷarcciyum paṟṟip pārt tōm. tamiḻakattil uḷḷāṭci muṟi mutalil ceṉṉi nakarattiltāṉ tōṉṟiyateṉpataṉippārttōm. kipi.1540liruntu 1850 variyilāṉa ceṉṉi mānakara vaḷarcciyiyum pārttōm. tamiḻakattil iraṭṭi āṭci toṭaṅkiya poḻututāṉ uḷḷāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷiṉ vaḷarcci muṭukkamāka vaḷarntatu. uḷḷāṭci maṉṟac caṭṭaṅkaḷ palavum caṭṭa 
21) 
tāynilavaralāṟu 
maṉṟattāl koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉa, kiḻakkintiya vaṇika āṭcikkuḻu uḷḷāṭci vaḷarcciyil akkarik kāṭṭāmaliruntatāl makkaḷāṭcit toṭakkappaṭṭa iraṭṭi āṭcik kālattil muṭukkamāy vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu. 1920-lēyē 70-liruntu 80 variyilāṉa nakara maṉṟaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. āyiṉum, 1979 ām āṇṭu intiya araciyal caṭṭattiṟkuppiṉ tāṉ ceṉṉi mānakarāṭci muḻumiyāka vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu. nakaramaṉṟakkuḻu, nilikkuḻu āṇiyar, nakarattanti ākiyōr ēṟpaṭṭa ṉar. avarkaḷiṉ atikāraṅkaḷum variyaṟukkappaṭṭaṉa. 
nayaṉmik kaṭciyiṉ āṭcikālattil peṇkaḷukku 1921-l vāk kurimi vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu, ciṟupāṉmiyarukku nikarāḷiyam vaḻaṅkap paṭṭatu. 
1933ām āṇṭu ceṉṉi mānakarat talivar (President) eṉṟa pataviyic ceṉṉi mānakarattanti (Mayor of Madras Corporation) eṉṟum māṟṟiṉārkaḷ. eṉavē, talivarāka irunta vayavar. irācā muttiyā ceṭṭiyār ceṭṭināṭu aracar ceṉṉi mānakara mutal tantiyāṉār. avar ci-3-1913 mutal 7-11-1933 vari mānakarat tantiyākac ceyalpaṭṭār. ceṉṉi mānakara maṉṟam toṭaṅkiyati liruntu aṉṟu vari pirāmaṇarkaḷē ''mānakara maṉṟat talivarkaḷāka iruntaṉar. ivar tāṉ mutal ''tanti'' eṉpatum * mutal pirāmaṇa rallātavar" eṉpatum kuṟippiṭattakkatu. varuppuvāripaṭinikarāḷiyampaṭi mutalil nakarattantiyāṉavarum ivarēyāvār.vakuppuvāri paṭinikarāḷiya vitippaṭi mutaṉ mutalākac ceṉṉi mānakarat tantiyāṉa mutal āti tirāviṭarcē. civacaṇmukampiḷḷi yāvār. 
1958ām āṇṭu ceṉṉi mānakarāṭciyil 50-liruntu 100 pirivukaḷ (wards) ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. atē āṇṭil nakaramutuvarkaḷ (Alderrhern) muṟiyum oḻikkappaṭṭatu. 
1951-l ceṉṉi nakara nakariyam" (Madras City Municipality) eṉpati "ceṉṉi nakariya mānakarāṭci " (Madras CityCorporation) eṉa māṟṟappaṭṭatu. 1973-l 100-liruntu 173 pirivukaḷāka (Wards) nakarāṭcip pirittāḷappaṭṭatu. 1945 ām āṇṭu potut tērtalukkuppiṉ 1957 vari pērāyakkaṭciyiṉ (kāṅkiracu āṭciyē toṭarntu iruntatu. ik kālakkaṭṭattiltāṉ uḷḷāṭcittuṟikkeṉa taṉi amicca cum" ēṟpaṭṭatu. "niyamaṉa muṟi" aṭiyōṭu oḻikkappaṭṭatu.) 
1953-l ceṉṉi makaḷaṭivattil mottamirunta 25 māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷukkum, ō8 nakariyaṅkaḷukkum, ''nakaramippuc caṭṭam" (Town Plarl ning Act) koṇṭuvarappaṭṭu, ataṉpaṭi orē mātiriyāṉa uḷḷāṭci amippu ēṟpaṭṭatu. itaṉaṭippaṭiyiltāṉ pañcāyattu irācciyam, kumukāya vaḷarccit tiṭṭaṅkaḷ, putiya pāñcāyattukkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttutal mutaliyaṉa tōṉṟi ""kumukāyanalamē nāṭṭiṉ nōkkam' eṉap pāṭupaṭat toṭaṅkiṉa.

ceṉṉi mānakarāṭciyil mānakarāṭcit talivarkaḷ (Presidents) uṟuppiṉarkaḷāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭaṉar. 1919 ām āṇṭu mutal 1932 ām āṇṭuvari "'talivarkaḷ" eṉṟa pataviyil pattu pēr irunta ṉar. 1933 ām āṇṭil talivar eṉṟa patavi nakarat tanti (Mayor) eṉa māṟṟappaṭṭatu. mutal nakarat tantiyāka vayavar. irācā muttiyā ceṭṭiyār tērnteṭukkappaṭṭār.1933 mutal 2000 vari tērnteṭuk kappaṭṭa nakarat tantiyarkaḷ vakuppuvārip paṭinikarāḷiyappaṭi tērn teṭukkappaṭṭaṉar. 200 mutal makkaḷē nēraṭiyāka mānakarat tantiyit tērnteṭuttaṉar. avaruṭiya patavik kālamum caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉar kaḷip pōl aintāṇṭukaḷāṉatu. avvāṟu mutaṉ mutalāka makkaḷāl tērnteṭukkap peṟṟavar ti.mu.ka.vic cērnta mu.ka. sṭāliṉ (Mr.K. Stalin) āvār. āṉāl, *'oru āḷukku oru patavi'' eṉṟa caṭṭa vitiyiṉ paṭi avar ''mānakarattanti "'pataviyi 2002-l iḻantār. itaṉāl avar ceṉṉic caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉar pataviyit takkavittukkoṇṭār. 
vakuppuvāri paṭinikarāḷiya vitippaṭi mēyar patavi vakikkappaṭṭa tāl intiyak kiṟittuvar, iculāmiyar, ātitirāviṭar mutaliyōrum mānakarat tantiyākap patavi vakittaṉar. ātitirāviṭanakarattantiyarkaḷil cē, civacaṇmukam piḷḷi (1937-38) eṉ. civarācu (1945-46), pi. paramēcuvaraṉ (1953-54), ci. kucēlar (1951-53), vi. pālacuntaram (1969.70) mutaliyōr kuṟippiṭattakkavarāvar, 
ciṟappāṉa ulaka mānakara maṉṟaṅkaḷip pōl ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkuvatu ceṉṉi mānakaramākum. mānakarap paṇikaḷil mikavum ciṟappāṉaviyākak karutappaṭuvaṉa toṭakkakkalvi vaḷarcciyum, māṇavarukku matiya uṇavaḷikkum tiṭṭamum, kuṭici māṟṟu vāriyamum, taṭiyilāk kuṭinīr vaḻaṅkum muṟiyum, ataṟkākac cattiyamūrtti pūṇṭi) nīrttēkkattiyum, ceṅkuṉṟam ērinīrt tēkkattiyum ēṟpaṭuttiyatum, ulakat tarattiṟku oppāṉa nakaraccālikaḷ, cākkaṭi ēntukaḷ, mēmpālaṅkaḷ, pūṅkākkaḷ mutaliyaṉavumākum, kacappāṉa ceyti, mānakarap paṇikaḷ araciyal kaṭcikaḷiṉ kāḻppu uṇarvukaḷāl cila camayam cemmik keṭṭu niṟpatākum. 
uḷḷāṭci vaḷarcci kālantōṟum kālattiṟkēṟpa vaḷarntu koṇṭē vantu makkaḷ cēviyē makēcaṉ cēvi" eṉṟa tattuvattil ceyalpaṭukiṟatu. itaṉāltāṉ kipi. 1934-l vaṭṭāṭcik kaḻakaṅkaḷ (Taluk Boards) mūṭappaṭṭu māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷukku (District Ecards) mukāmiyaḷikkappaṭṭatu. piṉṉar māvaṭṭak kaḻakaṅkaḷ 1953-l mūṭap paṭṭu, kālattiṟkēṟpa vaṭṭāra vaḷarcci aluvalakaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. intiyāviṉ uyirnāṭi kirāmaṅkaḷē eṉṟa kāntiyāriṉ viruppappaṭi pañcāyattukkaḷum, pañcāyattu rācciyamum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. aintāṇṭut tiṭṭak kālaṅkaḷil virintu vaḷarccip peṟṟaṉa. 1959.79 kālakkaṭṭat til ivaṟṟiṟku āṇṭuttiṭṭaṅkaḷum, valuvāṉa nitiyum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.

tāynila varalāṟu 
''rippaṉ eṅkaḷ appaṉ"" eṉṟu intiyar aṉivarālum pōṟṟip pukaḻappaṭum rippaṉ piraputāṉ navīṉa uḷḷāṭci muṟiyi intiyāvil aṟimukappaṭuttiṉār. avar pāmarap potumakkaḷum āṭcimuṟiyil paṅku peṟṟup payiṟci peṟuvatē inta uḷḷāṭciyiṉ nōkkameṉṟār. avaruṭiya kaṉavu iṉṟu muḻumiyāka niṟivēṟi viṭṭatu. vākkāḷarkaḷukkuc cotturimi irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa viti maṟintu, vayatu vantōr ellōrukkum vākkurimi ēṟpaṭṭatu. 1909 ām āṇṭu ēṟpaṭṭa araciyal caṭṭappaṭi kiṟittuvar. iculāmiyar, cīkkiyar mutaliya ciṟupāṉmiyiṉarukkum, 1919 ām āṇṭilēṟpaṭṭa intiya araciyal caṭṭappaṭi tāḻttappaṭṭavarukkum uḷḷāṭci maṉṟaṅ kaḷilum, caṭṭa nāṭāḷum maṉṟaṅkaḷilum paṭinikarāḷiyam koṭukkap paṭṭatu. 
1950 ām āṇṭiliruntu naṭimuṟikku vanta intiya araciyal caṭṭattilēyē uḷḷāṭcimuṟiyum kuṟippākap pañcāyattu irācciyam mum oru pakutiyākac cērkkappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē iṉṟu cāti camaya vēṟupā ṭiṉṟi intiyar evarum uḷḷāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷil paṅkēṟkalām. ittakiyap payiṟciyāl caṭṭamaṉṟa, nāṭāḷum maṉṟaṅkaḷilum amiccar, kuṭiyaracuttalivar pōṉṟa uyarpatavikaḷilum evarum varalām. eṉavētāṉ, rippaṉ cuṇṭa kaṉavu iṉṟu naṉavākiviṭṭatu eṉkiṟōm. 
' iṉṉamum kuṟippiṭṭa iṉattiṟkuppaṭinikarāḷikaḷi niyamaṉam" ceyyum muṟiyum uḷḷatu.tāḻttappaṭṭavarippōlavē makaḷirukkum iṭa otukkīṭu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pala pañcāyattukkaḷil makaḷirum talivarkaḷāka uḷḷaṉar. atilum āti tirāviṭa makaḷirukkeṉṟu taṉi iṭotukkīṭu uṇṭu. āyiṉum, pala āyiram āṇṭukaḷākac cātippirivukaḷ ḷālum, tīṇṭāmiyālum vāṭiya intiyar putitāka araciyal kaṭcikaḷ' eṉṟa pēyāl āṭṭippaṭikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. caṭṭamaṉṟa nāṭāḷumaṉṟat tērtalkaḷil maṭṭum araciyal kaṭcikaḷiṉ peyarāl vēṭpāḷar pōṭṭiyiṭuvatu nallatu. uḷḷāṭci maṉṟaṅkaḷil araciyali aṭiyōṭu oḻittāl uḷḷāṭci oḷimayamāṉatākat tikaḻum, 
viṭutalikkuppiṉ tamiḻakam 
(1947 mutal iṉṟu vari) 
muṉṉuri 
1947 ām āṇṭu ākacṭu 15 ām nāḷ āṅkilēyariṉ aṭimip piṭiyiliruntu pāratanāṭu viṭutaliyaṭintatu. āṉāl, viṭutalikkup piṉ potuvāka intiyāvilum, kuṟippākat tamiḻnāṭṭilum amiti yāṉa cūḻnili illi. aṉṟu tamiḻakam taṉittiyaṅkavilli. ceṉṉi maṇṭilattiṉ oru pakutiyākavē iruntatu. āntiramum, kēraḷattiṉ oru pakutiyum, kaṉṉaṭattiṉ oru pakutiyum im maṇṭilattil iṇintu iruntaṉa. eṉavē, tamiḻ, teluṅku, maliyāḷam, oriyā, kaṉṉaṭam ākiya moḻi pēcum makkaḷum, ciṟupāṉmi moḻiyiṉarum iṅkiruntaṉar. nāṭu viṭutali peṟṟavuṭaṉ im makkaḷ moḻivaḻi mānilaṅkaḷ piriya ' vēṇṭumeṉa virumpiṉar.pērāntiram vicāla āntiram vēṇṭumeṉak kōri āntirar muṭukkamāṉap pōrāṭṭam toṭuttaṉar. itaṉi muṉ vittup poṭṭi ciṟīrāmulu' eṉṟa āntira tēciyavāti, taṉ uyiriyum viṭṭār. kalakam kaṭṭukkaṭaṅkāmal pōkavē āntirattit tamiḻakatti viruntu taṉiyē pirittu, 1.10.1953-l āntira mānilam uruvākkiṉar. ' 
, āntirattiyaṭuttut tamiḻakattilum āntirattōṭum, kēraḷat tōṭum iṇintu irunta ceṉṉi maṇṭilat tamiḻppakutikaḷip pirittut tamiḻakattōṭu iṇittut taṉittamiḻnāṭu vēṇṭumeṉa vaṭa tamiḻ nāṭṭil tamiḻaracukkaḻakam' teṉ tamiḻnāṭṭil "tāyt tamiḻaka iyakkam' mutaliyaṉavum pōrāṭiṉa. pērarntiram pirikkappaṭumpōtu cittūr māvaṭṭam muḻuvatum ataṉuṭaṉ iṇikkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu, atiluḷḷa tiruttaṇi, tirukkāḷatti, pallavaṉēṟi, kaṅkuntikkuppam, cittūr ākiya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, tamiḻarkaḷ vacippatāl, avaṟṟiyum tamiḻakattōṭu cērkkavēṇṭumeṉat tamiḻaracukkaḻakam pōrāṭiyatu. kaṭiciyil cittūr tavirtta maṟṟavaṟṟil cila pakutikaḷ tamiḻakattuṭaṉ iṇikkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl vaṭakkelliyil oruvāṟu amiti ēṟpaṭṭatu.

1pērāyak kaṭci āṭciyiṉ cātaṉikaḷ (1947-1967) 
1935 ām āṇṭu intiya araciyal caṭṭappaṭi 1937-l naṭanta tērtalil pērāyak kaṭci (kāṅkiras veṟṟippeṟṟu, 1937 mutal 1939 vari 18 tiṅkaḷ ci. irācakōpālāccāriyār talimiyil amic caravi amittu āṇṭatu. iraṇṭām ulakappōril intiyat talivarkaḷik kalantōcikkāmal piriṭṭāṉiyar intiyāviyum pōrilīṭupaṭuttiyatāl etirpput terivittu, pērāyak kaṭcit tāṉē amiccaraviyik kalittuk koṇṭatu. 
itaṟkuppiṉ 1939 mutal 1945varip pōrkkāla āṭci allatu iṭikkāla āṭci naṭantatu. tēciya viṭutalip pōriṉ tākkattāl 1946-l tērtal naṭantatu. atil pērāyak kaṭciyē veṟṟippeṟṟu ceṉṉi maṇṭilattil āṭci amittatu. teluṅkarāṉ pirakācam talimiyil 30 4.14-l amiccaravi amintatu. moḻic cikkalāl pērāyak kaṭciyilum uṭkaṭcippūccilēṟpaṭṭatu. itaṉaṭippaṭiyil pirakācam14.3.1947-l patavi tuṟantār. avariyaṭuttut tamiḻarāṉa ō.pi. irāmacāmi reṭṭiyār mutalamiccarāṉār. avar 2331947liruntu 1949 vari mutalamiccar patavi vakittār. ivar mutalamiccarāka irunta poḻututāṉ intiyāvukku viṭutali {75-8-1947) kiṭittatu. cātaṉikaḷ 
ivaruṭiya āṭcikkālattil potuvuṭimikkārarkaḷ pala puraṭcikaḷic ceytaṉar. avaṟṟi aṭakkuvataṟkākavē potu amitip pēṇum caṭṭam koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu; uṇavuppaṟṟāk kuṟiyic camāḷikka niyāya vilik kaṭikaḷ ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa; kāykaṟi uṟpatti atikappaṭuttappaṭṭatu; pācaṉa ēntukaḷ atikarikkap paṭṭaṉa; ātitirāviṭar nalatti mēmpaṭuttat toḻilāḷar nalat" tuṟiyi iraṇṭākap pirittu aricaṉa nalattuṟi'yi ēṟpaṭuttiṉār; 1947l kōyil nuḻivuccaṭṭam koṇṭuvantār; putiya kalvik kōṭ pāṭṭāl maṉarppurakkuḻantikaḷukkum kalvip payaṉ kiṭikkappeṟṟatu. ,

ci. irācakōpālāccāriyār (irācāci - 1952-1954) 
1952-l intiyāviṉ mutal potuttērtal naṭantatu. pērāyak kaṭciyē veṟṟip peṟṟatu. cakkaravartti irācakōpālāccāriyār 
mutalamiccarāṉār. cātaṉikaḷ 
* "kulakkalvit tiṭṭam' * koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. itaṉi etirttu nāṭu muḻuvatum naṭanta kiḷarccikaḷāl atu kiviṭappaṭṭatu, inta etirppiṉ etiroliyāka irācāci patavit tuṟantār. kulakkalvit tiṭṭattāl palanūṟu paḷḷikaḷ mūṭappaṭṭaṉa. pala āciriyarkaḷ vēli iḻantaṉar. 
ku. kāmarācar (1954-1963) - irācāci patavi tuṟantatum, tamiḻnāṭupērāyak kaṭcit talivarāṉa ku. kāmarācar mutalvarāṉār. mutaṉmuṟiyāka 1958 mutal 1957 vari mutalamiccar patavi vakittār. 1957-l naṭanta nāṭṭiṉ iraṇṭāvatu potut tērtalil pērāyakkaṭciyē mīṇṭum veṟṟippeṟṟatāl 1957 mutal 16 vari iraṇṭām muṟiyākak kāmarācar mutalamiccarāṉār, 1952-l naṭanta mūṉṟāvatu potut tērtalilum pērāyak kaṭciyē veṟṟip peṟṟatāl mūṉṟāvatu muṟiyākak kāmarācarē mutalamicca rāṉār. 1952 mutal 1963 vari mutalamiccarāyiruntu, mūtta kaṭcit toṇṭarkaḷ patavi vilakik kaṭcippaṇikaḷic ceyya vēṇṭumeṉat tāḷē oru tiṭṭatti vakuttu mutalamiccar pataviyit tuṟantuveḷi yēṟiya avari aṉittintiyap pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ talivarākkiṉar. avaruṭiya tiṭṭatti avaruṭiya peyarālēyē "kāmarācar tiṭṭam" eṉṟaḻittaṉar, cātaṉikaḷ 
kalvippuraṭci : ci. irācakōpālāccāriyār (irācāci) avarkaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭap paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷik kāmarācar tiṟantār; * "ellōrukkum kalvi'' eṉṟa taṉ neñcārnta kōṭpāṭṭic ceyalpaṭutta ārtōṟum paḷḷikaḷit tōṟṟuvittār. 1955-56-l eṭṭām vakuppuvari ellōrukkum ilavacak kalviyaḷittār. 1950-500l aintu kallukku oru uyarnilip paḷḷit tiṭṭattiṉ kīḻ uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷiyum mūṉṟu kal cuṟṟuvārattiṟkuḷ oru toṭakkap paḷḷiyum toṭaṅkiṉār. .

irācāciyiṉ āṭcikkālattil uyar nilippaḷḷiyil paṭittavar eṇṇikki 31900 māṇavarkaḷākum. kāmarācar āṭcikkālattil itu 7,15,000 āka uyarntatu. kāmarācar kalvikkuc celaviṭum tokiyē nāṭṭiṉ celavut tokiyil mutaṉmiyāṉatu. itikkaṇṭa potu makkaḷ tāṅkaḷē muṉvantu ēḻukōṭi urupā naṉkoṭiyāka aḷittaṉar. culḷivīṭutōṟum ceṉṟaṭintatu. 
19-5-l 40 narkaḷil kaṭṭāyak kalviyic ceyalpaṭuttiṉār. mutal vakuppu mutalē āṅkilam paṭikkac ceytār. itaṉāl paṭṭap paṭippuppaṭitta āciriyarkaḷ atikamāka āciriyar paṇiyil amarttap paṭṭaṉar. āciriyar vāḻkkit taram uyarttappaṭṭatu. toḻilkalvi, maruttuvakkalvi mutaliyaṉa mēmpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.kōviyilum, maturi yilum palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ toṭaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. āciriyap payiṟcik kalluārikaḷ ēḻiliruntu patiṉēḻāka uyarntaṉa. āciriyappayiṟcip paḷḷikaḷ 107viruntu 138 āka uyarntaṉa. poṟiyiyal kallūrikaḷil māṇavar eṇṇikki atikarikkappaṭṭatu. paḷḷi māṇavarkaḷukku ilavaca matiya uṇavut tiṭṭam mukāmip peṟṟatu. ataṟkāka atikap paṇam celaviṭappaṭṭatu, tamiḻ āṭci moḻiyātal - ku. kāmarācar iraṇṭām muṟiyākat tamiḻakattiṉ mutalvarāṉa vuṭaṉ tamiḻ moḻiyē tamiḻakattiṉ āṭcimoḻiyāṉatu. ataṟkāṉa āṭcimoḻic caṭṭamuṉvarivi kalvi amiccar ci. cuppiramaṇiyam caṭṭamaṉṟattiṉ muṉ vittup pēcumpōtu kaṭanta aimpatu, aṟupatu āṇṭukaḷāka aṟiñar perumakkaḷ ayarātu uḻittatiṉ palaṉākavē, iṉṟu tamiḻ tamiḻaka niruvākat tuṟiyi āḷappōkiṟatu. avarkaḷiṉ uḻippāl iṉṟup pūttuk kuluṅkum tamiḻ nāḷik kāyākik kaṉiyākit tamiḻarkaḷukku iṉcuvi amutāyp payaṉpaṭappōkiṟatu. itaṟkāka, āṭcimuṟiyil eppaṭi tamiḻic ceyalpaṭuttuvatu eṉpati āynturikka " āṭci moḻi āyvukkuḻu" oṉṟi ēṟpaṭuttiṉōm. akkuḻu, aracuttuṟikaḷil aluvalakaṅkaḷil tamiḻmoḻiyic ceyal paṭuttum vaḻivakikaḷi āyntu aracukku aḷittatu" eṉṟār. 
mutalil ik kuḻuviṉar tayāritta āṭciyāḷarkaḷ (tuṟiyil . paṇiyāṟṟuvōr) kiyāḷum āṅkilac coṟkaḷukku iṇiyāṉa tamiḻccoṟkaḷi uruvākki * āṭciccol akarāti" (Glossary of Administrative Terrms) (English-Tamil) eṉṟa oru nūlākat tamiḻaka aracu 1966-l veḷiyiṭṭatu. itaṉi mēlum cemmippaṭutti, aṭuttu vanta timuka aracum veḷiyiṭṭatu. 
āṭcimoḻit tiṭṭac ceyalpāṭu 
tamiḻi āṭci moḻiyāka ākkum caṭṭattip pērāyak kaṭci āṭci 1957 ām āṇṭu, caṉavarit tiṅkaḷ 21 ām nāḷ koṇṭuvantatu.

tāynilavaralāṟu 
ic caṭṭattic ceyalpaṭutta aracu oru kuḻuvi amarttic ceyalmuṟi naṭavaṭikkikaḷi aḷikkac ceytatu. ik kuḻuviṉ muyaṟciyāl veḷi yiṭappaṭṭatē mēlē kūṟappaṭṭa ''āṭcic col akarāti"yākum. 
aṭuttu, āṭci aluvalakaṅkaḷiluḷḷa eḻuttarkaḷ, pirivut talivarkaḷukku ituvariyil āṅkilattil eḻuti vanta muṟiyi māṟṟi 
eppaṭit tamiḻil eḻutuvatu yeṉpataṟkup payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭatu. ituvari āṅkilat taṭṭaccukkaḷē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṟku īṭākat tamiḻt taṭṭaccukkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiṉar. 
tamiḻ teriyāta paṇiyāḷarkaḷukkut tamiḻil eḻutavum, paṭikkavum payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭatu. ittakiya māṟṟattiṉpōtu paṇiyāḷar cuḷukku aiyam ēṟpaṭāmalirukkac cariyāṉa tamiḻkkōppukaḷit tayārikka aluvalakaṅkaḷilēyē moḻipeyarppup pirivu" (Tarislation Sction) ēṟpaṭṭatu. 
1955ām āṇṭilēyē āṅkilac curukkeḻuttu muṟiyip pōlavē tamiḻc curukkeḻuttu muṟi veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. iti naṭi muṟippaṭutta aracē itaṟkāka oru ciṟanta payiṟci niliyatti uruvākkiyatu. itaṉāl tamiḻc curukkeḻuttāḷarum, taṭṭaccāḷarum perumaḷavil aluvalakap paṇikaḷil amarttappaṭṭaṉar. 
mānilat tērvāṇiyattāl tērvu ceyyappeṟum paṇiyāḷar kaḷukkut tamiḻil kōppukaḷit tayārittal, tamiḻil taṭṭaccu ceytal mutaliyavaṟṟil payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. naṭuvaṇ āṭciyāḷarkaḷākat tērnteṭukkap peṟum intiya āṭcippaṇiyāḷarkaḷ (IAS) intiyak kāval tuṟip paṇiyāḷarkaḷ (I.P.S), mutaliyōrukkum, vēṟṟumoḻip paṇiyāḷarkaḷukkum āṭcit tuṟiyil tamiḻil paṭikkavum, eḻutavum ciṟappāṉa payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭatu. 
tamiḻaka aracuppaṇiyil cēruvataṟkup pōtiya tamiḻaṟivu irukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum aracu āṇi piṟappittatu. 1957-l tamiḻaka aracup paṇiyil putitākac cēruvataṟkum, patavi uyarvu peṟuvataṟkum, māṟṟup paṇiyil cēruvataṟkum mānila aḷavu tamiḻ aṟivut tēviyeṉum 
āṇi piṟantatu. ataṟkāṉat tuṟittērvum naṭattappaṭṭatu. 
1961 ām āṇṭu cōtaṉi aṭippaṭiyil intuccamaya aṟa niliya āṭcit tuṟiyiṉ tuṇi āṇiyar aluvalakaṅkaḷ, mīṉ vaṉattuṟi utavi iyakkunar aluvalakam, pativuttuṟiyil māvaṭṭap pativāḷar aluvalakam uḷḷiṭṭa uyarnili aluvalakaṅkaḷilum, nitittaṇikkit tuṟiyilum, tamiḻil aluvalkaḷ naṭipeṟat toṭaṅkiṉa. 
1961 ām āṇṭu mutalē māvaṭṭa niliyil amintuḷḷa palvēṟu tuṟikaḷilum, cārnili aluvalakaṅkaḷilum tamiḻil aluvalakap paṇikaḷ toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉa. 1953-l 38 tuṟikaḷil tamiḻ āṭcimoḻiyāṉatu.
