vitivilakkukaḷ 
1. paṇappaṭṭiyalkaḷ, campaḷap paṭṭiyalkaḷ, karuvūlaṅkaḷ, mānilak kaṇakkāyar aluvalakam, campaḷak kaṇakku aluvalakam ākiyavaṟ ṟuṭaṉ mēṟkoḷḷum kaṭitattoṭarpukaḷ āṅkilattil irukkalām. cārnili aluvalakaṅkaḷ tuṟittalimi aluvalakaṅkaḷuṭa ṉum, mānila aracuṭaṉum ūr nayaṉmi maṉṟam nīṅkalākap piṟa nayaṉmi maṉṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉum mēṟkoḷḷum kaṭitat toṭarpukaḷ 
āṅkilattil irukkalām. 3. mēlmuṟiyīṭukaḷ toṭarpāṉa caṭṭa āṇikaḷ āṅkilattil 
irukkalām. 4. naṭuvaṇ aracu, ayalnāṭukaḷ, ayal nāṭṭut tūtarakaṅkaḷ, ayal nāṭṭavar ayalnāṭṭu niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ, piṟa mānilaṅkaḷ mutaliya

1. tamiḻ āṭcimoḻit tiṭṭatti aracu aluvalakaṅkaḷil ceyalpaṭutta āvaṉa ceyyavum, aracāṇikaḷ aṟikkikaḷ, 
aṟivuṟuttalkaḷ veḷiyiṭavum ikkuḻu āvaṉa ceytatu. 2 palvēṟu cuṟṟaṟikkikaḷip piṟappittut tamiḻ āṭcimoḻit " tiṭṭatti vaḷarttatu. 
3. aluvalakaṅkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum caṭṭaṅkaḷ, vitikaḷ, naṭimuṟi nūlkaḷ, paṭivaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟit tamiḻil moḻi peyarttatu. 4. tamiḻt tiṭṭam ceyalpaṭuttappaṭum aluvalakaṅkaḷiṉ ceyal 
pāṭṭu nilikaḷi āyvu ceytu aṟivuri nalkiyatu. 5. āṭcimoḻikkuḻuviṉ talivarāyiruntavariyē mānila muḻumikkum taṉi aluvalarāka amarttiṉar. piṉṉar ip patavi iyakkunar pataviyāka (Director of Tamil Levelopment) māṟiyatu. ivar aṉittintiyap paṇiyāḷarāvār. ivarukkuk kīḻppala aluvalar amarttappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ moḻipeyarpput toṭarpāṉa paṇikaḷ, āyvup paṇikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. 
caṅka kālattil āṭci moḻiyāka irunta tamiḻ kālappōkkil nilimi iḻantu pāli, camaṟkirutam, pāracīkam, arāpi, urutu, pōrttu kīciya, āṅkila moḻikaḷāl tākkuṇṭatu. āṅkilēyar kālattil oru paranta nilap parappil āṭci amippuc cīrpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, āṭci moḻi āṅkilamākavē iruntatu. mēli nāṭṭuk kalvimuṟi yāl aṉivarum kalvik kaṟkum vāyppu ēṟpaṭṭatu. tamiḻ tāḻntatu. 
1835-l mekkālēpirapu intiyarukku āṅkilak kalviyik kaṟpikkac ceytār. āṅkilak kalviyāl mēnāṭṭu āṭcimuṟi, aṟiviyal, paṇpāṭukaḷ intiyarāl aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl tāy moḻippaṟṟu mēliṭṭatu, tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ tamiḻi āṭcimoḻiyākka vēṇṭumeṉavīriṭṭeḻuntaṉar, 
camaṟkirutam tamiḻit taṉ mayamākkik koṇṭati māṟṟit taṉittamiḻiuyirppikkac ceytaṉar.paritimāṟ kaliñar, tiruvika vēta nāyakam piḷḷi , maṟimali aṭikaḷār, pārati, pāratitācaṉ cōm cuntara pāratiyār, kācupiḷḷi , vipulāṉantar mutaliya eṇṇiṟanta tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ tamiḻi ariyaṇiyēṟṟap pāṭupaṭṭaṉar. 
pala kaḻakaṅkaḷum, pala iyakkaṅkaḷum tamiḻi āṭcimoḻiyākka muṭukkamāyk kiḷampiṉa.kālamum kaṉintatu. 1920 ām āṇṭilēṟpaṭṭa iraṭṭi āṭcimuṟiyil makkaḷiṉ paṭinikarāḷikaḷ nāṭṭi āḷat toṭaṅkiṉar. caṭṭamaṉṟaṅkaḷil tamiḻk kural olittatu.

(a) vēḷāṇmi poruḷātāra mēmpāṭu: 
tamiḻaka makkaḷ tokiyil 50 viḻukkāṭu vēḷāṇmiyilum, mītamuḷḷa 40 viḻukkāṭu piṟa toḻilkaḷilum īṭupaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. mēlum tamiḻaka makkaḷtokikku ēṟpa vilinilaṅkaḷ illi. vēḷāṇmit toḻilil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷavarkaḷil 43 viḻukkāṭṭiṉar vēḷāṇ kūlikaḷā yuḷḷaṉar. kāraṇam pōtiya nilavuṭimikkārarkaḷ illi . 1948-l "perunila uṭamikkārar oḻippuc caṭṭam '' (jamīṉ oḻippuc caṭṭam koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatālum, 191l nila uccavarampuc caṭṭam koṇṭu varappaṭṭatālum tamiḻakattil ōraḷavuuḻavarkaḷ eṇṇikki atikarit tatu. 1948, 1959 ām āṇṭukaḷil koṇṭu varappaṭṭa "paṇṇiyāḷ kūli nirṇayac caṭṭaṅkaḷ'', 1952, 195ām āṇṭukaḷil koṇṭuvarap paṭṭa "uḻavar nalac caṭṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavi uḻavarkaḷiṉ naṉmikkum, pātukāppukkum mēmpāṭu aḷittaṉa.

mutal, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭak kālaṅkaḷil uṇavu uṟpattikku mutaliṭam aḷittatāl attiṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ muṭivil muṟiyē 3094, 5,000 kalleṭiṭaṉ tavaca uṟpatti ēṟpaṭṭatu. ikkāla vēḷāṇmi muṟiyil navīṉa muṟikaḷ kiyāḷappaṭṭaṉa. vētiyiyal muṟiyil tayārāṉa uraṅkaḷ, pūccikkolli maruntukaḷ, poṟukku vitikaḷ mutaliyaṉa payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. tīvira vēḷāṇmit tiṭṭam 
ittiṭṭam pōrṭu amippiṉ oru vaḻikāṭṭiṭattiṭṭamākum. cōtaṉi muṟiyil tañci māvaṭṭattil 1950-l it tiṭṭam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ēṉeṉṟāl im māvaṭṭattiltāṉ 14 ilakkam kuṟukkattil ēkkaril nel payiriṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ nel uṟpattiyil nāṉkil oru paṅku nel immāvaṭṭattil uṟpattiyākiṟatu. 
taṉippaṭṭa kuṭiyāṉavaṉ mutaṟkoṇṭu ūr, māvaṭṭam, mānilam, naṭuvaṇ āṭcikaḷ vari kūṭṭuṟavu muṟiyil uṇavu uṟpattip perukkum muṟikaḷik kiyāṇṭu uṇavu uṟpattiyip perukkuvatē intat tīvira vēḷāṇmit tiṭṭattiṉ mukāmi nōkkamākum. kuṭiyāṉavaṉiṉ āṇṭu varumāṉatti atikarikkac ceytu ataṉvaḻi avaṉ vāḻum palariṉ varumāṉattiyum atikappaṭuttiṉāl vēḷāṇ camit tuṟiyiṉ poruḷātāram mēmpaṭuvatōṭu ovvoru ūrum taṉṉiṟivup peṟumeṉpatum, itaṉāl vēḷāṇmiyil makkaḷukku naṇakkam ēṟpaṭum eṉpatum ittiṭṭattiṉ kuṟikkōḷākum. ittiṭṭat tiṉāl uṟpatti 20 viḻukkāṭu vari atikarikkumeṉṟu kaṇakkiṭap paṭṭatu. vivacāyak kaṭaṉutavi 
vēḷāṇmiyi kākkuvippataṟkāka vēḷāḷarukkuk kaṭaṉutavit tiṭṭamum aṟimukappaṭuttappaṭṭatu. 1956-ām āṇṭukaḷil, atāvatu iraṇṭāvatu aintu āṇṭut tiṭṭak kālattil 20 kōṭi rūpāy tamiḻaka vivacāyikaḷukkuk kaṭaṉaḷikkappaṭṭatu. 1962 mutal ituvē eṇpatu kōṭiyāka uyarttappaṭṭatu. itaṉi araciṭamiruntu peṟum 
vaḻivakikaḷ eḷitākkappaṭṭaṉa. nīrppācaṉa ēntukaḷ 
vēḷāṇmikku uyirnāṭiyāṉa nīrppācaṉam tamiḻakattil kāmarācarkālattil atikappaṭuttappaṭṭatu. nīrppācaṉaṅkaḷ 1. kīḻpavāṉit tiṭṭam

iḵtu 1959-l cuṭṭi muṭikkappaṭṭatu. inta nīrttēkkattāl maturi māvaṭṭattil 2000 kuṟukkam nilapparappup pācaṉam peṟṟup payaṉaṭikiṟatu. itaṟkāka aracu 330 kōṭi urupā celaviṭṭatu. 4. amarāvati nīrttēkkam

tiruvaṇṇāmali vēluār mutaliya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷukku II,000 kuṟukkam nilapparappukku ivvaṇiyiṉ nīr payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. itaṉi 1957 ām āṇṭu navamparil cuṭṭi muṭittaṉar. itaṟkāṉa celavu 1243 kōṭi urupā ākum. 6. kiruṭṭiṇakiri nīrttēkkam 
cēlam māvaṭṭattiṟkup payaṉpaṭum ittiṭṭattiṟku aracu urupā 2253 kōṭi celaviṭṭu 1957l kaṭṭi muṭittatu. itaṉāl ōrakuṟukkam nilapparappup payaṉaṭikiṟatu.

ciṟiya nīrppācaṉaṅkaḷ 
mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa periya nīrppācaṉat tiṭṭaṅkaḷit tavira pal ciṟiya nīrppācaṉat tiṭṭaṅkaḷum kāmarācar āṭcik kālattil niṟi vēṟiṉa. 
tamiḻakattil tōrāyamāka 27,000 ēri, kuḷaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟic cīrpaṭuttak kāmarācar kālattil urupā eṭṭu kōṭi celavāṉatu. kuṟippāka mūṉṟāvatu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭakkālattiṟkumuṉ (1951-1956) paḻiya ērikaḷum, kuḷaṅkaḷum cīrpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. putiya kiṇaṟukaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa, 
mēlum, vēḷāṇmi nīrppācaṉattiṟku vēṇṭiya nīr irikkum iyantiraṅkaḷ tārāḷamākak koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl tamiḻakattil uḷḷa payiriṭappaṭum150 kuṟukkam ilakkam nilattil mūṉṟilorupaṅku niliyākap payiriṭum vakiyil nīrppācaṉa ēntukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. āṟṟu nīrppācaṉam tōrāyamāka 150 ilakkam kuṟukkam nilapparappil naṭakkiṟatu. 
(ā) toḻil mēmpāṭu poruḷātāra vaḷarccikku vēḷāṇmikku aṭuttu mikavum mukāmi vāyntatu toḻil vaḷarcciyākum. toḻilkaḷ yāvum navīṉa toḻiṟcālikaḷiltāṉ vaḷarcci aṭikiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē ataṉik kaṉarakat toḻiṟcālikaḷ vaḷarcci yeṉṟum, ciṟurakat toḻiṟ cālikaḷ vaḷarcciyeṉṟum, pirittaṟiyalām. ivi iraṇṭumē aracē ēṟṟu naṭattum toḻiṟcālikaḷeṉṟum, taṉiyār ēṟṟu naṭattum toḻiṟcālikaḷeṉṟum pirittaṟiyalām. eṉavē, tamiḻakattil toḻil vaḷarcci intiyāvilē mūṉṟāvatu iṭattip piṭittuḷḷatu. 
1955-l irāmanātapuram ālaṅkuḷattil tamiḻakat toḻil vaḷarcci niṟuvaṉam toṭaṅkat tiṭṭamiṭappaṭṭatu. 1970-l iṅkuc cimeṇṭu toḻiṟcāli toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. inta niṟuvaṉam 47 toḻil tiṭṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭuḷḷatu. mutal aintāṇṭut tiṭṭak - kālattil urupā 12 ilakkamum, iraṇṭām aintāṇṭut tiṭṭak kālattil urupā 396 ilakkamum ciṟutoḻil vaḷarccikku otukkap paṭṭaṉa. iraṇṭām intāṇṭut tiṭṭak kālattil urupā 154 ilakkattil toḻil pēṭṭikaḷi uruvākka otukkappaṭṭaṉa. attakiya toḻiṟpēṭṭikaḷ kiṇṭi, virutunakar, nelli, mārttāṇṭam, maturi, tirucci, kāṭṭuppāṭi, cēlam, ampattūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uruvākkappaṭṭaṉa. kittaṟikaḷ 
tamiḻakattil mottam 427 ilakkam kittaṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. 1956-l * 'kittaṟi necavāḷar kūṭṭuṟavu caṅkam"" ēṟpaṭṭatu. itil 357 iyakkam

kittaṟikaḷ 1400 mukāmik kittaṟi necavāḷar kūṭṭuṟavu amippiṉ kīḻ ceyalpaṭṭaṉa. aracu necavāḷar mēmpāṭṭiṟkāka āṇṭoṉ ṟukku urupā 154 ilakkam otukkiyatu. kuṭicit toḻilkaḷ 
kuṭicit toḻil mēmpāṭṭiṟkāka aracu kuṭicit toḻilkaḷ mēmpāṭṭuk kaḻakam" oṉṟi 1960-l toṭaṅkiyatu. itaṉ . vaḻi pala āyiram vaḷarppura makkaḷukku vēlivāyppukaḷ kiṭittaṉa. calip pōkkuvarattu 
poruḷātāra vaḷarccikku mikavum payaṉpaṭuvatu cālappōkku varattu ākum, karppuraṅkaḷiyum, nakarppuraṅkaḷiyum iṇikkavum, cantikaḷi vaḷarkkavum cālip pōkkuvarattu iṉṟiyamiyāta tākiṟatu. itaṉ mēmpāṭṭiṟkāka aracu "tamiḻnāṭu cālip pōkku varattukkaḻakam" eṉṟa niṟuvaṉatti ēṟpaṭuttiyatu. kāmarācar āṭcik kālattil tamiḻakattil mottam 170ilakkam kilōmīṭṭar tūrattiṟku tamiḻnāṭu pēruntukaḷ ōṭiṉa. toḻiṟ caṅkaṅkaḷ 
toḻiṟ vaḷarcci yeṉpatu toḻiṟcālikaḷiṉ vaḷarcci eṉpatik kaṇṭōm. iyantirattāl avi iyakkappaṭṭālum, toḻilāḷarkaḷē itaṉi iyakkiṉar. eṉavē, toḻiṟpēṭṭi kaḷippōlavē toḻilāḷar kuṭiyiruppukaḷum perukiṉa. avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkeṉa mutalāḷikaḷi allatu aracik kēṭṭuppeṟat toḻiṟcaṅkaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. avi toḻilāḷar oṟṟumi, muṉṉēṟ ṟam mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu nāṭṭuṭimivaḷam, muṉṉēṟṟam, orumippāṭ ṭiṟkum pāṭupaṭṭaṉa. toḻiṟcālic caṭṭaṅkaḷ palavum tōṉṟiṉa. 
tamiḻ nāṭṭil toḻiṟcaṅkaṅkaḷiṉ tantiyeṉap pōṟṟappaṭu pavarkaḷ eṉem, cōci, pipi, vāṭiyā, ēci.ci. antōṉippiḷḷi , tiru.vi.ka, em.ci. irācā mutaliyōrāvar. tamiḻnāṭu aḷavilum, tēciya aḷavilum, ulaka nāṭukaḷ aḷavilum toḻilāḷar cikkalkaḷip pēcat tāḷikikaḷum, araciyal kaṭcikaḷumirukkiṉṟaṉa. toḻilāḷarē toḻiṟ caṅkat talivarāka iruppatē nalam. itu araciyalukku appāṟpaṭṭu iruttal mika, mikanalam. piṟpaṭṭōr mēmpāṭu 
piṟpaṭṭōr makkaḷ tokiyik kaṇakkiṭṭu, avarkaḷukkuḷ ḷēyē mikavum piṟpaṭṭōrik kaṇṭaṟintu avarkaḷukkuc caluki kaḷik koṭuttavar kāmarācar.pērāyak kaṭci āṭcikkuppiṉ ēṟpaṭṭa ti.mu.ka. āṭci kālattil mu.karuṇāniti itaṟkeṉa oru āṇiyatti amartti ataṉ parinturikaḷic ceyalpaṭutti, piṟpaṭṭōr nalattuṟi yic caṭṭappaṭi amittār.

kāmarācar āṭciyil tāḻttappaṭṭōrukkum, piṟpaṭṭōruk kum, mikavum piṟpaṭṭōrukkum vēṟupāṭiṉṟik kalvic calukikaḷum, aracuppaṇikaḷil paṭippu vayatuvarampu mutaliyavaṟṟilum camamākavē naṉmi ēṟpaṭṭatu. piṟpaṭṭōril 54 cātiyiṉar mikavum piṟpaṭṭōr (Most Backward) eṉak kaṇṭaṟintu taṉic calukikaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ivarkaḷil āṅkila aracāl kuṟṟapparampariyiṉar (Criminal Tribes) eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭavarkaḷiyum aracuppaṇikaḷil muḻumiyāka iṭampeṟac ceytār, mīṉavar nala mēmpāṭu 
mīṉavarkaḷ mikavum piṉtaṅkiyavarkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭiṟ kākkuṟinta viliyil 'pāplō muṟi iyantirap paṭakukaḷ' vāṅkit tarappaṭṭaṉa; itip pōlavē nilāṉ mīṉpiṭi valikaḷum malivu viliyil koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ittōṭu urupā 36 ilakkam niti avarkaḷiṉ poruḷātāra mēmpāṭṭiṟkāka otukkappaṭṭatu. nākap paṭṭiṉam, kaṭalūr, irāyapuram, līpuram ākiya tuṟimukaṅkaḷil mīṉ tuṟikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. piṭitta mīṉkaḷik keṭṭuppōkāmal pātukāttu vikka āṅkāṅkē kuḷircātaṉa aṟikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.

kalviyil tamiḻnāṭu ciṟanta muṉṉēṟṟamaṭintatu. kalvittuṟi ivariṭamē iruntatu. varumāṉa uccavarampu illāmal ellā māṇavarukkum paḷḷi iṟuti vakuppuvari ilavacak kalviyaḷittār, kāmarācar kālattil tara makkaḷ tokiyuḷḷa ūrkaḷil uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷ toṭaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. paktavatcalam 300 makkaḷ tokiyuḷḷa ūrkaḷilellām uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷit toṭaṅkiṉār. toḻil nuṭpappaḷḷikaḷi atika eṇṇikkiyil toṭaṅkiṉār. cēlattil putiya poṟiyiyal kallūriyit toṭaṅkiṉār. tiruccirāppaḷḷiyil vaṭṭārap poṟiyiyal kallūri toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. 
mūṉṟukaḷ cuṟṟu vaṭṭārattiṟku oru toṭakkappaḷḷiyit toṭaṅkiya kāmarācar aṭiccuvaṭṭip piṉpaṟṟi ivar orukal cuṟṟu vaṭṭārattiṟku oru toṭakkappaḷḷiyit toṭaṅkiṉār. avaṟṟil 41 āciriyarkaḷip putitāka amarttiṉār. āciriyikaḷukku ūrppuṟaṅkaḷil 750 kuṭiyiruppukaḷik kaṭṭiṉār. āciriyarkaḷiṉ ōyvu peṟum akaviyi 55liruntu 58 āka aracu ūḻiyarkaḷukku iruppatip pōlavē uyarttiṉār. putitāka aintu aracuk kalik kallūrikaḷit toṭaṅkiṉār, āṟu taṉiyār kalluārikaḷit toṭaṅka aṉumati aḷittār. māli nērak kalluārikaḷum toṭaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ceṅkalpaṭṭu, kōvi, tirunelvēli ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil putiya maruttuvak kalluārikaḷ toṭaṅkappaṭṭaṉa, ti.2.

pērāyakkaṭci nāṭṭiṉ viṭutalikkup pal āyiram iṉṉuyiri īntatu. eṉavē, viṭutalip peṟṟa nāṭṭil kalvi, poruḷātāram mutaliyavaṟṟil mika akkariyōṭu kaṟippaṭātakikaḷōṭu amiccar kaḷum, piṟarum pāṭupaṭṭaṉar. nāṭṭiṉ vaḷarccikku aṭittaḷamāka aintāṇṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷ amintaṉa. tamiḻakattiṉ tavappayaṉākak karumavīrar kāmarācar toṭarntu mūṉṟumuṟi mutalvarāki mutal mūṉṟu aintāṇṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷic ceyalpaṭuttiṉār. aḻikkap peṟṟatu. aṇṇā itaṉi māṟṟit tamiḻ nāṭu' eṉṟu peyariṭṭār, tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakam ki.pi. 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ ''tamiḻ nāṭu' eṉap peyar peṟṟatu! 
1955 ām āṇṭil koṇṭu varappaṭṭa intuttirumaṇac caṭṭam" eṉpatit tirutti cuyamariyātit tirumaṇac caṭṭam yeṉa veḷiyiṭṭār. tanti periyārāl toṭaṅkappaṭṭa cuyamariyāti iyakkam 1925-liruntu naṭatti vittat "taṉmāṉat tirumaṇaṅkaḷum" itaṉpaṭi cellupaṭiyāyiṉa. ittirumaṇattil aiyartiṭiyātu. ammi mitittal, aruntatippārttal, tīvalam varutal mutaliya caṭaṅkukaḷ kiṭiyātu. tāli kaṭṭalām, allatu mōtiram māṟṟikkoḷḷalām. allatu māli māṟṟikkoḷḷalām. ituvum intut tirumaṇattip pōlavē caṭṭappaṭi cellum. tēvippaṭṭāl pativu ceytu koḷḷalām. kulam, kōttiram, cāti kaṭṭut tiṭṭaṅkaḷ itil illi, varuṇācarumappaṭi pirāmaṇaṉ uyarntavaṉ. avaṉ tāliyeṭuttuk koṭuttāltāṉ tirumaṇam puṉitamāṉatākum eṉṟa cāti ēṟṟattāḻvi oḻippatē it tirumaṇattiṉ nōkkameṉṟār. aṇṇā itaṉi ''vāḻkki oppantam'' eṉakkūṟi āṇum, peṇṇum camameṉap paṟicāṟṟiṉār. taṅkap patakkam vaḻaṅkutal 
oru āti tirāviṭa maṇamakaṉ allatu maṇamakaḷ vēṟṟuccāti maṇamakaṉ allatu maṇamakaḷit tirumaṇam ceytu koṇṭāl inta maṇamakkaḷukku aracu taṅkappatakkam vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭatti aṟimukap paṭuttiṉār. ittiṭṭattil 1968 mutal 1976-kkuṭpaṭṭa kālak kaṭṭattil 417 tampatikaḷ taṅkappatakkam peṟṟārkaḷ. 
uruvavaḻipāṭu intuc camayattiṉ aṭippaṭik kōṭpāṭā kum. camayam taṉippaṭṭōriṉ aṭippaṭi urimiyākum. āṉāl, aracu aluvalakaṅkaḷil intukkaḷ allātavarkaḷum paṇiyāṟṟuvatāl uruva vaḻippāṭṭil nampikkiyillātavarkaḷiyum nampikkik koḷḷu māṟu ceyvatip pōl aracu aluvalakaṅkaḷil intuk kaṭavuḷkaḷiṉ uruvappaṭaṅkaḷi vittu vaḻipaṭuvatākum. eṉavē, ittakiya uruvap paṭaṅkaḷi aracu aluvalakaṅkaḷiliruntu akaṟṟi viṭavēṇṭumeṉa aṇṇā āṇiyoṉṟip pōṭṭār.

aṉṟē mu. karuṇānitiyi mutalamiccarākkiṉar.

tāynila varalāṟu 
mu. karuṇāniti (10.2.1969 - 31.1.1976) cātaṉikaḷ a) kumukāya muṉṉēṟṟattil: 1. tīṇṭāmi oḻippukkuṟṟac caṭṭam, civil urikamap pātukāppuccaṭṭam 
(Protection of Civil Rights Act) ākiya tīṇṭāmikku etirāṉa caṭṭaṅkaḷic ceyalpaṭuttit tīṇṭāmikkuṟṟaṅkaḷic ceyvōr, tūṇṭuvōr, uṭantiyāka iruppōr mutaliyōrik karuṇāniti taṇṭittār. itaṟkeṉa 'paṟakkum paṭikaḷ' eṉum kāval paṭikaḷi amarttit tīṇṭāmik kuṟṟam naṭakkum iṭaṅkaḷukkum, naṭakka mukāntiramuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷukkum aṉuppik kuṟṟavāḷikaḷit taṇṭit tum, kuṟṟam nikaḻāmal taṭukkavum ceytār, pātikkappaṭṭavarukku vaḻakkaṟiñarkaḷi amartti vaḻakkāṭa utaviṉār. 2 intu aṟaniliyac caṭṭattit tirutti, aṉittuc cātiyiṉarum arccakarākum caṭṭa muṉvarivu (1970) 
arccakar patavi cāti aṭippaṭiyil amintatu. pirāmaṇaṉ tavira maṟṟa cātiyiṉar ip patavikku varamuṭiyātu. kaṭavuḷ vīṭṭil kōyilil cātikūṭātu eṉpatu timuka viṉ kōṭpāṭu. eṉavē, pūci vitimuṟikaḷik kaṟṟut tēṟum evarum arccakarāka varalāmeṉṉum nōkkattil " aṉittuc cātiyiṉarum arccakarākalām" eṉṟa caṭṭa muṉvarivikkaruṇānitikkoṇṭuvaramuṉintār. itaṟkāka 1959 ām āṇṭu intuc camaya aṟaniliyac caṭṭattil uḷḷa vitikaḷ55, 56 ākiya iraṇṭiyum tirutti allatu māṟṟi amikka vēṇṭum eṉa muṭivu ceytār. 
viti 55-l intu camaya aṟaniliya aṟakkaṭṭaḷiyiṉpaṭi, intuk kōyilkaḷ, maṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil paṇiyāṟṟuvōr vāḻiyaṭi vāḻiyāka "'takappaṉukkuppiṉ makaṉ" eṉṟa muṟiyil paṇi ceyyavēṇṭum. ip paṇikaḷil āṭkaḷi amarttum poṟuppu aṟaṅkāvalarkaḷukkē uṇṭu. itil aracu taliyiṭak kūṭātu." eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu; vitika-l arccakar pataviyum ittakiyatē. appatavi veṟṟiṭamāki viṭṭālum, vāricu illāmal pōyviṭṭālum ataṉi nirappa vēṇṭiya poṟuppu aṟaṅkāvalaruṭiyatu. kuṟṟaṅkuṟikaḷēṟ paṭṭālum kuṟṟavāḷiyit arccakaṉi taṇṭippatu tuṇi āṇiyar poṟuppu. itil aracu taliyiṭa urimi illi eṉat tiṭṭa vaṭṭamākak kūṟappaṭṭu uḷḷatāl, ic caṭṭa vitikaḷi māṟṟiṉāl vēṇṭiya takutikaḷiyuṭiyavari arccakarāka amartta muṭiyum eṉpati yuṇarnta karuṇāniti, mutalil ic caṭṭattit tiruttum caṭṭa muṉvarivic caṭṭamaṉṟattil 1970 ām āṇṭu koṇṭuvantār. 
āṇiyarperpavāḷiyit (arccaṭiyatu. kuṟṟaṅku 
viṭutavikkuppiṉ tamiḻakam 
11] - 
- 
- ''pala āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē āti - tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ kōyil nuḻivu ēṟpaṭṭu viṭṭatu. 1950 ām āṇṭu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa araciyal caṭṭattil evarukkum enta oru paṇiyum avaruṭiya cātiyiṉ peyarāl maṟukkamuṭiyātu eṉṟum, araciyal caṭṭaviti 7l tīṇṭāmi oḻikkappaṭṭatu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatāl āṇṭavaṉ muṉ aṉivarum camam eṉṟa vitiyi nilināṭṭavē intac caṭṭattik koṇṭuvarukiṟōm'' eṉṟu karuṇāniti kūṟiṉār. caṭṭam niṟivēṟi yatu. kuṭiyaracut talivar opputalukkāka aṉuppappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, arccakarkaḷ, marapukaḷukkum, ākamatarumaṅkaḷukkum etirāka uḷḷatāl iccaṭṭatti nirākarikkak kōri tilli ucca nayaṉmi maṉṟattil vaḻakkut toṭuttaṉar. itaṉi ēṟṟu, ucca nayaṉmi maṉṟam ic caṭṭatti nirākarittatu. timuka aracu tam muyaṟciyil tōṟṟatu. 3. āti-tirāviṭarkaḷik kōyil aṟaṅkāvalarkaḷākkutal: 
āti - tirāviṭar kōyil nuḻivu ēṟkaṉavē nikaḻntuviṭṭatu. araciyal caṭṭappaṭi tīṇṭāmi illi. eṉavē, aṟaṅkāvalarkaḷāka avarkaḷi niyamippatu ti.mu.ka. aracukku eḷitākiviṭṭatu. mutalil 49 āti tirāviṭarkaḷ periya kōyilkaḷiṉ aṟaṅkāvalarkaḷāka amarttappaṭṭaṉar. 4. piṟpaṭṭōr mēmpāṭu: 
piṟpaṭṭōr (backward Class) yeṉṟa collum ataṟkāṉa viḷakka uriyum mutalil intiya araciyal caṭṭattil illi , 1951-l intiya araciyal caṭṭam mutaṉmuṟiyāka tiruttam ceytapiṉtāṉ intiya araciyal caṭṭam15 (4) 16 (4) vitikaḷil cērkkappaṭṭatu. ataṟkuc cariyāṉa poruḷum, viḷakka uriyum tantavar intiya araciyal caṭṭac ciṟpi ṭākṭar.pi. ār. ampētkār avarkaḷē! 
''kumukāya niliyilum, kalviyilum piṉtaṅkiyavarkaḷ piṟpaṭṭava rāvar" eṉpatu. avar tanta viḷakkamākum. araciyal caṭṭattil iṭam peṟṟa ivarkaḷukku mānila aracukaḷ taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil muṉṉēṟṟat tiṟkāṉa ēntukaḷic ceyyalāmeṉṟatāl piṟpaṭṭac cātikaḷiṉ paṭṭiyali tamiḻnāṭu aracu tayārittatu. itaṟkākac caṭṭanātaṉ āṇikkuḻuvi1971-l ēṟpaṭutti piṟpaṭṭa cātikaḷ evi eviyeṉak kaṇṭaṟintu, avaṟṟiṉ mēmpāṭṭiṟkāka ti.mu.ka. aracupāṭupaṭṭatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ motta makkaḷtokiyil 54 viḻukkāṭu piṟpaṭṭōr iruppatākak kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭatu.

caṭṭanātaṉ āṇik kuḻuviṉ parinturiyil piṟpaṭṭōril ''micuvum piṟpaṭṭōr" (Most Backward) tōrāyamāka 25 viḻukkāṭu, uḷḷatākak kūṟappaṭṭiruntatu. itaṉaṭippaṭiyil ti.mu.ka., āṭcik kālattil 1968-l mikavum piṟpaṭṭōr [Most Backward Class) paṭṭiyal tayārikkappaṭṭatu. mēlum pala cātikaḷ piṟpaṭṭōr paṭṭiyalil cērkkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ piṟpaṭṭa cātikaḷiliruntu iculām. kiṟittuvam māṟiya cātikaḷum piṟpaṭṭavarkaḷākavē kaṇakkiṭap paṭṭaṉar. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka nāṭār cātiyiliruntu kiṟittuvam māṟiyavar kaḷum, intu nāṭārkaḷum piṟpaṭṭavarākavē koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. āṉāl, tāḻttappaṭṭa cātiyiliruntu kiṟittuvam māṟiyōr piṟpaṭṭa varākavē atāvatu muṉṉēṟiyavarkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. itu ēṉ eṉṟu puriyavilli . 
mu. karuṇāniti ti.mu.ka. āṭciyi amittavuṭaṉēyē 1959-l ''piṟpaṭṭōr nalattuṟiyit tōṟṟuvittār. nayaṉmik kaṭci 1920-l āṭcikku varuvataṟku muṉpē piraṭṭāṉiyar toḻilāḷar nalat tuṟiyit (Labour #WelfarE [EpartiTIEnt) tōṟṟuvittaṉar. omantarar irāmacāmi reṭṭiyār mutalvarāṉavuṭaṉ 1947-lēyē at tuṟiyi aricaṉa nalattuṟi toḻilāḷar nalattuṟiyeṉa iraṇṭākap pirittār. 1947-liruntu karuṇāniti mutalvarākum 1969 ām āṇṭuvari aricaṉa nalattuṟiyāl piṟpaṭṭōr nalamum kavaṉikkappaṭṭu vantatu. āṉāl, karuṇāniti āṭcikku vantavuṭaṉ aricaṉa nalattuṟiyiliruntu pirittu ''piṟpaṭṭōr nalattuṟi' eṉum putiya tuṟiyi ēṟpaṭutti, ataṟkuk kālañceṉṟa eṉ.vi.naṭarācaṉi amiccarākkiṉār. ittuṟikkup peruntokiyi otukki araciṉ cellakkuḻantiyāka ataṉip pēṇikkāttār. kuṟippākap piṟpaṭṭōr kalvi mēmpāṭṭiṟkāka mikap periya toki otukkappaṭṭatu. atu māṇavariṉ utavittokikkum, viṭutikaḷukkum celaviṭappaṭṭatu. 5. ciṟītaṉa tiruttac caṭṭam(1958) 
oru peṇṇit tirumaṇam ceyyumpōtu varataṭcaṉi vāṅku vatum kēṭpatum kuṟṟameṉappaṭṭaṉa. maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷ peṟṟōr allatu uṟaviṉariṭamiruntu nēraṭiyākavō allatu maṟimukamākavō ciṟītaṉam allatu varataṭcaṉi vāṅkiṉāl avarkaḷ āṟu tiṅkaḷ mutal iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ vari ciṟi taṇṭaṉip peṟuvar eṉpatu ic caṭṭattiṉ karupporuḷākum. 
mēlum, kuṟṟaviyal caṭṭam 174-l 3iṉpaṭi oru peṇ tirumaṇa māki ēḻu āṇṭukkuḷ varataṭcaṇik koṭumiyāl iṟantupaṭṭāl avaḷ cāvukkuk karaṇiyamāṉavarkaḷ taṇṭaṉikkuriyavarāvar. 6. karukkalippuc caṭṭam (1971)

āti - tirāviṭap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ uṇavu, uṭi, uṟiviṭam ākiya aṭippaṭit tēvikaḷik kūṭap peṟāmal vāḻntu vantaṉar, nakarppuṟaṅkaḷil vāḻnta am makkaḷ paḷḷamāṉa, cērum, cakatiyumuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil kuṭicikaḷil vāḻntaṉar. inta vāḻviṭaṅkaḷiyum, potuvāka tamiḻnāṭu muḻuvatilumuḷḷa āti tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ vāḻ viṭaṅkaḷiyum mēmpaṭuttavē "tamiḻnāṭu āti tirāviṭar vīṭṭuvacati vāriyam"' 1974-l toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. itaṟku naṭuvaṇ āṭciyum peruntokiyi māṉiyamāka vaḻaṅki vantatu.

māntaṉi māntaṉ vaṇṭiyil vittu iḻuttuc cellum kivaṇṭi allatu naravaṇṭi (Hand Pulling Rickhaw) makkaḷāṭciyil irukkakkūṭātu eṉṟa aṭippaṭiyil itaṉit timuka aracu oḻittatu. ivvāṟu 2000 naravaṇṭikaḷi oḻittu, attoḻilāḷarkaḷukku tuvic cakkara vaṇṭikaḷi (Cycle Rickhaws) aracu ilavayamākak koṭuttatu. itaṟkākak koṭimaṉam paṭittōrkaḷum urupā 25 ilakkam koṭuttaṉar. perumpālāṉavarkaḷ naravaṉcaṭikaḷic contakkārariṭam miruntu vāṭakikkup peṟṟē iḻuttu, campātittu vāḻntaṉar. attakiya contakkārarukkut talā urupā 200 aracu iḻappāṭāka vaḻaṅkiyatu. 
ivvāṟu intiyāvilēyē piccikkārari oḻittatum, naravaṇṭikaḷi oḻittatum tamiḻnāṭutāṉ. 10. ēḻi, ētilikarukku ilavaca maruttuva utavikaḷ 
a) kaṇ cikicci mukāmkaḷ 
ūrppuraṅkaḷil mukāmkaḷi amittu ēḻi makkaḷukku ilavaya kaṇ cikiccikaḷi aḷippatōṭu, kaṇ kaṇṇāṭikaḷiyum avarka ḷukku vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭatti 1972-l toṭaṅkiṉar. ittakiya tiṭṭattik kaṇṇoḷi vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭam' eṉṟaṉar. ittakiya tiṭṭam pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ āṭcik kālattilumiruntatu. āṉāl timuka, āṭciyil muṭukkamākkappaṭṭu, makkaḷiṭiyē varavēṟkappaṭṭatu. itaṟkākap potumakkaḷē muṉ vantu oṉpatu ilakkam urupā naṉkoṭiyum aḷittaṉar. itil aintu ilakkam urupāyi emēem.

itil toḻil vaḷarcci, vēḷāṇmi vaḷarcci mutaliyaṉa aṭaṅkum. cēlam urukku āli, ālaṅkuḷam cimeṇṭu toḻiṟcāli, tamiḻnāṭu ciṟutoḻil vaḷarccik kaḻakam mutaliyaṉa toḻil vaḷarcciyil mukāmip paṅkēṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟāl pala āyiram pēr vēli vāyppum peṟṟaṉar. 
vēḷāṇmi vaḷarccikkākap putiya vēḷāṇmic cākupaṭi muṟikaḷ kiyāḷappaṭṭaṉa. cātāraṇak kalappikaḷit tavirttu iyantirakkalappikaḷ uḻuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻnāṭu vēḷāṇmik kaḻakam" ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ataṉmūlam 300 ṭirāk ṭarkaḷ māṉiyaviliyil aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. vēḷāṇmi ārāyccik kācup putukkōṭṭiyil aṇṇā paṇṇi ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. pala iṭaṅkaḷil vitippaṇṇikaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa. nīr iṟikkum miṉcāra iyantiraṅkaḷukkuk kaṭaṉaḷikkappaṭṭatu. ivaṟṟāl tamiḻ nāṭṭil "pacumippuraṭci ēṟpaṭṭatu. 
mēlum niliyāṉa vēḷāṇmi vaḷarccikkup pala nīrppācaṉa tiṭṭaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. miṉ taṭṭuppāṭṭit tavirkka 170 kōṭi urupāyil miṉ uṟpatti niliyaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. "

13. āṭcimoḻi mēmpāṭu 
pērāyakkaṭci āṭcik kālattil tamiḻnāṭu āṭcimoḻic caṭṭam 1956-l niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭatu. atu 19. 1. 1957 mutal ceyalpaṭat toṭaṅkiyatu. āṭcimoḻic caṭṭattiṉ kūṟupāṭukaḷ 1. araciṉ maṟuuttiravu varumvari āṅkilam toṭarntu aluvalakak 
kāriyaṅkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭutal vēṇṭum. 2. araciyal caṭṭattiṉ 46vatu 347vatu pirivukaḷukku uḷaṟiṉṟi aluval 
muṟi kāriyaṅkaḷi āṅkila muṟiyil toṭarntu payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum 3. tamiḻil veḷiyiṭa vēṇṭiyavaṟṟukku tamiḻiyē payaṉpaṭuttalām. 4. caṭṭamūlaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟit tamiḻilēyē payaṉpaṭuttalām, 
itaṟkāṉa aṟivikkiyi mānila aracu muṉkūṭṭiyē veḷiyiṭa vēṇṭum. caṭṭa mūlaṅkaḷāvaṉa: 
tamiḻnāṭu caṭṭamaṉṟattil koṇṭu varappeṟum caṭṭamuṉ varivukaḷ, allatu caṭṭat tiruttaṅkaḷ intiya araciyal caṭṭattiṉ kīḻp pēraṟikkiyāka pirakaṭaṉam veḷiyiṭappeṟum avacara kālac caṭṭaṅkaḷ, naṭuvaṇ āṭciyāl veḷiyiṭappaṭum caṭṭam, mānila aracāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa caṭṭam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kīḻ mānila aracāl piṟappikkappaṭum āṇikaḷ vitikaḷ, oḻuṅkumuṟi vitikaḷ, tuṇi vitikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟit tamiḻilēyē veḷiyiṭal vēṇṭum; enta oru aṟivikkiyum, iru avikaḷilum vittu vivātittu muṭivu kāṇa vēṇṭum, 
ivvāṟu ac caṭṭattil kaṇmūṭittaṉamākat tamiḻi maṭṭumē aluvalakak kāriyaṅkaḷil ceyalpaṭuttāmal āṅkilatti vēṇṭiya iṭattil payaṉpaṭutta vaḻi ceyyum vakiyum kūṟappeṟṟatu. itaṉi virivupaṭutti, āṅkilatti nīkkit tamiḻi maṭṭumē payaṉpaṭuttum. aṭuttak kaṭṭappaṇiyil aṭuttu vanta tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻaka āṭci muṭukkamāyc ceyalpaṭṭatu. 
1947-l aṇṇā mutalvarāṉavuṭaṉ paḷḷikaḷilirunta inti āciriyarkaḷukku māṟṟup paṇikaḷ aḷittu intiyip paḷḷikaḷi liruntu nīkkiṉār. āṭcimoḻi tamiḻ maṭṭumēyeṉa aṟivittār. āṭci moḻi vaḷarccikkāka 1968- l ka tamiḻ vaḷarccittuṟi" eṉṟa oru taṇittuṟiyi ēṟpaṭutti, ati muḻuvaḷarcciyaṭinta tuṟipōl ceyalpaṭuvataṟku vēṇṭiya ellā ēntukaḷiyum ceytār; 1857 mutal 1957variyil oru nūṟṟāṇṭu kālattil veḷiyāṉa tamiḻ nūlkaḷit tokuttu ''accāṉa tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal" veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu.

tāynilavaralāṟu 
1971-l "āṭcic col akaramutali'' mūṉṟām patippāka veḷiyiṭap paṭṭatu. 
1970 ām āṇṭu kūṭiya tamiḻ vaḷarcci ārāycci maṉṟam' tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟi eḻuti veḷiyiṭa muṭivu ceytatu. ataṟkut talimi tāṅkiya mutalvar mu.karuṇāniti tamiḻaka varalāṟṟi eḻuta oru kuḻuvi amittār. ak kuḻuvukkut tamiḻaṟiñar mu. varatarācaṉār talivarākkappaṭṭār. ak kuḻu, varalāṟṟu aṟiñarkaḷiṭamiruntu 
āyvuk kaṭṭurikaḷip peṟṟut tamiḻaka varalāṟṟit tolpaḻaṅkālam mutal iṉṟu vari pala maṭalaṅkaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭatu. kaṭṭuriyāḷarkaḷ koṭukkum kaṭṭurikaḷi ik kuḻu āyntapiṉ avaṟṟi nūlākap patittu veḷiyiṭap patippāciriyarkaḷ nirantarap paṇiyāḷarkaḷāka amarttappaṭṭaṉar. ippaṇi, tamiḻ vaḷarccit tuṟiyil ceyalpaṭṭatu. 
tolpaḻaṅkālam, caṅkakālam, pallavar - pāṇṭiyar kālam, cōḻap peruvēntar kālam, pāṇṭiyar perum vēntarkālam, mutaliya maṭalaṅkaḷākat tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu' veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. 
aṭuttapaṭiyākap paḷḷikaḷukkum, kallūrikaḷukkum tamiḻil pāṭanūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭum tiṭṭamum ceyalpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ataṟkākat "tamiḻ nāṭṭuppāṭanūl niṟuvaṉam" eṉṟa niṟuvaṉamum ēṟpaṭṭatu. ciṟanta āṅkila nūlkaḷ tamiḻil moḻip peyarkkappaṭṭaṉa. itip pōlavē, tamiḻil uḷḷa nūlkaḷ āṅkilattilum moḻip peyarkkap paṭṭaṉa. tamiḻmoḻiyi aṟiyātār tamiḻik kaṟṟuṇarumpaṭi ēntukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. piṟa mānilaṅkaḷiluḷḷa palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷil tami ḻukkāka tuṟi ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. āṇṭutōṟum ciṟanta nūlkaḷukkup paricu koṭuttu eḻuttāḷarkaḷ ūkkuvikkappaṭṭaṉar. itit tavira ciṟanta āyvu nūlkaḷip paṭippavarkaḷukkup paṇa utaviyum ceyyap paṭṭatu. nalivuṟṟat tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷukku ōyvūtiyamum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. 
āṇṭutōṟum taliciṟanta aṟiñarkaḷukkup pāratitācaṉ virutu, periyār virutu. aṇṇā virutu mutaliyaṉa aḷittu avarkaḷi ūkku vittaṉar. ciṟanta tamiḻppulavar oruvari "aracavik kaviñar" eṉum pataviyilamarttit paḻantamiḻar āṭciyi malarac ceytatu ti.mu.ka aracu. payiṟci vakuppukaḷ 
30.11.1957 mutal āṭci moḻit tiṭṭam tamiḻakattil naṭi muṟikku vantatu. atuvari āṅkilam terintavarkaḷē aracup paṇikaḷil iruntaṉar. avarkaḷukkut tamiḻ terintirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟa kaṭṭāya milli. eṉavē, āṭcimoḻi tamiḻ' eṉṟavuṭaṉ tamiḻ teriyāta paṇiyāḷarkaḷukkut tamiḻil payiṟci aḷikkavēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. 
ceṉṉi mānakarilum, māvaṭṭaṅkaḷilum, tamiḻp payiṟci vakuppukaḷ" naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. ittakiya payiṟci vakuppukaḷ 1984 ām āṇṭu cūṉ tiṅkaḷil toṭaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ivi patiṉōru tiṅkaḷkaḷ

naṭantaṉa. payiṟciyiṉ muṭivil tērvum naṭattappaṭṭatu. ittakiya payiṟci vakuppukaḷ 1964 mutal 177vari toṭarntu naṭantaṉa. 
intiya āṭcippaṇi (I.A.S) intiyak kāvalpaṇi (I.P.S) mutaliya paṇiyāḷarkaḷ tamiḻakattiṟku otukkappaṭumpōtu avarkaḷil tamiḻ teriyātavarkaḷumiruntaṉar. ittakiyap payiṟciyāl avarkaḷ tamiḻil paṭikkavum, eḻutavum, kōppukaḷip paṭittu āṇi iṭavum tēṟiṉar. avarkaḷiṉ niruvākap payiṟciyōṭu tamiḻp payiṟcit tiṭṭamum cērkkappaṭṭatu. 
tamiḻvaḷarcci iyakkunar, māvaṭṭa āṭciyar aluvalakaṅkaḷi yum, tuṟit talimi aluvalakaṅkaḷiyum, kālavāriyākat tamiḻil āṭci aluvalakaṅkaḷ naṭappatip paricōtittu āyvu ceytār. itaṉ curaṇiyamākat tamiḻ vaḷarccittuṟiyil taṉi aluvalarkaḷ eṇṇikki : atikarittatu. eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam 
aluvalakaṅkaḷil tamiḻvaḻi kāriyaṅkaḷi virintu ceyalpaṭutta vēṇṭi, tamiḻil taṭṭaccu iyantiraṅkaḷ, tamiḻil curukkeḻuttu muṟi mutaliyaṉa naṭimuṟikku vantaṉa. tamiḻmoḻiyil uyir, uyirmey, mey mutaliya eḻuttukaḷiṉ eṇṇikki atikamiruppatālum, avaṟṟiṉ ōcikaḷukkēṟpak kuṟil neṭilkaḷiruppatālum taṭṭaccu, curukkeḻut tiṟku ottuvarumāṟu eḻuttuc cīrtiruttam ceyya vēṇṭiyatu avaciya māyiṟṟu. itu tamiḻ āṭcimoḻikkuttāṉ eṉṟālum, pulavar perumakkaḷ itaṟkut taṭiyāka niṉṟaṉar. eṉavē, avarkaḷiṉ karuttu orumippāṭṭiṉip peṟuvataṟkāka aracu tamiḻ eḻuttuc cīrtiruttak kuḻu'' oṉṟi ēṟpaṭutti, ataṟku ""makāvittuvāṉ mē.vī. vēṇu kōpālap piḷḷi avarkaḷit talivarākkiyatu. ik kuḻu tamiḻ vaḷarcci iyakkunar mēṟpārviyiṉ kīḻ iyaṅkiyatu. pulavar perumakkaḷ itaṉ uṟuppiṉarkaḷāka iruntaṉar. kuṟukiyakālamē ikkuḻu āyviṉi mēṟkoṇṭatu. kuḻuttalivar nōyvāypaṭṭamiyālum, ti.mu.ka. āṭci māṟṟattālum ikkuḻu ceyaliḻantu niṉṟatu. tamiḻ āṭcimoḻi vaḷarcci varalāṟu 
1996-l naṭanta potut tērtalil tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam veṟṟip peṟṟu āṭci amittatu. kaliñar mu. karuṇāniti nāṉkām muṟiyāka mutalamiccarāṉār. ivar talimiyilaminta amiccaravi yil 25 amiccarkaḷ iṭam peṟṟaṉar. inta amiccaravi 13-5-1ppati | mutal 235.1996 vari pataviyiliruntatu. ik kāla iṭiveḷiyil tamiḻ moḻi vaḷarccikku ivvāṭci āṟṟiya paṇikaḷ aḷaviṭaṟkariyatākum, tamiḻ āṭcimoḻi - tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭut tuṟiyiṉ amiccarāka iruntavar āṉṟaviḻnta cāṉṟōrāṉa tamiḻkkuṭimakaṉ tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarcciyit taṉatu uyir mūccākakkoṇṭu ceyalāṟṟiṉār. tirāviṭa

muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻaka āṭcip pataviyēṟṟa 13-5-196 - nāḷaṉṟē tamiḻ āṭcimoḻi, tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭuttuṟi taṉiyākap pirintu ceyalpaṭat toṭaṅkiyatu. amiccariṉ āḷukikkuṭpaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ ciṟappāṉavi tamiḻp palkalikkaḻakam, ulakat tamiḻ ārāycci niṟuvaṉam, tamiḻ vaḷarcci iyakkakam, centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutalit tiṭṭa iyakkakam ākiyavi kuṟippiṭattakkaṉavākum. iviyāvumē tamiḻ moḻi vaḷarccikku vērkaḷākum. tamiḻ vaḷarcci karutip palvēṟu paṇikaḷ mēṟkoḷḷappeṟṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, akaramutalikaḷ tayārittal, ulakat tamiḻ ārāycci niṟuvaṉam, kalikkaḷañciyam, centamiḻc coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutali, tamiḻ taṭṭaccuppoṟikaḷi aluvalakaṅ kaḷil payaṉpaṭuttutal, tamiḻ curukkeḻuttu nūl, tamiḻp palkalik kaḻakam toṭaṅkiyatu, tamiḻ vaḷarcci iyakkakam mēṉmippaṭuttutal, tamiḻil akavi (Pager] tamiḻil maruttuvam, tamiḻil kōyil vaḻipāṭu, tirāviṭap palkalikkaḻakam amittal, tuṟic ciṟappu akarāti maḻaliyar paḷḷikaḷil tamiḻ, vāṉoliyil tamiḻ, viḷamparappalaciyil tamiḻ, mutaliyaṉa kuṟippiṭattakkaṉavākum. 
akavi mutirnta tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷukku nitiyutaviyaḷittal, añcal vaḻik kalviyil tamiḻ vaḻippāṭaṅkaḷi naṭattutal, ceṉṉiyil aṟiviyal niṟuvaṉam amittal, āṭci moḻik karuttaraṅkukaḷi naṭattutal, tamiḻicip paḷḷi, tamiḻ ici nāṭakamaṉṟam, ulakat tamiḻar amippukaḷiṉ oruṅkiṇippu, vaḷartikaḷil tamiḻ, karunāṭakattil tamiḻ, tamiḻic cemmoḻiyākkum muṭukkam, tamiḻil aṟiviyal nūlkaḷip paṭittal, tamiḻil ciṟanta nūlkaḷukkup paricu vaḻaṅkum tiṭṭam, tērvāṇiyam naṭattum tērvukaḷ tamiḻ vaḻiyil naṭattutal, ciṟanta tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷiṉ nālkaḷi nāṭṭuṭimiyākkutal, ciṟanta nūlkaḷi yāccu niti utavi aḷittal, tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟṟu varivut tiṭṭatti virivupaṭuttutal mutaliya ariya paṇikaḷum virintu 
mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. akaramutalikaḷ 
akaramutalikaḷ akkālattil nikaṇṭukaḷ eṉappeṟṟaṉa. ki.pi. 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭil uruvākiya nikaṇṭukaḷ iruvakippaṭum. avi oru collukkāṉa pala poruḷkaḷik kūṟum akaramutali, oru collukkāṉa etirmaṟiyik kūṟum akaramutali yeṉpaṉavākum. oru col palporuḷ akarāti kaviti vaṭivilāṉatu; ituvari 22 akarātikaḷ itip pōl uruvākiyuḷḷaṉa. oru collukku etirāṉa coṟkaḷik kūṟum akarāti urinaṭiyilāṉatākum. ki.pi. 1732-l vīramāmuṉivar tokutta ittakiya "catur akarāti" itaṟku vaḻikāṭṭi yākum. tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻaka āṭciyil 1969-l "centamiḻc coṟ piṟappiyal akara mutavittiṭṭam" toṭaṅkappeṟṟatu. itaṟkeṉa taṉi " akaramutalit tiṭṭa iyakkakam"toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu,

itaṟkut talivarāka moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar amarttam ceyyap peṟṟār. 1997 vari akaramutali iyakkakam 'a' mutal 'oḷa' variyilāṉa coṟkaḷit tokuttu veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. toṭarntu paṇikaḷ naṭantu varukiṉṟaṉa. āṭcic col akarāti 
āṭciyic cemmippaṭuttum nōkkil tamiḻ vaḷarccittuṟi 1957, 1962, 1971, 1983 ākiya āṇṭukaḷil muṟiyē mutalām, iraṇṭām, muṉṟām, nāṉkām patippukaḷāka āṭcic col akarātiyi veḷi yiṭṭatu. 1990-l aintām patippum veḷivantuḷḷatu. ēṟattāḻa 78 tuṟikaḷukkāṉa ciṟappuc col tuṇi akarātikaḷum taṉiyē veḷiyiṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. mottattil oṉpatāyiram āṭciccoṟkaḷ ivvakarātiyil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. akarātikaḷ ovvoru tuṟikkum vaḻaṅkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. 
mēlē kūṟappeṟṟa cumār 61 paṇikaḷiṉ paṭṭiyalil tamiḻ moḻiyic cemmippaṭutti virivākkum ceyal tiṭṭaṅkaḷ ovvoṉṟum ti.mu.ka. āṭciyil cevvaṉē ceyalpaṭṭatu. 
- 58 - akavi muṭinta tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷukkut tiṅkaḷ tōṟum urupā 400-liruntu 500 āka niti utaviyaḷikkappeṟṟatu: ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakattiṉ vāyilāka añcalvaḻik kalviyip payiṟṟuvippataṟku morīciyacu, rīyūṉiyaṉ, ṭarpaṉ, maṭakācukar ākiya pakutikaḷil añcal vaḻik kalviyit toṭar aracu āvaṉa ceytatu. kīḻttici ōliccuvaṭikaḷ nūlakam 
kipi. 1869-l toṭaṅkappeṟṟa in nūlakattil tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, camaṟkirutam, urutu pōṉṟa iculāmiya moḻikaḷiluḷḷa 12,314 ōlic cuvaṭikaḷ cēkarittup pātukākkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟil tamiḻ ōlic cuvaṭikaḷ maṭṭum 16000-kkum mēmpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. " in nūlakam ituvari ēṟattāḻa 420 ōlic cuvaṭikaḷi acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. timuka āṭciyil cumār 4,000 catura aṭi parappuḷḷa in nāvakam kuḷircātaṉam ceyyap peṟṟum, cuvaṭikaḷi nuṇpaṭac curuḷil koṇṭuvaravum ēṟpāṭu ceytatu. ivaṟṟiṟkāka tōrāyamāka 38 ilakkam urupāy celaviṭṭatu. aracu aruṅkāṭciyakaṅkaḷ 
ceṉṉi aruṅkāṭciyakattip pōṉṟē 12 māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil ēṟpaṭuttap peṟṟirunta aruṅkāṭciyakaṅkaḷōṭu 1996-l mēlum aintu māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil aruṅkāṭciyakaṅkaḷēṟpaṭuttappeṟṟaṉa. kapilar eḻutiya "kuṟiñcip pāṭṭu" eṉum nūlil kuṟippiṭap peṟṟuḷḷa ota malarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi aṭiyāḷaṅkaṇṭu avaṟṟip pāṭam ceytu aruṅkāṭciyakattil avip pārvikku vikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉ.

āciyaviyal niṟuvaṉam (Institute of Asian Studies) 
tamiḻiyal āyvukkāka tamiḻaka aracu 1997-1998-l inniṟuvaṉattiṟ kāka urūpāy 90 - ilakkam niti utavi aḷittatu. tamiḻ ilakkiya kalikkaḷañciyam, tamiḻ - cappāṉ - teluṅku - kaṉṉaṭam akaramutali ākiya paṇikaḷi inta niṟuvaṉam ceytuvarukiṟatu. itaṟkāka 1998-99-l urūpāy 20 ilakkam māṉiyamāka aracu aḷittatu. tamiḻmoḻi karuttaraṅku 
māvaṭṭan tōṟumuḷḷa aracu aluvalarkaḷukkum tamiḻ āṭcimoḻi āvatiluḷḷa nirikuṟikaḷic cuṭṭikkāṭṭumpaṭiyāṉa karuttaraṅkukaḷ naṭipeṟṟaṉa. kuṟippāka aracāṇikaḷ, caṭṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ poruḷ viḷaṅkumpaṭiyum, avaṟṟi naṭimuṟip paṭuttum vaki paṟṟiyum karuttaraṅkil āyappaṭṭaṉa. ik karuttaraṅku kaḷāl 100viḻukkāṭu toyviṉṟit tamiḻ āṭcimoḻiyāṉatu. icip paḷḷikaḷ 
ceṉṉi, maturi, kōvi ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil maṭṭumirunta icip paḷḷikaḷip pōlavē mēlum pattu māvaṭṭat talinakaraṅ kaḷil 1996-kkup piṉ icip paḷḷikaḷ ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟil mottam ici vallunarkaḷ paṇiyilamarttam ceyyap peṟṟaṉar. iṇiyam (Internet) 
iṉṟiya aṟiviyalil pūtta malarākat tikaḻvatu iṇiyam. ulakatti oru noṭiyil iṇikkum ikkaruviyiṉ payaṉi 1997-98ām āṇṭu tamiḻaka caṭṭamaṉṟa nikaḻcciyil naṭanta varavu - celavut tiṭṭa nikaḻcciyāl ulakam aṟintatu. virivupaṭuttappeṟṟa iṇiyattāl tamiḻaka varalāṟu, tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟu, tamiḻk kalikaḷiṉ ciṟappu, cuṟṟulā payaṇa vaḻikāṭṭutal ākiyavi ceytip pakkam"(Home Page), eṉum muṟiyil tamiḻilum, āṅkilattilum tarappaṭukiṟatu. 
mēlum, iṇiyattil ulakattilēyē atika ceytip pakkaṅ kaḷik koṇṭa tamiḻiltāṉ mutalil veḷiyiṭap peṟṟatu. 1900 - l ulakattamiḻ iṇiyam mānāṭu' tamiḻaka aracāl naṭattappeṟṟu ulakattamiḻ iṇiyap palkalikkaḻakam amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. intiya āṭcip paṇittērvu[I.A.S. Examination)

a.i.a.ti.mu.ka. āṭciyiṉ cātaṉikaḷic curuṅkak kāṇpōm. 1. piṟpaṭṭōr nalam mēmpaṭutal 
*' paṟṟuka piṟpaṭṭōr mēmpāṭṭi " itu tirāviṭa iyakkaṅ ḷiṉ cittāntam tamiḻnāṭṭil āṭciyik kippaṟṟa vēṇṭumāṉāl piṟpaṭuttappaṭṭa cātikaḷic cikkaṉappiṭittukkoḷ" eṉṟa paṭṭāṅ kit ti.mu.ka.vukkup piṟaku a.i.a.ti.mu.ka.vum piṉpaṟṟi ataṉiyē tērtalil tāraka mantiramāka uccarittup patavikku vantatāl a.iatimuka piṟpaṭṭōr nalattil akkaṟi koṇṭatu. a) vakuppu vārippaṭi nikarāḷiyam 
1921-l koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa vakuppu vārip paṭinikarāḷiyam 1951-l araciyal caṭṭa aṭippaṭi urimikaḷik kāṭṭi ucca nayaṉmi maṉṟattāl cellāteṉat taḷḷappaṭṭatu. piṉṉar ēṟpaṭṭa araciyal tiruttattil "piṟpaṭṭōr pātukāppup peṟṟaṉar. itaṉaṭippaṭiyil tāṉ timuka āṭciyil 1969-l piṟpaṭṭōr nalattuṟip paṭikkap paṭṭatu. avarkaḷukkāka irunta iṭa otukkīṭu25liruntu 32 catavikitamāka uyarttappaṭṭatu. 
makōrā.(em.ji.ār āṭciyil poṟuppiyēṟṟavuṭaṉ 1982 l cē.ē. ampācaṅkar āṇikkuḻuvi amarttiṉār. atil 11 caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷum, piṟpaṭṭa nalattuṟi iyakkunar, caṭṭati tuṟit talivar maṟṟum makkaḷ maṉṟat talivarkaḷumāka 21 uṟuppi ṉarkaḷ iṭam peṟṟaṉar. piṉṉiṇippu IV-l kāṇka) 
ik kuḻuviṉ parinturiyiṉ pēril 1986-l piṟpaṭṭōr cātikaḷ 201 eṉa aṟiyappaṭṭatu. ivarkaḷ tamiḻaka makkaḷ tokiyil 54 catavikitamāka uḷḷaṉareṉṟum kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭatu. ivarkaḷ kumukāya nili, poruḷātāra nili mutaliyavaṟṟil piṉtaṅkiyavarkaḷeṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭatu. eṉavē, piṟpaṭṭōrukku iṭa otukkīṭu ti.mu.ka. aracu ceyta 32 viḻukkāṭṭiliruntu 50 viḻukkāṭāka uyarntatu. inta otukkīṭu kalvi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷilum, aracup paṇikaḷilum ceyalpaṭuttappaṭṭatu, ataṟkēṟṟāṟpōl varavu-celavil niti otukkīṭum uyarttappaṭṭatu.

1983-l 'tamiḻnāṭup peṇkaḷ vaḷarccik kaḻakam" ēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭatu. vaḻikāṭṭum miyaṅkaḷ mūlam mūṉṟu cēvi illaṅkaḷ" (Service Hormes) eṭṭut toḻil uṟpatti niliyaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟil peṇkaḷukku piṉṉal, tiyal, taṭṭaccu mutaliya vaṟṟil payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭatu. "makaḷir maṉṟaṅkaḷ" eṉavum toṭaṅ kappaṭṭaṉa. ittakiya makaḷir maṉṟaṅkaḷ mottam II 220 iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkāka uruṭā12.5 ilakkam celaviṭappaṭṭatu. ivaṟṟil kuḻanti vaḷarppu, cattuṇavu, ciṟutoḻilkaḷ paṟṟip peṇkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkap paṭṭa ṉa. 
ivaṟṟit tavira kuḻantikaḷ kāppakaṅkaḷ" tōrāyamāka 1988 nāṭu muḻuvatum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟil akavi mutal ikkuṭpaṭṭa kuḻantikaḷ pēṇikkākkappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl kuḻantikaḷ iṟappu vikitam kuṟintatu. ittakiya makaḷir kuḻanti nala niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ ḷāl kuṟippāka vitavikaḷ, ūṉamuṟṟap peṇkaḷ, kiviṭappaṭṭap peṇkaḷ ākiyōrum aṉātikaḷāṉa kuḻantikaḷum kāppāṟṟap paṭṭaṉar. 1986-87l maṭṭum kuḻantikaḷip pēṇik kākka urupā 117 82 ilakkam celaviṭappaṭṭatu,

a.i.a.ti.mu.ka. āṭciyiṉ pōtu uyarnilip paḷḷippaṭippu pattu āṇṭukaḷōṭu kārppurattilēyē mēl nilikkalvi" iraṇṭu tuṇṭukaḷ paṭikka vaḻi ceyyappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl pattāṇṭukaḷ paṭittu viṭṭuk kallūriyil paṭikka nakarppuram cellum vaḻakkam māṟi, karp purattilēyē kallūrip paṭippu vantuviṭṭatu. aṭuttu, kallūri iḷaṅkalip paṭippu mūṉṟāṇṭāka māṟiyatu. āṉāl mēlnilip paṭippu muṭinta uṭaṉēyē maruttuvam, poṟiyiyal mutaliya toḻiṟ paṭippukaḷi ūriliruntē nērē ceṉṟu paṭikkum vāyppu ēṟpaṭṭatu. eṉavē, ūrilēyē maruttuvar, poṟiyāḷar, aṟiviyalār, toḻil tuṟi vallunar, mutaliyōri mēlnilip paḷḷippaṭippu nirṇayittu viṭṭatu. palkalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ

kum, cīruṭi vaḻaṅkuvataṟkum, pottakaṅkaḷ, eḻutuporuḷkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum celaviṭappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, paḷḷip paṭippi iṭiyilēyē niṟutti viṭum māṇavar vikitam kuṟintatu. atē camayam māṇavar varukiyum atikarittatu. paḷḷikaḷiṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷi nīkkutal 
paḷḷikaḷ aracup paḷḷikaḷ, uḷḷāṭcip paḷḷikaḷ eṉa iruvakiyāka iruntati ivvaracu oṭṭumottamāka ellāvaṟṟiyum * 'aracuppaḷḷikaḷ" eṉṟākkiyatu. itaṉāl āciriyarkaḷ aṉivarum aracu ūḻiyarkaḷāyiṉar. avarkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyum, pātukāppum atikarittaṉa. paṇimuṟikaḷ, ōyvu ūtiyam mutaliyaṉa orē cīrāka amintaṉa. iruvakik kallūrikaḷ 
kallūrikaḷum aracuk kallūrikaḷ, taṉiyār kallūrikaḷeṉa iru vakiyākac ceyalpaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟil paṇiyāṟṟum āciriyarkaḷiṉ nilikaḷilum, paṇi, ūtiyam mutaliyavaṟṟilum vēṟupāṭukaḷiruntaṉa. ivi palkalikkaḻaka māṉiyakkuḻuviṉ parinturippaṭi orē cīrāṉavi yāka ākkappaṭṭaṉa. aracukkallūrikaḷilum, taṉiyār kallūrikaḷilum paṇiyāṟṟum āciriyarkaḷukku orēvitamāṉa ūtiyam vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. itiyum aracē nēraṭiyāka vaḻaṅkiyatu. aṉatiya vikitam, paṇi nilikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum ōyvu ūtiyamum ivarkaḷukku orē mātiriyāka nirṇa yikkappaṭṭaṉa, toḻil nuṭpak kallūrikaḷ, vēḷāṇmik kal lūrikaḷ, poṟi iyal kallūrikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil paṇiyāṟṟum āciri yarkaḷum itē niliyil matikkappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl, māntavaṉamum āṟṟalum vaḷarntaṉa, 8, nalvāḻvu mēmpāṭu (a) cukātāra vaḷarcci 
ūrppuraṅkaḷil nalavāḻvi mēmpaṭuttac cuṟṟuc cūḻalkaḷit tūymippaṭuttutal, toṟṟunōykaḷ paravāmal taṭuttal, taramāṉa maruntu māttirikaḷik koṭuttal, nōy taṭuppu muṟikaḷik kiyāḷutal mutaliyavaṟṟi aracu ceytatu.kuṟippākattāy, cēy nalatti mukāmiyācukkoṇṭatu. vātanōy taṭuppu taṭuppuc coṭṭu maruntu viṭutal, ammi, mañcaḷkāmālit taṭuppūci, cattuṇavu vaḻaṅkutal mutaliyavaṟṟi mēṟkoṇṭatu. 
putiya maruttuvamaṉikaḷ, maruttuvak kalluārikaḷ mutaliyaṉa ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. eṭṭu maruttuvak kalluārikaḷi amittu, āṇṭu tōṟum 1,015 maruttuvarkaḷi uruvākki, makkaḷukkuppaṇipuriya aṉuppappaṭṭaṉar. mottattil 14 māvaṭṭa maruttuvamaṉikaḷ, 104 vaṭṭa maruttuvamaṉikaḷ, avarkaḷil toṭakkac cukātāra maruttuva niliyaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. puṟṟunōy taṭuppu, kuṭumpak kaṭṭuppāṭu 
ākiyavi muṭukkamāy mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.

9. miṉuṟpattiyil mēmpāṭu - 1970-71l 57 mekāvāṭ yūṉiṭṭukaḷāyirunta miṉuṟpatti 1985 - 86-l paṟkaḷ mekāvāṭ yūṉiṭṭukaḷāy uyarttappaṭṭatu. miṉ uṟpatti nilakkariyi erittum, aṇuvip payaṉpaṭuttiyum peṟappaṭṭatu. vēṇṭiya nilakkariyi iṟakkumati ceytu miṉuṟpatti ceyyappaṭṭatu. kalpākkattil aṇumiṉ uṟpatti ceyyappaṭṭatu. pōtiya nilakkari tamiḻnāṭṭil kiṭikkātatāl 1985-86-l urupā 57.87 kōṭi miṉ uṟpattikkākac celaviṭappaṭṭatu. 
putitāka ūrppuraṅkaḷukkum, kuṭicippakutikaḷukkum miṉcāram vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatāl 1986-l maṭṭum 45ilakkam kuṭicikaḷil miṉviḷak kerintatu. āti - tirāviṭar kuṭiyiruppukaḷ mottam 64,012kku miṉ viḷakkukaḷ ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. 1984-85-l 074 ilakkam vēḷāṇminīririppu iyantiraṅkaḷukku māṉiya viliyil miṉcāram vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, vēḷāṇmi uṟpatti atikarittatu. 10, pōkkuvarattu ēntukaḷ mēmpaṭutal 
1985-l tamiḻaka ūrkaḷiṉ makkaḷ toki 324 kōṭiyākum. ūrppañcāyattukkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki 12,339 ākum, nakaraṅkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki 434 ākum. 1985-l tamiḻakattil ōṭiya pēruntukaḷiṉ eṇ kaṇikki 15,021 ākum. ivaṟṟil 12 pōkkuvarattukkaḻakaṅkaḷ 7,829 pēruntukaḷi iyakkiṉa. ōvvoru nāḷum 56 ilakkam payaṇikaḷ pēruntukaḷil payaṇittaṉar. nāḷtōṟum 35 ilakkam kilō mīṭṭar tārampēruntukaḷ ōṭiṉa. 
pēruntukaḷi nāṭṭuṭamiyākkiya aṇṇāviṉ kālantoṭṭu, tamiḻnāṭṭup pōkkuvarattukkaḻakam palkip perukiyatu. kuṟippāka mūli muṭukkukaḷiluḷḷa ūrkaḷukkellām pēruntukaḷ ceṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl 440 ūrkaḷ pēruntup pōkkuvarattāl iṇikkappaṭṭaṉa, itaṉāl a.i.a.ti.mu.ka. aracu 400 putiya pēruntukaḷi 163 kōṭi urupā celavil vāṅkiyatu. iṉṟu intiya mānilaṅkaḷilēyē 24 maṇi nēramum eṇticikaḷilum nillāmal ōṭikkoṇṭirukkum pēruntup pōkkuvarattu uḷḷa mānilam tamiḻakam maṭṭumēyām. 11. nīrppācaṉa mēmpāṭukaḷ

āṅkilēyarkaḷāl ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭa ik kaḻakam kumukāyap poruḷātāra vaḷarccikkut taṭiyāka iruntatu. itaṉāl 1.12.1980-l makōrā em ji āri oḻittār. 2. pārampariya marmaṇiyakkārar, karṇam oḻippu 
kōyililē arccakar pataviyippōl ip patavikaḷum takappa ṉukkuppiṉ makaṉ yeṉṟa muṟiyil paramparic cottāka iruntaṉa, itaṉāl kuṟippiṭṭa cātiyiṉar parampariyāka otukkappaṭṭu, oṭukkap paṭṭaṉar. inta parampari muṟiyi oḻittu, avaṟṟiyum aracuppaṇika ḷākka vēṇṭumeṉa āti tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ talivarkaḷ paṉṉeṭuṅ kālamākak kēṭṭu vantaṉar. āṉāl, itu makōrā, emjiār) kālattil tāṉ niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭatu. paramparip patavi muṟiyi oḻittu itaṉi ūr aluvalar (Village Administrative Officials) eṉa māṟṟiṉār, tērvāṇiyam naṭattum tērvil tēṟiya evarum ip paṇiyil amarttappaṭṭaṉar. mēlum, aracuppaṇikaḷ vakuppuvāri paṭinikarāḷiyat tiṉ aṭippaṭiyil amarttappaṭuvatāl ūr maṇiyakkārarākavum, karṇamāka vum ōr āti tirāviṭarkūṭa varalāmeṉṟa niyati ēṟpaṭṭu viṭṭatu. intiya āṭcippaṇi, intiyak kāvalpaṇi (I.A.S & I.P.S) ākiyavaṟṟil tēṟiya āti tirāviṭar māvaṭṭa āṭciyāḷarākavum, māvaṭṭak kāvaltuṟi atikāriyākavum vantapōtum ūr maṇiyakkāra rāka varamuṭiyāta nili iruntati makōrā. em.ji.ār māṟṟiyamit tār. itu kumukāyattilēṟpaṭṭa māperum puraṭci ''aracu āti - tirāvi ṭarkaḷukkuppōṭum nalattiṭṭaṅkaḷ avarkaḷip pōy aṭiyāmal taṭuttavarkaḷ parampari maṇiyakkārarum, karṇamumēyāvar; iṉi attakiya taṭi ēṟpaṭātu" eṉṟu. ivvāṇiyi veḷiyiṭumpōtu makōrā. em ji ār kūṟiṉār. 3. uḷḷāṭcit tuṟikaḷil iṭa otukkīṭu

4. aricaṉa nalattuṟi peyarmāṟṟam 
''aricaṉ"' eṉṟa collē araciyal caṭṭattil illi . āṉālum kāntiyār iccolli uccarittu aricaṉatteyvamāṉār! eṉavē, pērāyak kaṭciyiṉ vaḷarccikkut tāraka mantiramāka iḵtu uccarikkappaṭṭatu. ōpi. irāmacāmi reṭṭiyār (1947-49) ceṉṉi maṇṭilat talimi amiccarāka iruntapōtu toḻilāḷar nalat tuṟi"yi iraṇṭākap pirittu atiliruntu "aricaṉa nalattuṟi"yi . ēṟpaṭuttiṉār. aṉṟu mutalmakōrā (em ji ār kālam vari aricaṉa nalattuṟi peyar māṟṟamillāmalēyē iruntatu. makōrā.""aricaṉ" eṉṟa peyar tamiḻp peyarāka illi yeṉpataṟkāka ataṉi * 'āti - tirāviṭar"" eṉṟu māṟṟiṉār. itaṟkuppiṟaku aricaṉa nalattuṟi āti tirāviṭar nalattuṟiyeṉṟum, aricaṉa mantiri āti tirāviṭa nalattuṟi amiccar eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. 5. terukkaḷukkirunta cātippeyarkaḷi nīkkutal 
terukkaḷ allatu vītikaḷ cātip peyarkaḷāl aḻikkap paṭṭaṉa. periyār pācaṟiyil vaḷarnta talivarkaḷāl tamiḻakam 1967. viruntu āḷappaṭṭatu. cātip paṭṭaṅkaḷit taṅkaḷiṉ peyarōṭu cērppatilli yeṉṟu uṟutiyeṭuttuk koṇṭa ivarkaḷtirukka ḷukku maṭṭum cātip peyarkaḷ vittu vaḻaṅkuvati eppaṭi viṭṭu viṭṭaṉareṉṟu teriyavilli, āṉāl, atē pācaṟiyil vaḷarnta makārā.periyārip pōlavē uṟutiyāka iruntu appeyarkaḷi nīkkiviṭṭār! itaṉi mu. karuṇānitiyum etirttār. āṉāl, makōrā. itil piṉṉaṭiyavilli "cuṅkurāma ceṭṭit teru" eṉpati cuṅkurāmaṉ teru eṉṟum, taṇikācalam mutali teru eṉpatit taṇikā calam teru eṉṟum ivvāṟāka 'cāti'kaḷi veṭṭiṉār. 
''ma.kō.rā."' mānta nēyam uṭiyavar. avarukkukkaṭci, cāti, camayam mutaliyaṉa kuṟukkē niṟkātu. 5. tamiḻaka mēlavi (Legislative Council)yi oḻittal

tāynila varalāṟu 
mutal amiccarāyiruntār. mūṉṟām muṟi mutal amiccarāy (14.2.1985 - 24.12.1997 iruntapōtutāṉ 1986l avar caṭṭamaṉṟattil tīrmāṉam koṇṭu vantu mēlviyi oḻittuviṭṭār. avvāṇṭu mutal tamiḻakattil oṟṟic caṭṭamaṉṟa mēyuḷḷatu. iti ēṉ oḻittār eṉpataṟkāṉa viḷakkamum, ataṉ naṉmi tīmikaḷum aṟiñarkaḷiṭiyē vivātap poruḷākavēyuḷḷatu. 1. aṉittuc cātiyiṉarum arccakarākum caṭṭavarivu 
ti.mu.ka. āṭciyil arccakar caṭṭam koṇṭu varappaṭṭu, ucca nayaṉmi maṉṟattāl nirākarikkappaṭṭatik kaṇṭōm. aiatimuka āṭci ēṟpaṭṭavuṭaṉ makōrā. (em. ji. ār. itupaṟṟi āynturikka nayaṉmiyar (ōyvu) makārācaṉ talimiyil oru kuḻuvi amittār. inta kuḻu 1987 ām āṇṭu aracukkut taṉatu parinturikaḷik koṭuttatu. akkuḻuviṉ parinturikaḷāvaṉa: 
1. aṭippaṭi urimikku etirāṉatu:- arccakar patavi parampari urimiyuṭiyateṉa 1959 ām āṇṭu intu aṟaniliyac caṭṭam viti 55 l kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āṉāl itaṉit tirutti timuka aracu 1971 ām āṇṭukoṇṭu varappaṭṭa arccakar caṭṭattil antap parampari urimi nīkkappaṭṭatu. arccakarkaḷ itaṉi etirttu, ucca nayaṉmi maṉṟattil vaḻakkup pōṭṭaṉar. ucca nayaṉmi maṉṟam ''parampari urimiyi oḻippatu araciyal caṭṭam aḷitta aṭippaṭi urimikku 
etirāṉatu" eṉṟu tīrppu aḷittatu. 2. ākamavitikaḷukku puṟampāṉatalla:- pirāmaṇarit tavirtta piṟa cātiyāri arccakarākkuvatu 'ākama vitikaḷukkup puṟampāṉatu alla" eṉṟu ikkuḻu kūṟiyatu. ēṉeṉṟāl ākamavitippaṭi kaṭṭiya pala kōyilkaḷil pirāmaṇarallātārum arccakarāka vuḷḷaṉar; ākama vitikaḷukku māṟākat tēvatāci muṟi oḻikkap paṭṭuviṭṭatu: ākama vitikaḷukkup puṟampāka āti tirāviṭarkaḷ (tīṇṭātārkōyil nuḻivu ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatu ākama vitikaḷuk kup puṟampāka uyirppalit taṭi koṇṭu varappaṭṭu viṭṭatu: caṭṭattiṉ muṉ aṉivarum camam eṉpatu araciyal caṭṭa vitiyākiviṭṭatu; eṉavē,' 'āṇṭavaṉ muṉ aṉivarum camam" eṉpati uṟuti ceyya aṉittuc cātiyiṉariyum arccakarākku vatit taṭukka muṭiyāteṉak kuḻu parinturi ceytatu. itaṟku araciyal caṭṭamē taṭiyāka niṉṟālum, atiyē tiruttalar meṉṟu kuḻut talivar kūṟiṉār. 3. ākamavitikaḷi aṟintavarē arccakarāka vēṇṭumeṉṟāl 
arccakarāvōrukku atil payiṟci aḷikkalām ataṟkāka aracē ākamakkalluārikaḷit tiṟantu attakiya payiṟci aḷikkalām meṉṟum parinturic ceyyappaṭṭatu.

4. ''ākamavitikaḷ pirāmaṇar tavirtta piṟacātiyiṉariyum, peṇka 
ḷiyum arccakarākak kūṭātu yeṉṟu taṭukkavilli. eṉavē, peṇkaḷum arccakarākalām" eṉṟum parinturikkap paṭṭatu. peṇkaḷ kōyilukkuḷ nuḻintu mūlavar irukkikku varak kūṭāteṉṟāl tēvatācikaḷum peṇkaḷ tāṉē? avarkaḷi ēṉ aṉumatittaṉar? eṉṟum viṉā eḻuppiyatu. 
itaṟkup piṉṉum, aracu arccakar caṭṭattik koṇṭuvantu ceyalpaṭutta muṭiyāmal mayakkamuṟṟatu. āyiṉum kumukāyac cīrtiruttam ceytu kumukāya nayaṉmiyik kaṇṭuviṭat tuṭitta makōrā. emjiār) mēlum itupaṟṟi āynturikka 1984-l nayaṉmiyar kiruṭṭiṇacāmi reṭṭiyār talimiyil mīṇṭum oru kuḻuvi ēṟpaṭuttiṉār. ik kuḻuvum pal parinturikaḷit tantatu. ataṟkup piṉṉum aracāl arccakar caṭṭatti niṟivēṟṟic ceyalpaṭutta muṭiyavilli. neñcōṭu kiḷattal 
1. iṭotukkīṭṭu muṟiyi etirttu "cāti aṭippaṭiyil iṭotukkīṭu kūṭātu: itu araciyal caṭṭamaḷittuḷḷa aṭippaṭi urimikaḷukkup puṟampāṉatu" eṉṟu kūṟi ataṉi etirtta pirā maṇarkaḷ arccakar pataviyi maṭṭum pirāmaṇa cātikkē uriyatu yeṉṟu kūṟuvatu enta vakiyil niyāyamākum? 
pirāmaṇar tavira maṟṟa cātiyār arccarāki āṇṭavaṉ uruvac ciliyit toṭṭu nīrāṭṭiṉāl tīṭṭuppaṭṭuviṭum eṉkiṟārkaḷ. itu araciyal caṭṭa viti 17kku etirāṉatu. avvitiyil 26.950-liruntu tīṇṭāmi ip puṉita pāratattiliruntu aṭiyōṭu oḻikkappaṭṭu viṭṭateṉṟum, tīṇṭāmik kuṟṟam ceyvōr taṇṭaṉikkuḷḷāva reṉṟum kūṟukiṟatu. āṉāl, pirāmaṇarkaḷ akkuṟṟattiyē ceykiṉṟa ṉar! 
ivvāṟu, cāti aṭippaṭiyil iṭotukkīṭṭi etirkkum pōtu araciyal caṭṭavitikaḷ 25(1) maṟṟum 26yum cuṭṭikkāṭṭi avaṟṟil veṟṟi peṟṟa pirāmaṇar, atē araciyal caṭṭa vitikaḷukku etirāka **pirāmaṇa cātikkē arccakar patavi'' eṉṟu kūṟi ac caṭṭam niṟi vēṟāmal taṭuttuviṭṭaṉar. itaṉi mēlum valuppaṭuttakkāñci caṅkara maṭattil maṭat talivarkaḷāka pirāmaṇariyē vittuk koṇṭu, varuṇāciramattik kaṭipiṭikkiṉṟaṉar. 
uṇmiyāṉa makkaḷāṭci muṟiyi nilināṭṭavum, nākarikak kumukāyatti uruvākkavumē tēvatāci muṟiyi oḻittārkaḷ, tīṇṭātārik kōyilpukac ceytaṉar. atē paṇpāṭukaḷik kaṭṭik kākka arccakar pataviyilum entac cātiyiṉarum amaralāmeṉṟu viṭṭuk koṭuttāleṉṉa? 
tāynilavaralāṟu 
tēvatācikaḷ pirāmaṇac cātiyic cērntavarkaḷallar. avarkaḷ takkapaṭi payiṟcip peṟṟapiṉ tēvatācikaḷ āyiṉar. avvāṟē, payiṟcip peṟṟa evariyum arccakarkaḷākkiṉāleṉṉa? 
mutaṟpaṭiyākac celvi jeyalalitā arccakar payiṟcik kallūriyit toṭaṅkiyuḷḷār. 6. kāviri nīrppaṅkaṭṭiccikkalkaḷ 
intiya varalāṟē kāvirippaṭukiyiliruntutāṉ toṭaṅkappaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, atu cintu veḷiyiliruntu toṭaṅkappaṭutal kūṭātu eṉṟum kūṟum nākarikamum, paṇpāṭum aṟiñarkaḷukkē oru māperum cikkali uṇṭākkiyuḷḷatu. iṉṟiyak karunāṭaka - tamiḻakaṅkaḷuk kiṭiyē avvāṟṟu nīrip pakirntu koḷḷuvatilēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa cikkalkaḷ, 1956-l moḻivaḻi mānilaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyataṟkuppiṉṉarē veḷippaṭiyākat teriyavantaṉa. ic cikkal kāviri pāyum karunāṭakam, kēraḷam, tamiḻakam, pāṇṭiccēri ākiya mānilaṅkaḷukkiṭiyi leṟpaṭṭālum itil atikam tākkuṇṭut tuṉpappaṭuvatu tamiḻakamē ākum. ātalāl nēraṭi mōtalkaḷ, tamiḻakattiṟkum curunāṭakattukku miṭiyil tāṉ naṭakkiṉṟaṉa. oru piṉṉōṭṭam - 
āṅkilēyar āṭcik kālattil potuppaṇittuṟip poṟi yāḷarāka irunta vayavar. ārtarkāṭṭaṉ eṉpavar kāviriyil veḷḷāṟu kāviriyiliruntu piriyumiṭattil kāvirikkum, veḷḷāṟṟukkum matakukaḷ amittār. piṉṉar koḷḷiṭattil mēl aṇiyum, 936-l kīḻ aṇiyum amikkappeṟṟaṉa. ōppantam 
- 1892l micūr aracum, ceṉṉi maṇṭila aracum, taṅkaḷukkuḷ iyintu ōr oppanta uṭaṉpaṭikkic ceytu koṇṭaṉa. ataṉpaṭi micūrum, ceṉṉiyum oṉṟukkoṉṟu oppukkoṇṭu, muṉ aṉumatiyuṭaṉtāṉ kāviriyiṉ kuṟukkē nīrttēkkamō, kālvāyō amikka vēṇṭum eṉpatākum. - oppantam mīṟal 
eṭutta eṭuppilēyē micūr aracu ceṉṉi araciṉ aṉumati illāmalēyē 1892l kaṇṇampāṭi aṇiyik kaṭṭat toṭaṅki yatu. itaṟkuc ceṉṉi aracu maṟupput terivittum payaṉillāmal pōṉatu.1971-l miṉ uṟpattikkākac civacamuttiram nīrvīḻcciyi micūr aracu kūṭṭattoṭaṅkiyatu. itaṟkum ceṉṉi aracu aṉumati maṟuttatu, vaḻakku 
ivviru oppantamīṟalukkākac ceṉṉi aracu vaḻakkut toṭarntatu. ivvaḻakku alakāpātu nayaṉmi maṉṟattil pācāvap

peṟṟu, micūr aracu oppantatti mīṟiviṭṭateṉat tīrppuk kūṟap paṭṭatu. āṉāl, nayaṉmi maṉṟat tīrppi micūr aracu ēṟka maṟuttu, mēl muṟiyīṭu ceytatu. aṉṟiya intiyāviṉ ucca nayaṉmi maṉṟam ilaṇṭaṉiluḷḷa pirivikavuṉcil ākum. anta ucca nayaṉmi maṉṟam * iraṇṭu mānilaṅkaḷum taṅkaḷukkuḷ pēcit tīrttukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu tīrppuk kūṟiyatu. mīṇṭum oppantam 
1821924-l iraṇṭu aracukaḷum mīṇṭum paḻiyapaṭi oppantam ceytu koṇṭaṉa. itaṉaṭippaṭiyil oṉṟukkoṉṟu muṉ aṉumati yuṭaṉ kiruṭṭiṇarācacākar aṇi ākiyavaṟṟik kaṭṭikkoṇṭaṉa. aṇikkaṭṭukaḷukkuppiṉ, ivviru mānilaṅkaḷum kālvāykaḷ veṭṭum tiṭṭaṅkaḷit toṭaṅkiṉa. kaṉvā, piramaṅkaḷā, pārmē kōkaḷḷi, imcā mutaliya kālvāykaḷi micūr aracum, pavāṉi, kīḻpavāṉi, pavāṉi cākar, amarāvati ākiya kālvāyt tiṭṭaṅkaḷic ceṉṉi aracum toṭaṅkiṉa. cikkalkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam 
1956-l nirṇayikkappaṭṭa moḻivaḻi mānila ellik kōṭu kaḷāl cikkalkaḷ tōṉṟalāyiṉa. ellikkōṭukaḷ tōṉṟalāyiṉa. ellik kōṭukaḷ nirṇayikkappaṭṭapiṉ 1892lum, 1924lum ēṟpaṭṭa oppantaṅkaḷ kāṟṟil paṟantaṉa. karunāṭaka aracu taṉṉicciyākap pala putiya kālvāykaḷi veṭṭik kāviri nīr pāyum nilapparappik karunāṭakattil virivuppaṭuttikkoṇṭatu. itaṉit tamiḻakam, kēraḷam, pāṇṭiccēri ākiya kāvirippāyum mānilaṅkaḷ etirttaṉa. ittakiya 
etirppukaḷum cikkalkaḷum 1968liruntu valuvaṭintaṉa. tamiḻnāṭu vaḻakkumaṉṟam ceṉṟatu 
1971l tamiḻ nāṭu kāviri nīrp paṅkīṭṭuc cikkalit tīrkka oru "naṭuvar maṉṟam" vēṇṭumeṉṟuk kōri uccanayaṉmi maṉṟattil vaḻakkut toṭarntatu. āṉāl, aṉṟiya pāratattiṉ talimi amiccar itil taliyiṭṭu, kāviri pāyum mānilaṅkaḷ oṉṟu kūṭik kavantu pēcit tīrttuk koḷḷumpaṭiyum itaṉāl vaḻakkit tirumpap peṟṟukkoḷḷumpaṭiyum kēṭṭukkoṇṭatāl, tamiḻnāṭu vaḻakkit tiruppip peṟṟukkoṇṭatu. 1971-1972-l kēraḷam, karunāṭakam, tamiḻakam ākiya mūṉṟu mānilaṅkaḷum oṉṟu cērntu pēci iraṇṭu varivu oppantaṅkaḷ ceytu koṇṭaṉa. ivaṟṟil im mānilaṅkaḷ aṇikkaṭṭuvataṟkum, kālvāy veṭṭuvataṟkum muṉ oṉṟu maṟṟoṉṟiṭam muṉ aṉumati peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa viti illi. mēlum, karunāṭakam 1924l ēṟpaṭṭa oppantattiluḷḷa muṉ aṉumatip peṟa vēṇṭum" eṉṟa nipantaṉi cellātu eṉṟu kūṟi viṭṭatu, ēṉeṉṟāl anta oppantam mānila ellikkōṭukaḷi nirṇayitta

tāynilavaralāṟu 
1956kku muṉpunāṭu aṭimippaṭṭuk kiṭanta kālattil ceyyappaṭṭatu. āṅkilēyar āṭciyil ceytukoṇṭa inta oppantatti viṭutalik kuppiṉ ataṉik kaṭippiṭikka muṭiyātu eṉṟum kūṟiviṭṭatu. 
itaṟkuppiṉ, karunāṭakam taṉviruppam pōl kālvāykaḷi veṭṭiyatu. aṇikkaṭṭukaḷik kaṭṭiyatu. itaṟku maṟupput terivittum karunāṭakam kātil viḻavillieḷag75l tamiḻakam kāviri nīrp paṅkīṭṭuc cikkali naṭuvar maṉṟattiṟku viṭumpaṭik kēṭṭatu payaṉilli . 
1983lkāvirinīrp paṅkīṭu toṭarpāṉa vaḻakki vivacāyikaḷ nala urimic caṅkam ucca nayaṉmi maṉṟattil toṭuttatu. ivvaḻakku 1990 varit toṭarntu naṭantu vantatu. āṉāl, 2441990l naṭuvaṇ aracu taliyiṭṭu, inta vaḻakkit tirumpap peṟṟukkoṇṭu karunāṭakamum, tamiḻakamum taṅkaḷukkuḷ pēcit tīrttuk koḷḷumpaṭik kēṭṭuk koṇṭatu. āṉāl, pēccuvārttiyilum palaṉ ēṟpaṭavilli. naṭuvar nayaṉmi maṉṟam 
enta muṭivum eṭṭāta niliyil tamiḻakattiṉ vēṇṭukōḷuk kiṇaṅka naṭuvaṇ aracu 'taṭuvar nayaṉmi maṇṟam' amittu, atil nayaṉmiyar kuḻu oṉṟiyum amarttiyatu. ataṉ talivarāka nayaṉmiyar ciṭṭakōcu mukarci niyamikkappeṟṟār. avaruṭaṉ paṇiyāṟṟa eṉ.ecu. irāv, es.ṭi akarvāl ākiya iru nayaṉmiyarum amarttappaṭṭaṉar inta nayaṉmiyar kuḻu kāviri pāyum mānilaṅkaḷic cuṟṟip pārttu, ceytikaḷit tiraṭṭi āyntu, kīḻkaṇṭa tīrppi vaḻaṅkiyatu. naṭuvar nayaṉmiyar maṉṟat tīrppu 
''karunāṭakamum, tamiḻakamum vārāvāram kāviri nīrip pakirntu koḷḷa vēṇṭum,'' eṉpatē tīrppu ākum. 
it tīrppi karunāṭakam ēṟka maṟuttatu. mēlum, "naṭuvar nayaṉmiyar maṉṟattiṟkut tīrppuk kūṟum takuti illiyeṉṟum, atu nayaṉmi maṉṟamē allaveṉṟum karunāṭakam kūṟiyatōṭu, at tīrppuc cellāteṉa 'avacara caṭṭam oṉṟiyum karunāṭakam piṟappittatu. 
karunāṭakattiṉ inta atiraṭic ceyalukku māṟṟāka ucca naṉmi maṉṟam, ''karunāṭaka aracukku avacara caṭṭam piṟappikkum urimi illi" yeṉṟum, atu intiya araciyal caṭṭattiṟkup puṟampāṉatu" eṉṟum kūṟi, ''naṭuvar nayaṉmiyar maṉṟat tīrppē muṭivāṉatu" eṉṟum kūṟiyatu. naṭuvaṇ aracu, naṭuvar nayaṉmiyar maṉṟat tīrppit taṉatu aracitaḻil 20299l veḷiyiṭṭu uṟuti ceyttu,

uṇṇānōṉpu 
celvi. ce. ceyalalitā mutaṉ muṟiyāka tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mutalamiccarāṉār. [2551991 - 2321996) kāvirinīr paṅkīṭṭuc cikkalit tīrkka vēṇṭiyum, uṭaṉaṭiyākat tamiḻakattiṟkut taṇṇīr viṭumpaṭiyum kāntiya pāṇiyil uṇṇā nōṉpu nōṟṟār. iḵtu 187 1993liruntu 21793 vari nāṉku nāṭkaḷ ceṉṉik kaṭaṟkariyiluḷḷa makōrā. (em ji ār camāti muṉpu naṭipeṟṟatu. makkaḷ kontaḷittaṉar. itaṉiyaṟinta naṭuvaṇ araciṉ nīrppācaṉattuṟi amiccar cuklā avarkaḷ, celvi ceyalalitāvi uṇṇā nōṉpik kiviṭumpaṭik kēṭṭukkoṇṭār; naṭuvar nayaṉmiyar maṉṟat tīrppi niṟivēṟṟu vatāka vākkuṟuti aḷittār. naṭuvar nayaṉmi maṉṟat tīrppi niṟivēṟṟuvatik kaṇkāṇikkak kaṇkāṇippuk kuḻu amittal 
itaṟkup piṉṉum karunāṭakam naṭuvar nayaṉmiyar maṉṟat tīrppi ēṟṟuc ceyalpaṭa maṟuttu viṭṭatu. itaṉāl, naṭuvar nayaṉṉiyar maṉṟat tīrppuppaṭi iru mānilaṅkaḷum ceyalpaṭukiṉṟaṉavāyeṉpatik kaṇkāṇikka oru ''kaṇkāṇippuk kuḻu" ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. inta iraṇṭu kuḻukkaḷiṉ tīrppukaḷiyum karunāṭakam caṭṭic ceyya villi , mīṇṭum uccanayaṉmi maṉṟattil vaḻakku 
- tamiḻakattil kaṭumvarṭci ēṟpaṭṭatu. kāvirippaṭukiyil payiriṭappaṭṭa 133 ilakkam kuṟukkiyil uḷḷa neṟkatirkaḷ vāṭiṉa; itaṉāl tamiḻaka vivacāyikaḷukku urupā 800 kōṭi iḻappīṭu ēṟpaṭum apāyam neruṅkiyatu. itaṉi viḷakki 11.12.1995-l tamiḻaka aracu ucca nayaṉmiyar maṉṟattil taṇṇīr viṭumpaṭi āṇiyiṭakkōri maṉu koṭuttatu. uccanayaṉmi maṉṟamum tamiḻakattiṟku 13piṭiemci taṇṇīr viṭumpaṭit tīrppuk kūṟiyatu. āṉāl, uccanayaṉmi maṉṟat tīrppik karunāṭakam tuccameṉa niṉittu taṇṇīr viṭa maṟuttuviṭṭatu. aṉittukkaṭcik kūṭṭam 
in niliyil tamiḻaka aracu aṉittuk kaṭciyiṉariyum kūṭṭi itaṟku oru tīrvu kāṇumpaṭik kēṭṭukkoṇṭatu. itil 78 kaṭcit talivarkaḷ paṅkēṟṟaṉar. tamiḻakattiṟku aṟattiṉ aṭippaṭiyil karunāṭakam *naṭuvar nayaṉmiyar maṉṟat tīrppuppaṭi taṇṇīrviṭa vēṇṭum" eṉṟu orumaṉatāy tīrmāṉam niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭatu. it tīrmāṉamum ceviṭaṉ kātil autiya caṅkolipōl āṉatu. intiyat talimi amiccariṉ vēṇṭukōḷ

emci. tāyiṉum, curukkappeṟṟatu 
cantittu, tamiḻakattil vāṭum neṟkatirkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟiṉār. talimi amiccar kuṟintatu āṟu ṭiemci taṇṇīrāvatu viṭavēṇṭumeṉa curunāṭakattukku vēṇṭukōḷ viṭuttār. āyiṉum, curunāṭakam taṇṇīr viṭa maṟuttuviṭṭatu. piṉṉar, "muvarkuḻu" oṉṟum amikkappeṟṟatu. ataṉālum palaṉ ēṟpaṭavilli. itaṟkiṭiyilcelvi, ceyalalitā viṉ aiatimuka āṭciyum 1995-l muṭintatu. muṭivuri 
kāviri nīrp paṅkiṭṭuc cikkal karunāṭakam, tamiḻakattiṟkumiṭiyē oru nūṟṟāṇṭukkum mēlākat toṭarkiṟatu. oppantam, naṭuvar nayaṉmar maṉṟattīrppu, ucca nayaṉmiyar maṉṟat tīrppu, kaṇkāṇippukkuḻut tīrppu muvarkkuḻut tīrppu makkaḷ maṉṟattiṉ vēṇṭukōḷ vivacāyi 
ḷiṉ vēṇṭukōḷ mutaliya iṉṉapiṟavum karunāṭakattiṉ piṭivātat tiṟku muṉ eṭupaṭavilli. ''tīrppu'' eṉṟāl ceyalpaṭuttappaṭa vēṇṭum tavaṟiṉāl taṇṭaṉi vēṇṭum. ituvē nayaṉmi māṟṟattiṉ iṟiyāṇmi , āka, intiya ucca nayaṉmi maṉṟattiyum, intiya araciyal caṭṭattiyum, makkaḷ kuraliyum matikkāta oru aracu uḷḷa teṉṟāl atu karunāṭakamē. itaṟkut tīrvutāṉ.eṉṉa? 
* "īyirukkum iṭam eṉiṉum ip puviyil yāṉ avarkku aracu iṉi koṭēṉ." eṉṟa turiyōtaṉaṉ piṭivātam pōl iṉṟiya karunāṭaka mutalvar " oru coṭṭut taṇṇīrum tamiḻakattiṟkuk koṭēṉ" eṉkiṟār. ""tamiḻakam ucca nayaṉmiyar maṉṟameṉṉa, ulaka nāṭukaḷ maṉṟamē ceṉṟālum eṉṉi oṉṟum acikka muṭiyātu" eṉkiṟār. avari acikkappāratamum, pārata talimi amiccarum, ucca nayaṉmiyar maṉṟamum, araciyal caṭṭamum oṉṟum ceyyām virukkum pōtu kāviric cikkal appaṭiyētāṉirukkum. 
karunāṭakam aḷavukku mīṟi aṇikaḷiyum, kālvāykaḷiyum amittup pācaṉapparappi akalappaṭuttiviṭṭatu. atu taṉṉiṟivup peṟṟu, "taṉakku miñciṉāltāṉ tarumam" eṉpati eppaṭi niṟivu ceyya muṭiyum?

ulakiṉ mutal nakara nākarikap piṟappiṭam pūmpukār nāki māvaṭṭam pūmpukār arukē cumār 17,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu kaṭalil mūḻkiya oru pirammāṇṭa nakaramtāṉ ulakil mutaṉ mutalil tōṉṟiya navīṉa nakara nākarikamāka irukkakkūṭum eṉa, iṅkilāntic cērnta āḻkaṭal ārāycciyāḷar kirahām hāṉkāk eṉpavar kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷār. 
ivar kaṭanta 2001 ām āṇṭu pūmpukār kaṭaṟpakutiyil mēṟkaṇṭa tīvira āḻkaṭal ārāycciyiṉ mūlam inta uṇmiyik kaṇṭaṟin tuḷḷār. 
inta ārāycci, taṟpōtiya varalāṟṟu āyvāḷarkaḷiṉ karuntāṉa" mecapaṭōmiyā' pakutiyil cumēriyarkaḷāl cumār 500 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu nakara nākarīkam tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭatu eṉpatu tavaṟāṉatu eṉa terivikkiṟatu. - 
kirahām hāṉkāk eṉpavar iṅkilāntic cērnta ulakap pukaḻpeṟṟa āḻkaṭal ārāycciyāḷar. ivaratu pala kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷ 
varalāṟṟu ulakil perum paraparappi ēṟpaṭuttiyavi, 
mattiya araciṉ kaṭṭuppāṭṭil kōvāvil amintuḷḷa tēciya āḻkaṭal ārāyccik kaḻakam eṉṟa niṟuvaṉam kaṭanta 1991 ām āṇṭil varalāṟṟuppukaḻ peṟṟa pūmpukār nakarak kaṭaṟpakutiyil ōr āyviṉi mēṟkoṇṭatu. 
inta āyvukaḷ #3 ām āṇṭuvari toṭarntu naṭipeṟṟaṉa. āyviṉ pōtu, pūmpukār kaṭaṟpakutiyiliruntu cumār 3 kimī toli viṟkuḷ pala vaṭṭavaṭivamāṉa kiṇaṟukaḷ iruppatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭ. ṭatu. intak kiṇaṟukaḷ pūmpukār mutal taraṅkampāṭi variyilāṉa kaṭaṟpakutiyil paraviyiruppatu kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭatu. 
itu tavira caṅka kālattic cērntatu eṉa karutappaṭum cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷāl āṉa 'ṭa' vaṭiva kaṭṭaṭam oṉṟum kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭatu. 
ittuṭaṉ nīril cumār 23 aṭi āḻattil kutiri kuḷampu vaṭivil 85 aṭi nīḷamum, 2 mī uyaramum koṇṭa pala poruḷkaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyap paṭṭaṉa, 
ivi aṉittum pūmpukār kaṭaṟpakutiyil oru periya nakaram mūḻki irukkakkūṭum eṉpati uṟutippaṭuttum vitattil amintirunta pōtilum, tēciya āḻkaṭal ārāyccik kaḻakam, taṉṉuṭiya āyvi nitippaṟṟākkuṟi kāraṇamāka pātiyil niṟuttiviṭṭatu. - 
inniliyil, kaṭanta 2000 ām āṇṭu intiyāviṟku varuki purinta kirahām hāṉkāk, tēciya āḻkaṭal ārāyccik kaḻakattiṉariṭam pūmpukār paṟṟi vivaraṅkaḷik kēṭṭaṟintār. 
nitippaṟṟākkuṟi kāraṇamāka ārāycci pātiyil niṟuttappaṭṭa ṭati aṟinta avar iṅkilāntic cērnta 'cāṉal 4' eṉṟa pirapala tolikkāṭci niṟuvaṉam, amerikkāvic cērnta 'larṉiṅ cāṉal" eṉṟa tolikkāṭci niṟuvaṉam, ākiyavaṟṟiṉ nitiyutavi maṟṟum intiya 
208 
tāynilavaralāṟu : 
āḻkaṭal ārāycci kaḻakattiṉ ottuḻippuṭaṉ 2001m āṇṭu ārāycciyit toṭarntār. 
anta ārāyccikku atinavīṉa 'ciṭu skēṉ cōṉār eṉṟa curuvi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. intak karuvi pūmpukār kaṭaṟpakutiyil kuṟukkum neṭukkumāka nīṇṭa akalamāṉa terukkaḷuṭaṉ, uṟutiyāṉa kaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ iṭipāṭukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya oru pirammāṇṭa nakaram mūḻkiyiruppatit tulliyamākak kāṭṭiyatu. 
piṉṉar, akkāṭcikaḷi kirahām hāṉkāk navīṉa kāmirākkaḷ mūmpaṭam eṭuttār. 
inta mūḻkiya nakaram kuṟitta taṉatu ārāycciyit toṭarnta " hāṉkāk, anta nakaram kaṭalil cumār 75 aṭi āḻattil putiyuṇ ṭiruppatik kaṇṭaṟintār. 
inta nakaram cumār 1,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu mūḻki irukkak kūṭum eṉa hāṉkāk karutiṉār. 
taṉatu ārāycciyip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷi avar, iṅkilāntiṉ pukaḻ peṟṟa ṭarhām palkalikkaḻakattiṉ nilaviyal tuṟip pērāciriyar kiḷaṉ milṉē eṉpavariṭam terivittār. ' 
itaṉmītu ārāycci mēṟkoṇṭa kiḷaṉ milṉē, hāṉkāk kiṉ karuttu caritāṉ eṉa uṟutippaṭuttiṉarar. cumār 1500 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu kaṭalmaṭṭam 75 aṭi uyarntirukkakkaṭṭum eṉṟum, ataṉi vittup pārkkumpōtu inta nakaram 11,500 āṇṭu kāla paḻami vāyntatu eṉṟa muṭiviṉi aṟivittār. 
itaṉmūlam ulakil navīṉa nākarikam, namatu pūmpukār nakaril 17500 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu tōṉṟiyatu teḷivākiṟatu. - pūmpukāril mēṟkoṇṭa ārāycciyiṉ paṭaṅkaḷ, iṅkilāntu maṟṟum amerikkāvil aṇṭarvērlṭu eṉṟa peyaril tolik kāṭcit toṭarāka inta āṇṭu pipravariyil oḷiparappappaṭṭatu. intat tolikkāṭcit toṭar ulaka varalāṟṟu ārāycciyāḷarkaḷi āccaryattil āḻttiyuḷḷatu. ip paṭaṅkaḷi kaṭantavāram peṅkaḷu ril naṭanta kaṇkāṭciyil kirahām hāṉkāk veḷiyiṭṭār. 
''naṉṟi: tiṉamaṇi, poṟiyār,ṭica.19 2002. (pūmpukār āyvu tamiḻaka varalāṟṟuk kāla elliyip palapaṭi uyarttum vakiyil amintuḷḷatu, 
" kāla elliyiṉ kālip piṭittu iḻuppavarkaḷukkuc cāṭṭi yaṭi koṭuppatupōla inta āyvu nikaḻntuḷḷatu. 
pūmpukār āyvē ippaṭiyeṉṟāl kumarikkaṇṭa āyvu naṭantu muṭivu teriyavarumpōtu tamiḻaṉ varalāṟṟi maṟukkavō maṟikkavō muṭiyamā? 
kāppiyak kālap pūmpukār 1500 āṇṭeṉṟāl kaḻakak kālak kumarikkaṇṭam 5000 āṇṭeṉpatu mikiyā?)
